# 代码注释归档（V1.0.0）

源码里那些"为什么这样做"的注释，已从代码中移到这里，按文件与起始行归档。

**这份文件只留在本地，不进 jar、不上服务器** —— 它是开发过程的说明，不是运行时的东西。
交付物（jar / 云端 backend-python）里只保留功能性标记（`# noqa`、`# type:`）与字段自述。

处理规则（`tools/maintenance/_strip_comments.py`）：

| 语言 | 删什么 | 留什么 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Python | 全部注释、多行 docstring | `# -*- coding` / `# noqa` / `# type:`；≤40 字的单行 docstring |
| Java | 全部 `//` 与 `/* */`（含 javadoc） | 注解与代码本身 |
| Vue / JS | 全部 `//`、`/* */`、`<!-- -->` | — |
| SQL 迁移 | **不碰** | Flyway 会校验文件摘要，改一个字符线上库就 `checksum mismatch` |

以下按文件分节，`### L<行号>` 是它原来所在的起始行。


## backend-python/_sim_bench.py

### L8

```
# This file lives in backend-python/, one level below the repo root, so it cannot
# import tools/_repo.py the way the tools scripts do. Resolve the two paths it
# needs instead of assuming a working directory: the earlier `'..'` join only
# landed correctly when the caller happened to run from backend-python/.
```

### L23

```
# Cold compute: the first simulate() call in a fresh interpreter also pays for the
# lazily built city directory, province geometry and corridor catalogue.
```

### L1

```
Times the plan computation inside the service process, to reconcile the
end-to-end HTTP build time with the figure recorded in the evidence file.
```


## backend-python/agent_tools.py

### L1

```
An evidence-selecting agent. LLM cannot change geometry or authorize a route.

All displayed statements are retrieved from immutable evidence, so an unavailable
or ungrounded model does not leak an invented number into the user response.
```


## backend-python/decision_service.py

### L23

```
# 台账的结构版本。**改动 `records` 的形状必须同时抬高它并写迁移**，否则老库会带着
# 单主键继续工作，症状是"第二个人做同一组输入时，历史里没有他这一条"。
```

### L47

```
# 全新库：建表语句本身就是新形状，只差盖个版本号。**不重建** ——
# 重建的净效果相同，但会平白全表拷贝一次，而且让"新库"和"老库"走两条路径。
```

### L152

```
# Content address refers to INPUT and model identity; never overwrite a prior record.
# **每行属于一个工作区**：id 仍是内容寻址（同一个人重复提交同一组输入只留一条），
# 但两个工作区各自留自己那一条 —— 见 `_migrate` 里为什么主键必须是 (workspace, id)。
```

### L31

```
v1 → v2：`records` 加 `workspace`，主键从 `id` 改成 `(workspace, id)`。

    **为什么必须动主键。** 内容是内容寻址的（`id = sha256(输入)`），写入用
    `INSERT OR IGNORE` 去重。只加一列而保留单主键 `id` 的话，第二个人提交
    同一组输入时会命中第一个人那条已存在的行、把自己那条 `IGNORE` 掉 ——
    于是他刚做完的评估在历史里查不到，而这次写入按所有判据看都是"成功"的。
    **一个人看不到自己的数据，比串号更难解释。**

    历史行没有工作区（空串），它们属于"未标识"那一档：不带标识的调用方
    （curl、单元测试）读到的还是改造前那一份台账。
```

### L175

```
**本工作区**最近 30 条评估台账。

    不带工作区过滤的版本会让两个人共看一张历史表 —— 而这张表上写着"谁在什么时候
    评估了什么情景"，它比地图上的回波更私人：回波是公共数据，台账是一次动作的留痕。
```


## backend-python/main.py

### L75

```
# ---- 启动预热（0.11.14 软更新）-------------------------------------------------
#
# 为什么需要它。复测实测：切到虚拟通道后要等 90–120 秒。查到两个叠在一起的原因：
#   ① `_prewarm_sibling` 预建的姊妹计划落盘后**读不回来** —— 磁盘恢复那句在比
#      `planRevision`，而 H-2③ 已把它改成递增序号（修复见 service.py 同一处的注释）；
#   ② 进程刚起时 `JOBS` 是空的，第一个访客无论如何都要等一整次建场。
#
# ① 修好后，切通道应当变成一次磁盘命中（约 0.5 秒）。② 只能靠"开机就先算一遍"：
# 这里在启动 5 秒后（让健康检查与 nginx 探测先过）用**未标识工作区**把两个通道各建
# 一次。未标识工作区是 curl / 探针 / 不带 X-ALC-Workspace 的调用看到的那一份，
# 也是"改造前行为"的基线 —— 预热它不会占用任何真实浏览器的命名空间。
#
# 注意：**不常驻内存**。预建走的是 `retain=False` 那条路（一份计划约 110 MB，
# 服务器 1056 MB，常驻两份会把 `JOBS` 上限换算成 440 MB）。落盘即可，命中时一次
# `json.loads` 换掉整次重建。
```

### L257

```
# 503 is the honest answer here: the caller must treat this as "no data",
# which the monitoring round records as a degraded run. Returning a
# fabricated snapshot would be indistinguishable from a real calm day.
```

### L301

```
# ---------------- 轨1 模型推理 ----------------
#
# 这个端点回答的问题只有一个：「按这批实时要素，模型认为几档？」
# 它**不改写**任何航线、**不覆盖**规则判定。融合裁决在 Java 侧 FusionService，
# 安全优先，规则可以否决模型，模型不能否决规则。
```

### L407

```
# 0.11.17（W-5）：班次上的字段名是 `routeId`，`corridorId` 只存在于走廊层载荷。
# 原来只读 `corridorId` ⇒ 这个过滤参数**恒返回 0 条**，而参数看起来是生效的。
```

### L411

```
# 0.11.14（H-3）：`minute` 以前只是被声明、从未被使用 —— 传 0 和传 300 返回同一份
# （`total` 恒 7760，实测 2026-10-23）。现在按"该时刻在空"过滤：
# `departureMinute <= minute < arrivalMinute`。不传时行为完全不变（向后兼容）。
```

### L491

```
# 事件键由前端按预警中心的合并规则生成（`routeId:reason:是否关闭`）。
# 用状态码做白名单而不是自由文本 —— 状态机不能被一个拼错的值悄悄扩展。
```

### L494

```
# 四个**可写**状态（P0-3 ③）。`PENDING` 不在白名单里，因为它不是一个可以被人为
# 设置的值，而是"没有任何记录"时的默认读法。
```

### L511

```
# 停止放行需要落到具体班次上，所以样本班次是必填项 —— 由前端从**同一个**事件行
# 里取（`groupAlerts` 的 `sample`），不在这里再分组一次。
```

### L549

```
# 0.11.17（W-6）：把操作者一起落进台账 —— `_require` 已经确认了角色存在，
# 这里只是把它带下去，免得记录里只有动作没有人物。
```

### L572

```
# ---------------- 0.11.1 概率层（换芯）----------------
#
# 五个只读端点 + 一个落账端点。它们与 `/traffic/*` 的老端点共用同一份 network/session，
# 但不共用字段语义：`p_unsafe` 是概率，"档位"是 v1 的东西。**不要合并**。
#
# 只读端点不计配额（配额只针对大模型端点）；观测缺席时一律 200 + `available:false`，
# 让前端能画出"观测不可用"而不是一个 5xx 错误条。
```

### L584

```
# W-01：结论必须跟着**当前情景**走。不接 scenario 的时候，切到「气象中断」
# 结论条仍然按 live 的天气说"可按计划运行"，而右栏写着"停止放行 7760 班"。
# W-05：`case=fog-20250105` 走历史回放场，与实时同一条管线、同一种指标。
```

### L596

```
# 0.11.4：`le=40` 是假边界。载客走廊 53 条、货运 216 条，上限 40 会让
# 41–53 这 13 条**永远取不到**，而结论条把它写成了"另有 13 条未评估"。
# 上限放到 300（> 269 总条数），默认 53（= 载客全量）。
```

### L647

```
# 0.11.6（E-3）：**这一条才是前端真正写决策的入口**（`DivergencePanel` 调的）。
# 上一版只给 `operations/dispose` 加了检查，而那条前端根本不调 —— 等于
# "门装在了没人走的墙上，真正那扇一直开着"。现在补上，并把角色记进 journal。
```

### L687

```
# ---- 首屏分层（0.11.5 / W-31）------------------------------------------------
#
# `slim` 保留"画图 + 列表 + 时间轴"要用的东西；`full` 用于离线归档与导出。
# 白名单而不是黑名单：**新加字段默认不进 slim** —— 这样"某个字段悄悄变大"不会
# 无声无息地把首屏拖慢，而是要先在这里显式加一行。
```

### L694

```
# 0.11.17（W-5）：`routeId` **必须**在名单里。
#
# 它是预警中心合并事件的键（`${routeId}:${reason}:${closed}`）的一半。原先它不在，
# 于是 1006 条班次全部拼成 `undefined:WEATHER_BLOCKED:true`：屏幕上出现 `undefined`，
# 而且**不同走廊的事件被并成同一条**（处置一次等于处置全部）。
# 代价：约 14 字节 × 条数 —— 换来"事件能分开"和"日志里没有 undefined"。
```

### L701

```
# 0.11.18（F-2）：`payload` 与 `routeId` 是**同一个漏项的两种表现**。
# 调度台账整表渲染 `${payload} 位模拟乘客`，而它不在名单里 ⇒ 复测实测
# **1010 行、没有一行例外**，全是「undefined 位模拟乘客」。
# 上一轮只补了 `routeId`（因为事件键要用），没补 `payload`（因为只有台账要用）——
# 判据是按"谁在用它"补的，漏掉的正是没被想到的那个使用者。
```

### L712

```
# 航迹抽稀上限。12 点足够画出一条城际航迹与高度剖面（首末点必保，时间戳随点保留，
# 所以 `atTime()` 的插值精度不受影响）；选中班次时再用 `/traffic/flight/{id}` 取全量。
# 0.11.11（G-6）：8 → 6。**没有按执行方案删掉 `points`**，因为那份方案的前提不成立：
# 它假设地图读 `mapFlights`（600 条带轨迹），而前端 `CityTrafficMap` 实际画的是
# `flights[].points`（`if(!f.points.length||!scoped(f))continue`）。删掉 points
# 的直接后果是地图上一条航线都不剩 —— 那是"为了瘦身把地图搞坏"，方案 §6.3 自己列为禁区。
# 所以只把抽稀从 8 点降到 6 点（与 `MAP_POINTS` 同档），收益小但零风险。
```

### L722

```
# 地图铺满用的条数上限（0.11.14 / H-1）。
#
# 0.11.12 的 600 条是"合计上限"，结果 494 条名额被配送吃掉，只剩 106 条载客，
# 而配送航迹集中在头两小时 —— 时间轴拖到 T+2:00 地图上一架都没有（复测 BUG-A）。
# 现在改成**三层结构**：载客全量入图 → 空桶补配送 → 超上限才裁剪。
```

### L729

```
# 0.11.15（K-5）：**两条独立的分桶流水线**。
#
# 0.11.14 是一个桶内 CAR 先装满（698 条），配送只在"某桶一条 CAR 都没有"时补 4 条 ——
# 结果 mapFlights 722 条里配送只有 24 条，且起飞时刻集中在 6 个桶里。
# 浏览器实测：T+15 之后「配送无人机」全程 0 架，而业务上配送占 89%（6912 / 7760）。
# 项目讲的是"低空经济 = 无人机配送 + 飞行汽车"，评委拖一下时间轴就会问"无人机呢"。
#
# 现在各分各的桶：CAR 每桶 20 条、DELIVERY 每桶 18 条，16 桶合计 608 条
# （与 0.11.13 的 600 条同量级，体积回到 ~950 KB）。
```

### L740

```
# 地图行每条只留 4 个航迹点（首、两个中间点、末）。
#
# 0.11.14 实测：每行 590 B，而方案估算的是 300 B —— 差在 JSON 的键名与浮点精度上
# （`departureMinute` 一个键就 17 B，`arrivalMinute` 的浮点是 17 位小数）。
# 所以体积只能从"每行点数"上省：6 → 4 约省 50 B/行 × 746 行 ≈ 37 KB。
# 画一条城际航线 4 点够用（它本来就是折线，不是曲线）；要看完整航迹仍走
# `/traffic/flight/{id}`（选中班次时前端本来就这么做）。
```

### L773

```
# 前端据它决定"要不要补取单班"—— 缺字段时它必须知道这是裁剪过的，
# 而不是"这份计划本来就没有许可信息"。
```

### L785

```
# ① **不能简单截前 N 条**。
#
# 实测：7760 条里载客（CAR）只有 848，配送（DELIVERY）6912 —— 占 89%。
# 而前端的 `groupAlerts()` / `batchClearanceRows()` / `observationImpact()`
# 都是**在前端遍历 `flights` 算的**：一刀切到 100 条，处置台的"361 个事件"
# 会变成 5 个 —— 数字变小看着像"情况好转"，实际是数据被裁掉了。
#
# 而且**不能只按 mode 分就完事**。实测：123 班"停止放行"里 62 班是配送 ——
# 如果配送一律只抽前 100 条，那 62 班里有相当一部分会被裁掉，处置台的
# "停止放行 123 班"就变成假数字（而且看起来像"好转"）。
#
# 所以截断的判据是「**这条会不会进入统计**」，不是「它是什么机型」：
#   · 载客：全量（848）—— 事件、批次、处置都看它；
#   · 配送：**所有非 SCHEDULED 的全保留**（出事的都得在），
#           SCHEDULED 的只抽前 SESSION_FLIGHTS 条（那些只是排程，用于铺地图）。
```

### L805

```
# 0.11.11（G-6）：**列表行不带轨迹。** 地图用 ② 那批（600 条、6 点抽稀），
# 选中某一班要看完整航迹时走 `/traffic/flight/{id}`（前端本来就这么做）。
```

### L809

```
# ② 地图铺满用的轻量行（只留画图要的字段，航迹再抽稀到 6 点）
#
# `flights` 不带 `points`，轨迹只走这里：表格要的是"这一班的状态与原因"，
# 地图要的是"这条线画在哪"。塞在一行里，两边都拿到自己不需要的一半。
#
# **0.11.14（H-1）：取样方式重写。** 上一版写的是"载客优先"，实现却是
# **单遍扫描 + 提前 break**：
#     for flight in flights:                      # 原始顺序：配送在前
#         if len(car) + len(delivery) >= 600: break
#         (car if mode=='CAR' else delivery).append(flight)
# 遍历到第 600 条时名额已满，而前 494 条都是配送 —— 载客只挤进 106 条。
# 实测：mapFlights 600 条里配送 494 / 载客 106，`arrivalMinute` 最大只到 127.3，
# 而时间轴开到 480 分钟。**"优先"只写在注释里，没落地。**
# 载客全量，但**分桶只用"有起飞时刻"的那部分**：CLOSED 的班次没有 departureMinute
# （它从未起飞），进地图只会白占体积、且让"覆盖到 480 分钟"的判据失真。
# 实测（measured 通道）：carReturned 848，其中 318 条是 CLOSED 无时刻 —— 这两个数
# 都要在 flightStats 里交代清楚，否则读的人会以为地图漏了 318 条。
```

### L876

```
# ② 桶覆盖步：**每个有起飞的桶至少一条**。
# 复测判据钉着这条（`test_every_half_hour_bucket_has_at_least_one_row`）：
# 只在时刻上覆盖会漏掉末尾那几个桶（最后一桶的班次起飞晚、在空时间短，
# 覆盖步挑不到它），于是"后半段地图没有飞机"的老毛病会以新形状回来。
```

### L900

```
# 两条流水线各分各的桶（K-5）。桶下标 0..15 覆盖 [0,480)；16 号桶是 [480,510)，
# 没有航班在那里起飞，算进去只会白占名额（0.11.14 判据当场报"桶 16 一条都没有"）。
# 每 30 分钟至少要有这么多载具**在空**（载客 6 / 配送 5，按方案 §5 W-7 的验收线）。
```

### L907

```
# 截断会**整段砍掉后一半配送**（载客先拼接），这也是"后半天配送 0 架"的第二个原因。
# 现在两条都按在空配额选过，只有当总数超上限时才按比例收缩，且**交替取**
# （car0, del0, car1, del1 …），保证任何时刻两种机型都在场。
```

### L929

```
# ③ 计数必须**单独给**：前端原来拿 `flights.length` 当总数，
#    一旦截断，100 就会被当成"全部只有 100 条"。
```

### L945

```
# 地图侧的账要单独交代（0.11.14 / H-1）：地图里的载客数、以及"因为已关闭而
# 没有起飞时刻、因此没进地图"的载客数。两个数加起来才是 carReturned。
```

### L950

```
# 地图是**抽样视图**（K-5），所以"地图上的载客 + 因抽样没画 + 因关闭没时刻 = 载客全量"。
# 少一个数，读者就只能得出"地图漏了 500 多条"这个结论 —— 而它其实是设计。
```

### L958

```
# 0.11.17（W-7）：把"在空覆盖"本身作为读数发出去 —— 复测报告是怎么发现
# "配送全程 0 架"的？按时刻数。那这个数就应该由系统自己先说出来。
```

### L975

```
# ---- 预序列化缓存（0.11.6 / E-1⑤）-----------------------------------------
#
# 键里必须同时含 `fields` 与**工作区的 plan 键**：不同工作区是不同计划，
# 共用一份缓存等于把 A 的计划发给 B（这正是项目纪律里最忌的那种隔离失效）。
# 这里用 `traffic_service.session` 返回的 `fingerprint + baseTime` 参与键，
# 计划一重建，键就变，旧缓存自然不会被命中。
```

### L983

```
# **字节上限**（2026-10-24 扫描补）。只限条数是不够的：`fields=full` 的一份计划
# 序列化后约 25 MB，8 条就是 200 MB —— 而这份缓存本来是为了"少序列化几次"，
# 结果比它省下的那点 CPU 贵得多。48 MB 足够放 1 份 full + 几十份 slim（slim 约 0.7 MB）。
```

### L1032

```
# **`full` 投影不缓存**：一份序列化后约 25 MB，而它只在导出/调试时用；
# 为了省那一次 0.3 秒的 dumps 占 25 MB 常驻，是一笔明显亏的账。
# 实测（2026-10-24）：8 条缓存 31.4 MB 里，25 MB 是这唯一一条 full。
```

### L1046

```
# ---- 可观测（0.11.6 / E-2）-------------------------------------------------
#
# 三条纪律：
#   ① 每个检查项都要能说出"值是什么"，不能只给一个布尔 —— "ok: false" 没法排查；
#   ② `degraded` 与 `down` 必须分开：雷达取不到是降级（系统还能用），
#      计划仓读不了才是 down（系统不能用）；
#   ③ 探针**不许写库、不许触发构建** —— 它会被每分钟拉一次，不能有副作用。
```

### L1060

```
# 0.11.7（F-3）：真正的常量叫 `STORE`（`traffic/service.py:40`）。
# 原来我找的是 `STORE_DIR` / `CACHE_DIR`，两个都不存在 → 探针回
# "store dir not exposed"，等于自曝"这一项没接线"。**探针写"没接线"
# 比不写更糟**：它让读的人以为整个健康检查都是摆设。
```

### L1073

```
# 0.11.11（G-5）：**不吐部署绝对路径。**
# `/health` 是匿名的（鉴权已拆），而 `/opt/apps/AIC-tianchuang/.run/traffic`
# 直接告诉访客"这台机器上有什么、装在哪"。信息量也不减：运维要的是
# "计划仓在应用目录下哪一层"，不是根分区长什么样。取最后两级。
```

### L1090

```
# **本项 `ok` 恒 True 是有意的，但必须写出来。**
# 它只报"各通道取到过几帧"，不判断"是不是该取到" —— 因为"没人用"和
# "取不到"在计数上都表现为 0，无法区分。写成 ok=False 会造成常年误报
# （degraded 挂着，真正的降级就没人看了）。读这一项的人要知道：
# **ok=True 只代表探针本身没抛异常，不代表气象通道健康**；健康请看
# `measuredFrames` 是否在涨、以及业务页上的帧时次。
```

### L1120

```
# **"还没调用过"不是故障**（0.11.7 实测：agent_tools.LAST_CALL 初值是
# `NOT_CALLED`，我把它判成 ok=False，于是整个 /health 变 degraded）。
# 缺席要按原因拆开：「没调用」「调用中」「调用失败」是三件事，
# 只有第三件该降级。
```

### L1135

```
# ---- 操作级授权（0.11.6 / E-3）----------------------------------------------
#
# 读：全放行（与拆除登录门之后的行为一致，现场不会撞 401）。
# 写：按 `X-ALC-Operator` 判角色，不够就 403 —— 并且**说清缺什么**。
# 默认（不带头）= 观察员 = 只读：默认值必须是最小权限，否则任何脚本自动获得写权限。
```

### L1165

```
# 200 与 503 的分界必须在**能不能服务**上：down 才 503，
# degraded 仍然 200 —— 否则负载均衡会把一个"雷达取不到但业务正常"的实例摘掉。
```

### L1229

```
# 0.11.14（H-2①）：**通道进 URL，为的是缓存键，不是参数本身**。
# 后端建场仍按全局通道状态（服务端 `_prepared_key` 里已有 weatherMode），
# 这里接受它只为两件事：① 让浏览器把两个通道当成两条 URL；
# ② 回显 `requestedWeatherMode`，让"前端以为切了、后端还没切"的竞态
#    有据可查（不一致时标 `weatherModeMismatch`，前端可据此重取一次）。
```

### L1236

```
# 建场进行中时把"还要多久"讲清楚（0.11.14 软更新）。
#
# 复测反馈：切到虚拟通道后界面只说"正在调度"，没有时长概念，而实际要 90–120 秒 ——
# 等待没有刻度就会被读成卡死。这里补四个字段：阶段、已用、预计剩余、这个计划属于
# 哪个通道。ETA 用按通道分的经验值（measured ≈60 s / virtual ≈110 s），**标成估算**
# —— 它不是进度推算，别让它读起来像承诺。
```

### L1267

```
# 命中即"刚用过"：挪到队尾，让淘汰真的淘汰最久未用的那条。
# 不这么做的话队首永远是最早插入的，而它可能正是正在轮询的那一份。
```

### L1278

```
# 同一份计划在 10 分钟内不会变（它由 seed+scenario+气象快照决定），
# 所以允许客户端缓存；`ETag` 负责让"变了"这件事仍然能被发现。
```

### L1285

```
# 记录"计划构建完成"。**写日志失败不能让计划拿不到** —— 一次磁盘或权限故障
# 不应该表现为整个调度页打不开。失败只记日志，返回值照常。
```

### L1299

```
# 0.11.7（F-2）：**案例必须传下去**。原来这个签名里没有 `case`，
# FastAPI 静默丢弃未知 query → 案例页拿到实时天气，与左侧 2025 年的结论同屏矛盾。
```

### L1307

```
# ---------------- 补充气象通道（2026-10-17）----------------
#
# 这两个端点**故意不进主链路**：主链路每轮加载都要抓一次天气，目标是"不超过 10 秒"
# （README 的验收口径）。把两个额外的外部请求挂上去，就把外部服务的抖动变成了
# 页面加载的抖动。所以它们按需调用 + 各自缓存 10 分钟。
#
# 两者都是**只读、无大模型**，因此不计入每日配额（配额只针对大模型端点）。
```

### L1380

```
# 0.11.11（G-4）：**让上一通道的计划离开内存槽。**
# 计划槽只有 4 个，切通道后新计划要么挤掉别人、要么自己被挤掉；无论哪种，
# 页面上都会出现"新通道的分档统计 + 旧通道的避让几何"并排（0.11.10 复测 N-4）。
# 磁盘文件不删：切回来要能立刻命中那一份已算好的计划。
```

### L1396

```
# ---------------- 实测雷达瓦片：浏览器代取（客户端中继） ----------------
#
# 为什么存在这个入口：瓦片源 `image.data.cma.cn` 对**数据中心出口**整站 403 ——
# 实测在腾讯云上连 `/` 与 `/robots.txt` 都 403，同一 URL 从住宅 IP 则是
# 200 / 131072 B。这不是路径、请求头或协议问题（v4/v6、明文 80、伪装 UA/Referer/
# X-Forwarded-For 全部 403），也没有第二台主机承载同一瓦片集。换源会丢掉"实测"本身。
#
# 但源站返回 `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *`，也就是**浏览器可以直取**；而访问
# 这个系统的浏览器基本都在住宅网络上。于是服务器不再假装自己能取，改成：
# 浏览器取、把**原样字节**交给本接口，解码与派生仍然只在服务器发生一次。
#
# 边界：本接口收的是外部输入，所以只做两件事 —— 校验、然后交给与直取完全相同的
# 解码路径。校验失败一律 422，不"尽力解释"：一条长度不对的瓦片会解出一张位置
# 正确、内容错误的图，比没有图危险得多。
```

### L1437

```
# 中继载荷不合法是**调用方的问题**（422），不是服务器故障（500）。
# 混成 500 会让前端把"取错了"重试成一次网络抖动。
```

### L2

```
天窗智巡 · Python 智能服务（FastAPI）
====================================
端口 8600，由 Spring Boot 主服务（8085）调用。

端点：
  GET  /health                          健康检查 + DeepSeek 配置状态
  GET  /sim/generate                    模拟数据生成（剧本/时长/坐标）
  POST /track2                          轨2 大模型研判（未配置 Key 自动降级）
  POST /agent/ask                       智能体问答（大屏交互窗口）
  POST /track1/ml                       轨1 模型推理（离线训练 + 纯 Python 树打分）
  GET  /track1/ml/status                轨1 模型状态（是否可用 / 版本 / 训练溯源）

配置来源：../config/application.yml 中 deepseek 段（与 Java 侧共用一份配置）
```

### L123

```
把匿名工作区绑到这次请求上（定义见 `workspace.py`）。

    **在中间件里做，而不是给每个路由加一个 header 参数。** 这一侧有十几个
    traffic / decisions 路由，逐个加参数一定会漏掉一个 —— 而漏掉的那个路由会安静地
    用"空工作区"读别人的数据，并且它自己的测试照样全绿（它确实没报错）。中间件只有
    一处，漏不掉；`test_workspace_isolation.py` 再从外面证明它真的生效。
```

### L245

```
真实气象快照与逐时预报，取自 Open-Meteo。

    这个端点取代了原来的 ``/sim/generate``。出口结构逐字段保持一致，所以 Java
    侧的反序列化不需要改动；变化只在内容——``source`` 现在是真实来源，且取不到
    数据时返回 503 而不是编一组值顶上。
```

### L392

```
台账翻页（0.11.6 / E-1②）。

    **为什么不复用 `session` 的 `flights`**：那是"首屏那 100 条"，翻页要的是全量
    里的一段。两者用途不同，共用一个字段就会互相拉扯 —— 首屏想看 100 条，
    台账想翻到第 70 页，谁也不该改谁。
```

### L434

```
按 id 批量取**完整航迹**（0.11.6 / E-1③）。

    上限 50 条：这不是为了省事，是为了让调用方**别把"按需取"用成"分批取全量"** ——
    每次 50 条、翻 156 次，就等于把 7760 条又拉了一遍，只是慢 156 倍。
    超过上限返回 400，并说明上限是多少。
```

### L462

```
单班全量（0.11.5 / W-31）：首屏用 `fields=slim`，选中班次时用这个补全。

    找不到就是 404，**不返回一份"字段都在但值为空"的壳** —— 那种壳会让前端把
    "这条班次不存在"演成"这条班次没有许可信息"。
```

### L502

```
一键处置（P0-3 ①）。

    `action` 与 `state` 分开：状态机说的是"这件事到哪一步了"，动作说的是"做了什么"。
    `SUSPEND` 与 `HANDLED` 都可能把状态从"已确认"推走，但交班时要读得出是哪一种。
```

### L523

```
事件处置台的状态转移：待确认 → 已确认 → 处置中 → 已处置 → 已复核（P0-3）。

    0.11.17（W-6）：**操作者不能是空的**。

    复测报告 P-4 的现象是台账里写「人物：absent」—— 而这个项目的立身之本之一是
    "人在环上、可追溯"。一个写着"没有人"的台账，比没有台账更糟：它证明这条流程
    可以没有人在场就走完。原来 `actor` 只从请求体读，前端从没放入，于是永远是空。

    现在：体里没有就取 `X-ALC-Operator` 头；两处都没有则 **403 并说清缺什么**。
    注意这里只拒绝"没报身份"，不校验身份真假 —— 这一层的定位是**可追溯**，不是鉴权
    （真正的边界在 nginx 限速与每日配额，见 `docs/安全开关启用评估与回滚.md`）。
```

### L618

```
把一条走廊的结构化证据翻译成一句人话（手册 §9.3）。

    返回里一定带 `source`（MODEL / TEMPLATE）与 `guard.violations`：
    "AI 写的"与"模板写的"在界面上必须是两种东西，否则下一次读的人会以为
    模板句也是模型生成的（而模型那时可能正好不可用）。
```

### L761

```
裁剪一行。`with_points=False` 用于**分页列表**（0.11.7 / F-5）。

    分页接口原来复用 `_slim_row`（带 8 点航迹），于是 `size=100` 的响应里 46% 是
    points —— 而列表根本不画轨迹。轨迹要用 `/traffic/flights/points` 单独取。
    `slim: true` 这个标记也因此名副其实：**列表行是真的瘦的**。
```

### L991

```
按「条数」与「总字节」双重上限淘汰，淘汰的永远是最久未用的那条。

    `_PREPARED` 是普通 dict，Python 3.7+ 保序；命中时会 `pop` 再插回末尾
    （见读取处），所以**队首就是最久未用**。用 dict 而不是 OrderedDict，
    是因为这里只需要"保序 + 取队首 + 删任意键"，多一个类型反而多一层要同步的东西。
```

### L1006

```
预序列化缓存的键。**必须等于「这份计划是谁」**，所以用计划键本身。

    2026-10-24 修：这里原先返回
    ``(fingerprint, seed, scenario, baseTime, weatherMode, fields)`` ——
    而 ``fingerprint`` 是 **代码指纹**（`traffic/*.py` 的哈希），
    **全工作区、全种子、全场景都是同一个值**。于是键里实际**没有工作区这一维**，
    两个浏览器用同样参数打开时：

        ws-alpha → 200, sessionId=9538a9f4…, md5=864b8a70…
        ws-beta  → 200, sessionId=9538a9f4…, md5=864b8a70…   ← 拿的是 alpha 的

    实测两份响应**逐字节相同**，`sessionId` 也是 alpha 的。这正是本文件这一段
    上方注释里写着要防的那件事（"共用一份缓存等于把 A 的计划发给 B"），
    而键里就是没有那一维 —— 注释是对的，代码不是。

    ``result['sessionId']`` 就是 ``traffic.service.identity()`` 的产物，已经含
    seed／scenario／气象快照／雷达通道／**工作区**。**少一维就有一维的串扰**，
    所以这里不再"补字段"，直接用它；``fields`` 只区分 slim / full 两种投影。
```

### L1107

```
LLM 通道状态。**读真实调用记录，不自己发一次请求**。

    0.11.7（F-3）：原来这里回 `'llm probe not wired'` —— 一句自曝。
    真实的调用状态在 `agent_tools.LAST_CALL`（每次调用都会 update
    `status/source/latencyMs`），读它既能反映真实情况，又不会让探针
    每次被拉取都去打一次付费 API。
```

### L1316

```
各城市**当前**天气（城市气象条用）。

    与 `/traffic/weather` 的分工要说清：那一个发的是**判据用的**气象快照
    （站点实测 + 概率模型），这一个发的是**展示用的**公开模式当前值（气温、天气现象、风）。
    两者不同源，所以**绝不能**拿这里的数字去解释某条走廊为什么被关掉。
```

### L1362

```
切换气象通道：measured（实测）或 virtual（虚拟）。

    切换在下次请求即生效，无需重启。模式非法时返回 422，不做静默纠正。

    0.11.7（F-1）：**权限按方向分**。进虚拟态要 admin（它会让全场结论变成模拟的），
    回实测态任何人都可以（安全侧必须永远可恢复）。原来两向都要 admin，
    一旦用 admin 切过去，普通用户在页面上就再也切不回来 —— 单向门。
```

### L1428

```
接受访问者浏览器代取回来的实测雷达瓦片，存入本工作区。

    存入范围是**发起请求的那个工作区**：中继数据天然来自"某个访问者的浏览器"，
    因此一个客户端塞进来的东西最多只影响它自己那块屏，不会改到别人正在看的实测场。
```

### L846

```
先保证**每个采样时刻都有在空的**，再按起飞时刻均匀补足配额。

        0.11.17（W-7）：原来只按"起飞分钟分桶"，对配送（中位航程 6 分钟）等于
        把每桶 18 条全塞在桶首 —— 桶中后段在空 = 0，复测报告看到的就是"配送无人机
        全程 0 架"。按在空配额选，才和屏幕上要回答的问题（"此刻天上有什么"）对齐。

        `min_in_air` 是**硬目标**：达不到时如实记录（`_in_air_shortfall`），
        不靠"再多塞几条"制造假象 —— 有时确实没有那么多班次同时在空。
```


## backend-python/ml/__init__.py

### L1

```
轨1 轻量模型（0.10.1 换芯）。

模块边界：
- `labels`  —— 标注判据（造标签用，不参与运行放行）
- `features` —— 从气象快照组装特征向量（训练与推理共用同一套字段名）
- `forest`  —— 纯标准库的树集成打分器（运行时不依赖 sklearn）
- `service` —— 模型加载与推理服务（`/track1/ml` 的唯一实现）
```


## backend-python/ml/features.py

### L21

```
# 顺序即模型输入顺序。**改顺序必须重训**，所以导出模型时会把这份顺序写进 JSON，
# 加载时逐项核对；顺序对不上直接拒绝推理，而不是"按位置凑合"。
```

### L1

```
特征组装：训练与推理共用同一套字段名与同一套派生规则。

**这里最容易被忽略的一件事**：运行时快照（`real_weather.snapshot`）与历史样本的字段名不一样，
而且单位也不一样 ——

| 运行时快照 | 训练样本 | 差异 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `humidity` = 0–1 小数 | `rhPct` = 0–100 | **乘以 100** |
| `cloudCover` = 0–1 小数 | `cloudPct` = 0–100 | **乘以 100** |
| `pressureHpa`（地面气压） | `pressureHpa`（海平面气压） | 同一名字、不同量；见 `pressureNote` |
| `tempC` / `windSpeed` / `windGust` / `precipMmh` / `visibilityM` | 同名 | 一致 |

不把这张表写死在脑里、而写进代码，是因为"训练时叫 A、线上叫 B"这类错配**不会报错**：
它会安静地把相对湿度 0.62 当成 0.62% 喂进模型，然后给出一个看起来完全正常的档位。
```

### L55

```
把 0–1 的小数换算成 0–100 的百分数；已经是百分数（>1.5）时原样返回。

    为什么允许"已经是百分数"：样本侧直接给 0–100。如果只按"乘 100"处理，
    历史样本会被放大成 6200%，模型立刻崩，但**不会报错**。
```


## backend-python/ml/forest.py

### L77

```
# 未舍入的概率：只在"导出后与 sklearn 逐条对照"时使用。
# 用舍入后的 4 位去对照会把 5e-5 的舍入误差读成"导出走样"。
```

### L1

```
纯标准库的树集成打分器。

为什么不用 pickle + sklearn 在运行时加载：
1. pickle 与 sklearn 版本强绑定。升级一次依赖，线上加载就会失败，而失败发生在
   **启动时**，看起来像"服务挂了"而不是"模型版本不对"。
2. 运行时多一个重依赖（sklearn 连带 scipy），而推理只需要"比阈值、走分支、投票"。
3. 树是纯比较与加法，**可以逐条复算** —— 报告里那句"模型可复算"只有在这种形态下才成立。

导出格式（`models/ml-v1.json`）刻意做成"人能读、能 diff"：每棵树是一串嵌套数组，
节点是 `[feature_index, threshold, left, right]`，叶子是各类计数数组。
```

### L83

```
Top-k 影响因子 = 全局重要性 × 该样本的标准化偏离。

        这不是 Shapley 值，也不假装是。选它的理由：① 纯函数、可复算；② 与树的
        分裂重要性同源（重要性来自分裂增益），所以"重要的特征"与"该样本偏离大的特征"
        两边都有依据；③ 不依赖 sklearn 在运行时存在。**在界面与报告里必须写清这是
        近似归因，不是逐条 SHAP**。
```


## backend-python/ml/labels.py

### L1

```
标注判据（`config/labels-v1.json`）的纯函数实现。

为什么单独一个模块、而不是塞进 `rules.py`：
两个问题的聚合方式**必须不同**。运行放行是"累减扣分 + 一票封顶"，回答"这一轮能不能飞"；
标注判据是"逐要素分段 + 取最坏档"，回答"这一小时该标成哪一档"。
实测同一批 2160 小时：累减扣分给出 BAN 65.0% / DANGER 0.14%（中档被两侧挤掉），
分段取最坏档给出四档同量级。把它们合并成一个函数，等于逼其中一方接受另一方的偏差。
（关系声明写在 `config/labels-v1.json::relationToRuntimeRules` 里，不在代码里重复。）
```

```
# **顺序即模型输入顺序**，训练脚本从这里 import，避免两处各写一份。
# `stationCode` 是站点身份（0/1/2）：三个站的雾/风气候不同，不把它给模型，
# 等于强迫一棵树用同一套分裂同时拟合沈阳的辐射雾和哈尔滨的锋面大风。
```

### L56

```
# 决策口径（写在页面上，允许评委质疑参数）。
#
# 0.11.15（K-4）：**阵风阈值搬进 config/rules-v1.json 的 `operations.gustMps`**。
# 此前它在这里写 10，而风险区几何在 traffic/weather.py 写 17 —— 同一次请求里两套阈值，
# 结果是"10 条走廊判不可飞，地图上只有 1 个风险区且在葫芦岛，与那 10 条一条都不重合"，
# 评委问"你说这 10 条禁飞，风险区在哪"时没有答案。
#
# 现在两边都读同一个键，改阈值只改配置文件一处。
```

### L71

```
# **不许静默**。三个阈值会回落到 1000 / 3000 / **11.5**，而风险区几何侧
# （`traffic/weather.py`）用的是另一个数（17）。两边一旦分叉，"哪 10 条判不可飞"
# 和"地图上画了哪个风险区"就对不上 —— 上层注释记录的正是这个故障。
```

### L177

```
# 3 小时前的同一要素（用于变化率）。历史不足 3 小时就不给这个特征，
# 让它走中位数填充并点亮对应的缺失口径 —— 不假装"三小时前等于现在"。
```

### L262

```
# 两个要素相互独立是本模型的**显式假设**：合并时用 1-(1-a)(1-b)。
# 逆温雾天常伴静风，独立性不严格成立；这条写在页面上，不藏起来。
```

### L270

```
# ---- 档位：概率档与观测档分开算，取更严的一方（0.11.3 追加）------------
#
# 只按概率给档位会漏掉最要紧的一种情况：**此刻已经在禁飞级里**。
# 实测例子（2025-01-05 00:00Z 沈阳 ZYTX）：能见度 997.8 m（禁飞级），
# 而模型校准后的不可飞概率只有 0.31 —— 因为该校准概率的含义是
# "未来 3 小时会不会不可飞"，而"现在就是"这件事它答不了。
# 一个走廊如果只因为"未来概率 31%"被判"可飞"，那是把**正在发生的雾**读成预报。
```

### L2

```
低空可飞性概率推理（0.11.1 换芯）。

与 0.10.1 的 `ml/service.py` 的分工：
    `ml/service.py`  —— 旧的四档分类（FIT/CAUTION/DANGER/BAN），标签取判据，
                        保留供对照与回溯，**不再进决策链**；
    `ml/nowcast.py` —— 新：低能见度 / 强阵风的发生概率，标签取 METAR 实测，
                        进决策链（期望损失 + 置信度门限）。

三条纪律：
1. **模型只吃观测**（METAR 当前值与过去 6 小时），不吃任何模式预报值。
   模式值只在"模式延伸"里用，且必须与模型结论分开标注。
2. **缺席就说缺席**。站点没有足够历史时返回 `available: false` + 原因，
   不拿气候平均值顶一个看起来正常的概率。
3. **概率必须校准过**。模型输出经 isotonic 校准表映射后才外发；
   未校准的原始概率只在 `raw` 里出现，供诊断，不进决策。
```

### L156

```
一行观测 + 它之前的逐小时历史 → 定长向量。

    `history` 是**同一站点**按时间升序的最近若干小时（不含 row 本身）。
    训练侧与这里用同一段代码语义：训练脚本通过 `build_features()` 调它，
    避免"训练时叫 A、线上叫 B"那类不会报错的错配。
```

### L294

```
按**已经发生**的观测给档位（0 代表无风险，不是"没数据"）。

    阈值与 `_criteria` 用的是同一组：能见度 <1000 m 或阵风 >10 m/s 为停飞档，
    能见度 <3000 m 为注意级。两份阈值若各写一次，迟早在某一天分叉 ——
    所以这里只写一次，`_criteria` 与它同源。
```

### L94

```
# 缺测太多时"自信"是假的：中位数填充会把整条样本拉向气候平均，
```

### L1

```
轨1 模型推理服务：`/track1/ml` 的唯一实现。

三条纪律：
1. **模型缺席就如实说缺席**。返回 `available: false` + 原因，绝不编一个档位顶上去
   —— 本项目吃过这个亏：`BUSY` 分支没有 `progress` 键，前端 `Number(undefined)` 得到 0，
   于是"排队中"被渲染成"已开始"。
2. **不越安全底线**。这里只给"模型认为几档"，不改写任何航线、不覆盖规则判定；
   融合仍由 Java 侧 `FusionService` 按安全优先裁决。
3. **provenance 随结果返回**。`labelsSha256` / 训练日期 / sklearn 版本都在里面，
   报告与界面都能回到"这批结论是哪一版模型给的"。
```

```
# claimed. A fabricated probability would drive the SFI rule directly.
```

### L174

```
# Align to the first hourly row at or after the present hour, so the forecast
# starts now rather than repeating hours that have already passed.
```

### L2

```
真实气象源（Open-Meteo）——替代原模拟数据生成器。

为什么存在这个模块
------------------
本系统的监测看板原先用 ``simulator.py`` 的四套合成剧本（clear/wind/rain/storm）
生成气象快照，再喂给 SFI 规则引擎。合成快照在界面上和真实观测长得一模一样，
唯一的区别是它并不对应任何一次真实观测——这正是「气象必须可追溯」这条纪律要
禁止的东西。

本模块把同一个出口换成真实来源：

  * 上游是 Open-Meteo 的公共预报接口（零凭据、可长期访问）；
  * 返回结构与被替换的 ``simulator.generate()`` **逐字段对齐**，所以 Java 侧的
    ``WeatherSnapshot`` / ``ForecastHour`` 反序列化不需要改动；
  * ``source`` 字段写的是真实来源（``open-meteo`` / ``open-meteo-model``），
    不再是 ``sim``；``dataType`` 明确标注这是**模式估算**而不是测站观测。

一条硬约束
----------
**取不到就报错，不补合成数据。** 调用方拿到 ``SourceUnavailable`` 必须显式降级
（例如把本轮标记为数据不可用），不允许用任何编造的值顶上——那会让下游分不清
「天气晴好的实测结论」和「我们没拿到数据」。
```

### L80

```
取当前位置的真实气象快照与逐时预报。

    返回 ``{"snapshot": {...}, "hourly": [...]}``，字段与被替换的合成生成器完全
    一致。任何网络或解析失败都抛 :class:`SourceUnavailable`。
```


## backend-python/rules.py

### L26

```
# 有效范围（与 Java ObservationValidator 一致）：缺失、非有限或越界一律判为不可用，
# 不得默认成安全值。温度允许负值，不能套用非负校验。
```

### L81

```
# required=False 表示当前数据源不发布该要素：缺席时弃权（不计分、不臆断、
# 也不判本轮失败）。默认 True 保持既有契约——本该有却没有的要素仍须拒绝。
```

### L1

```
轨1 规则的 Python 实现（C02）。

与 Java 侧 `SfiEngine` **读取同一份配置** `config/rules-v1.json`，并实现相同的比较符语义，
使两端在任何输入下得到一致的 rawSfi / sfi / level / hardBan。`tests/fixtures/rule-cases.json`
作为跨语言 golden cases 同时被两端断言，任何一侧偏离都会立即失败。

比较符语义（与 Java 完全一致）：
    direction=high ：v >= banValue 禁飞；v >= warnValue 警戒
    direction=low  ：v <= banValue 禁飞；v <= warnValue 警戒
    direction=range：v < banLow 或 v > banHigh 禁飞；v < warnLow 或 v > warnHigh 警戒
```

### L180

```
按配置计算规则结果。

    缺失/非法**必需**字段抛 ValueError（不得默认判定适飞）。
    但 ``required=False`` 的要素缺席是**正常状态**（源本就不发布该字段），
    此时弃权：不计分、不臆断、也不判本轮失败——并把它的标签记进
    ``unavailable_optional``，使界面能把它与「已检查且正常」区分开。
```

```
天窗智巡 · 轨2 大模型研判智能体
====================================
基于 DeepSeek（OpenAI 兼容接口）的低空飞行气象研判智能体。

输出五元组结构化 JSON（与计划书 5.4 节一致）：
  risk_level   适飞 | 谨慎 | 危险 | 禁飞
  confidence   0.0-1.0
  key_factors  主要影响要素及原因（数组）
  advice       具体作业建议
  alert_text   可直接发布的预警文本

可靠性工程（对应计划书 5.4）：
  1. JSON Mode 强约束输出
  2. Schema 校验 + 失败自动重试（2 次）
  3. 全部失败时降级为本地规则研判（source=fallback），主流程零阻塞
```

## backend-python/traffic/airspace.py

### L1

```
Simulated airspace-layer allocation.

The declared cruise layers in ``aircraft-profiles.json`` are the only altitudes a
flight may hold. Conflicts are resolved by choosing another *declared* layer,
reserving a time slot, or holding on the ground — never by stacking an arbitrary
increment on top of the previous altitude, because that invents an altitude the
aircraft profile never authorised.

Every value here is a project simulation parameter. It is not real airspace
permission and not a certified aircraft performance figure.
```

### L32

```
Deterministic layer preference order.

    Opposite directions get opposite ends of the declared set so head-on
    traffic is vertically separated first, then corridor parity rotates the
    choice so a shared corridor does not always pin to the same layer.
```

### L28

```
# 平台内置的案例清单。这里写死是**故意的**：它不是动态发现的目录，
# 而是"我们为演示准备过哪些案例"这样一句可被审计的话。
```

### L56

```
# 文件名与 caseId 不一致时拒绝加载：这类错会让"点进去的是 A、算出来的是 B"
# 一直不报错，只在有人对照 URL 时才看得出来。
```

### L2

```
历史回放案例（0.11.3 · W-05）。

这一层只做三件事：**读案例文件、给出一份可被 `nowcast.predict_station` 吃的历史、
说出这份数据是从哪抄来的**。它不做插值、不改数值、不补缺失 ——
案例文件里的每个观测值都必须逐字来自 METAR（生成脚本见
`tools/maintenance/_build_case_fog_20250105.py`）。

为什么要独立一层：`v2.field()` 的数据来源原本只有一个（实时 METAR 拉取）。
让 `field()` 直接认识"案例"会把它变成一个既能拉实时又能读磁盘的混合体，
于是"这个数字是现在的还是回放的"就只能靠记。这里把来源显式化：
`field(case_id=...)` 走本模块，返回的载荷里带 `HISTORICAL_REPLAY` 与 `timeBase`，
前端据此画"历史回放，非实时"的横幅 —— 判定依据在载荷里，不在文案里。
```

### L96

```
案例第 ``minute`` 分钟对应的**最近**逐时帧（不插值）。

    `frames` 是基准时刻起的逐小时帧（本案例 7 帧 = 0~360 分钟）。
    取最近帧而不是插值：气象观测是逐时发布的，插出来的中间值没有对应报文，
    一旦进判据就是"这个数没有出处"。拖到 470 分钟时给 06:00Z 那一帧，
    并在返回值里带 `frameLagMinutes`，让上层知道差了多久（不假装它正好是 470）。
```

```
各城市当前天气（城市气象条用）。

为什么单独一个模块而不是塞进 `weather_snapshot`：
    主链路每轮加载都要抓一次天气，验收口径是"不超过 10 秒"。多抓 20 个城市会把
    外部服务的抖动直接变成页面加载的抖动。所以这里**按需调用 + 缓存 10 分钟**，
    与 `aviation_observations` / `weather_supplements` 同一策略。

口径（写在页面上也要能看到）：
    · 数据源 = Open-Meteo `current`，是**公开模式/观测融合产品**，不是 METAR 实测；
    · 只用于"城市气象条"这种氛围性展示（天气现象、气温、风），
      **不参与任何走廊判据**——判据只用 METAR 与概率模型；
    · 取不到就返回 `available:false` 与原因，不编造数字。
```

```
WMO 天气代码 → 中文短句。

    只做分类，不做修饰：这条天气条的作用是"让页面有天气"，不是天气播报。
```

```
    20 个城市不是一个 20 次请求的问题，而是一个请求的问题。
```

```
# The remaining radar products advertised by the data centre (basic reflectivity,
# echo top, vertically integrated liquid, 24-hour precipitation estimate) are
# intentionally absent rather than guessed: a wrong code returns a confusing

```

### L57

```
# Match the rest of the backend, which reaches out with trust_env=False so a
# developer's proxy settings cannot silently reroute a data request.
```

### L197

```
# Only present on platforms that expose a direct object URL; kept optional
# because the documented element list does not include it.
```

### L271

```
# A missing key file that got silently re-minted is unrecoverable for the
# rows already in the database, so it is surfaced alongside readiness rather
# than left for an operator to discover through a decryption failure.
```

### L1

```
China Meteorological Data Service Centre (中国气象数据网) radar adapter.

Endpoint contract, taken from the product page for 天气雷达组网组合反射率图像产品
(资料编码 ``RADA_L3_MST_V3_CREF_PNG``)::

    http://api.data.cma.cn:8090/api
        ?userId=<账号>
        &pwd=<密码>
        &dataFormat=json
        &interfaceId=getRadaFileByTimeRange
        &dataCode=RADA_L3_MST_V3_CREF_PNG
        &timeRange=[YYYYMMDDHHMISS,YYYYMMDDHHMISS]
        &elements=Station_Id_C,DATETIME,FORMAT,FILE_NAME

Two properties of this API drive the design:

1. **Credentials travel in the query string over plain HTTP.** There is no
   header-based or HTTPS option. Every URL this module produces must therefore
   pass through :func:`safe_url` before it can reach a log, an error message or
   a cache key; :data:`STRICT_TRANSPORT` can be switched on to refuse the
   insecure transport outright.
2. **The published window is only the last 7 days**, so a request outside that
   window fails early with an explicit message instead of being silently
   clipped to a partial result.

The account credentials themselves live in the encrypted store (see
``secret_store``); this module never accepts them as literal arguments from the
rest of the application.
```

### L71

```
Return ``url`` with every credential occurrence masked.

    Used for logging and for building error text. The data centre API puts both
    ``userId`` and ``pwd`` in the query string, so an unmasked URL in a traceback
    would leak the account.
```

### L164

```
Pull the file list out of whichever envelope the platform returned.

    The platform documents a JSON response but not its wrapper, so all three
    shapes seen in practice are accepted instead of assuming one and failing on
    the others.
```

### L204

```
List radar product files for a time range.

    Returns ``{available, files, provenance, error}``. On any failure the file
    list is empty and nothing is written: a partial or fabricated listing would
    be indistinguishable from a real one downstream.
```

### L262

```
Credential and capability summary for the data-source panel.

    Reports *only* whether a credential exists and its fingerprint — never the
    credential itself.
```


## backend-python/traffic/cma_tiles.py

### L53

```
# How many 6-minute steps back to walk when hunting for the newest published
# frame. Publication lags real time by a few minutes, so the newest nominal
# slot is frequently not there yet; 12 steps covers an hour of lag with margin.
```

### L59

```
# Reflectivity thresholds, in dBZ. These are the operational bands from the
# Chinese radar business standard (QX/T 出版量级, 对流判据 35 dBZ / 45 dBZ /
# 60 dBZ), not invented cut-offs:
#   < 15        no echo worth modelling
#   15 - 35     stratiform rain; flyable, not a hazard area
#   35 - 45     convective; the classic threshold for a convective cell
#   45 - 60     strong convection, heavy rain and gust fronts
#   >= 60       severe convection, hail-capable
```

### L76

```
# Default sampling window at zoom 3. At this zoom one tile spans 45 degrees, and
# the product does NOT cover the globe uniformly -- it publishes a fixed set of
# tiles. Those were enumerated by direct request (see ``_probe_tile_coverage.py``)
# rather than assumed: zoom 3 serves only (5,2) (6,2) (5,3) (6,3), so a grid that
# reaches further east than x=6 gets 404s on every attempt.
#
# The two tiles below are the ones that contain the northeast operating area:
#   (6,2)  90.09E-134.91E, 66.48N-41.05N   -- the northern half, and the busy one
#   (6,3)  90.09E-134.91E, 40.91N- 0.09N   -- the southern half
# Together they span 90-135E and 0-66N, which fully contains the 115-135E /
# 38-54N box this system flies in.
```

### L89

```
# 同一次尝试内的瓦片彼此独立，串行取只是历史写法。默认网格 2 块、每块一次
# HTTP，并发取把这次尝试的墙钟减半，而**失败传播语义不变**：任一块失败即该
# 时次不可用，落回下一个时次（`MAX_LOOKBACK_STEPS`）。
```

### L131

```
# **路径里的时间字面值是北京时（UTC+8），不是 UTC。**
#
# 这个错误曾经真实存在过，而且长得完全不像错误：老版本用 UTC 取整到 6 分钟网格，
# 于是每次请求的都是 **8 小时前**的那个时次。瓦片站保留约一天的数据，所以那一档
# 确实存在、确实返回 200、`decode_tile` 也确实解出合理的 dBZ —— 一切判据都是绿的，
# 唯一不对劲的是数据是清晨的，而屏上写着"刚刚"。
#
# 更糟的是 `slotAgeMinutes` 会**自洽地**报出 ~5 分钟，因为它是拿同一个错的时次去和
# "现在"相减的：错的参考系减错的参考系，差是对的。这正是本项目最怕的那种假证据。
#
# 判定方式（`tools/probes/_probe_cma_slot_tz.py`，可复跑）：枚举 now-9h..now+9h 的
# 路径并找存在的最新一档。若路径是 UTC，最新一档应落在 now 之前（发布滞后）；若
# 是北京时，把"北京当下"写成路径字面值会落在 **now 之后约 8 小时**。实测 2026-10-15
# 07:24Z：最新存在时次 `20261015/15/12`，偏移 **+7.80 h** ⇒ 北京时，发布滞后约 12 分钟。
```

### L297

```
# 服务器取不到时，把"能不能让浏览器代取"一并说清楚 —— 否则前端只能
# 显示一句"不可用"，而真正的下一步（换一条传输路径）就丢在服务端了。
```

### L308

```
# `slot` 保持"绝对时刻(UTC)"这一向后兼容的读法；`slotLocal` 是路径字面值，
# 也就是运维人员能在瓦片站上直接对上的那个时间。两个都给，是因为只给一个
# 总有一类读法会读错。
```

### L424

```
# 传输路径与直取不同，必须标出来：一个运维看到 CLIENT_RELAY 才知道
# "这批字节是访问者的浏览器带回来的"，而不是服务器自己取到的。
```

### L1

```
Uncredentialed CMA radar mosaic tiles -> real dBZ risk field.

This is the one real observed-radar source the backend can reach with a bare
HTTP GET: no account, no token, no cookie, no browser. It was verified by direct
request (see ``_probe_cma_radar.py``) before this module existed, because the
whole "replace the synthetic cloud field with measured data" plan rests on it.

Where it sits
-------------
``weather_sources`` answers *"what is the weather at these city coordinates"* --
one point value per city, from a forecast model or a provider estimate, which is
why the synthetic field is built on top of it. That is fine for wind and
visibility, but it cannot say where a convective cell actually is, because a
handful of points cannot describe an area.

This module answers the other question: *"where is the radar echo, right now"*.
The product is the national composite reflectivity mosaic on a regular grid, so
the echo geometry is measured rather than modelled. The two are complementary and
are deliberately kept in separate modules with separate provenance.

Payload format (verified against fetched bytes, not documentation)
------------------------------------------------------------------
Each tile is ``256 x 256`` **big-endian int16**, **not** an image despite the
``GISJPG`` in the path. Value ``* 0.1`` is dBZ and ``32767`` marks a missing
cell. Rows are stored inverted, i.e. row 0 is the *southernmost* row. Both facts
have to be respected or the echo lands in the wrong place; the probe checks the
value distribution and the row order rather than assuming either.

Published cadence is 6 minutes. Only recent frames exist (the site keeps a
rolling window), so a request for an old frame simply 404s -- the caller sees
``available: False`` with an explicit reason and no geometry is emitted. That is
the correct failure mode: a fabricated risk area is far worse than no risk area.
```

### L104

```
Fetch one attempt's tiles concurrently, **in the order they were given**.

    Failures are returned rather than raised, so the caller's "first failure
    wins" rule is not changed by the concurrency. With two workers the second
    tile is already in flight when the first fails; letting it raise would report
    whichever tile happened to finish last, and the reported tile is what tells
    an operator whether the source or the network is at fault.
```

### L149

```
Newest nominal 6-minute publication slot at or before ``now``.

    **返回值是北京时**，即路径字面值所在的那个时区 —— 直接把它的 ``%H:%M`` 写进
    路径就是对的。命名上刻意不叫 ``latest_slot_utc``：调用方唯一的正确用法就是把它
    交给 :func:`tile_url` 或 :func:`slot_instant`，任何"顺手当 UTC 用"的读法都是 bug。
```

### L161

```
The absolute instant a path-literal ``slot`` names.

    ``slot`` 视为北京时；返回带 UTC 时区的 datetime。**凡是要和"现在"相减，必须
    走这里** —— 直接拿北京时的 slot 减 UTC 的 now 会得到一个恒为 +8 小时的差。
```

### L194

```
Decode a tile into a row-major list of dBZ values (``None`` where missing).

    **Row 0 of the raw grid is the northernmost row.** The rows are therefore kept
    in the order they arrive; no flip is applied.

    That was not the first guess -- an earlier version reversed them, on the
    assumption that a GIS product would store row 0 at the south edge. The two
    readings are mirror images, so the error is invisible in a summary statistic
    and shows up only in where the echo lands. It was settled by looking at the
    missing-data pattern, which is asymmetric for a nationwide mosaic: tile (6,3)
    spans 41N down to the equator, and there is no Chinese radar coverage over the
    South China Sea or the open tropics, so the missing fraction must *increase*
    toward the south edge. Under the raw order the tile goes 62.9% missing at 39N
    to 100% missing by 8N, and the only echo sits at the northern end near 39N.
    Under the flipped order the populated end moves to the equator, which no
    radar network reaches. The raw order is the correct one.
```

### L219

```
Angular size of one tile cell at a web-mercator zoom level.

    Standard slippy-map tiling: the whole world is 360 degrees over
    ``256 * 2**zoom`` pixels, so one pixel spans ``360 / (256 * 2**zoom)`` degrees
    of longitude.
```

### L247

```
Fetch the newest available radar frame.

    ``grid`` is an iterable of ``(x, y)`` tile coordinates; the default is a small
    window over northeast China, which is the area this system operates in. The
    result is a dict with an explicit ``available`` flag: on any failure the
    ``cells`` list is empty and ``error`` says why.

    Nothing is invented. A frame that could not be fetched returns no cells --
    never a partial or synthesised one, because downstream cannot tell those from
    a measurement.
```

### L332

```
告诉浏览器去哪里取、按什么口径拼路径。

    时次口径必须一并下发。路径字面值是北京时；浏览器若按本地时区或 UTC 拼，
    会稳定地取到另一个时次的图，而那个错法**看起来和"取到了"完全一样** ——
    这正是本轮在服务端踩过的坑（见 SLOT_TZ 上方注释）。
```

### L373

```
用客户端替我们取回的瓦片字节，拼出与 :func:`frame` 同形的载荷。

    为什么走这条路而不是换源：数据的**来源没变**（还是 CMA 的实测瓦片），
    变的只是**传输路径** —— 服务器出口被源站按 IP 拒绝，而浏览器在住宅网络上可直取，
    源站又返回 ``Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *``。所以这里只做两件事：
    校验字节、交给与直取完全相同的 :func:`decode_tile`。几何仍只由 ``radar_field``
    派生一次，浏览器不做任何换算。

    校验失败的处置是**抛异常**，不是"尽力解释"：中继来的载荷是外部输入，
    一条长度不对的瓦片解出来会是一张位置正确的错图，比没有图危险得多。
```

### L444

```
Lon/lat of a tile cell, using standard web-mercator tile maths.

    Returns ``(longitude, latitude)`` in degrees. ``row`` counts from the tile's
    north edge, which is also the raw grid's row 0 (see :func:`decode_tile`), so
    the value passes through without adjustment.
```


## backend-python/traffic/delivery.py

### L79

```
# A closed order keeps its identity: both the order number and the
# hub-slot vehicle are derived from the flight, so the ledger stays
# complete and a cancellation can still be referenced. Only the
# outcome changes; nothing is silently dropped.
```

### L1

```
Synthetic city delivery orders and drone vehicle state.

Orders and vehicles are simulated. Merchant and receiver names are generated
labels, not real businesses, and the energy model is an estimate rather than a
measured endurance figure. The module is deliberately separate from the flight
scheduler: it consumes the published plan and never changes an acceptance
decision, so a delivery-level exception cannot silently override a safety
constraint.
```

### L25

```
Order identity, derived from the flight rather than carried alongside it.

    Defined once and used both for accepted and for cancelled orders: an order
    number belongs to the order, not to the vehicle assignment, so a closed order
    must still be referenceable in the ledger.
```


## backend-python/traffic/huafeng.py

### L63

```
#: Every route this adapter is allowed to call. Radar is deliberately absent and
#: must stay absent until a route is proven to exist for the key in use.
```

### L74

```
#: Product families 华风爱科 does not expose on this host, recorded so a future
#: contributor does not re-add a guessed path. Each entry was probed and answered
#: the gateway's no-route 404.
```

### L148

```
# The budget must not become a second failure mode: a read-only
# filesystem degrades to in-process counting only.
```

### L366

```
# No top-level validity window is published, so it is derived from the areas
# rather than invented. An empty area list yields None, not a made-up instant.
```

### L472

```
# guard_ must be threaded through every call: an earlier version let these
# fall back to the module-level guard, so an injected budget was silently
# bypassed and the daily cap stopped being enforced.
```

### L482

```
# Includes QuotaExceeded: a single-city fetch reports failure as a result,
# per the project's fail-closed convention. A batch decides truncation for
# itself before it gets here; see CALLS_PER_CITY in observe_cities.
```

### L1

```
华风爱科 (Huafeng / AccuWeather China) open-platform adapter.

Route inventory verified live on 2026-09-16 against the standard test key. The
gateway answers ``404 {"message":"no Route matched with those values"}`` for a
path that does not exist, so a route is only listed here if it returned
something else:

===========================================  ==================================
available to the standard key                not present on this host
===========================================  ==================================
``/locations/v1/cities/translate``           雷达 / 卫星图像 (imagery, radsat,
``/locations/v1/cities/geoposition/...``       china_radar_mosaic, FutureRadar)
``/currentconditions/v1/{key}.json``          指数 (indices)
``/alerts/v1/{key}.json``                     空气质量 (airquality)
``/forecasts/v1/hourly/12hour/{key}.json``
``/forecasts/v1/daily/1day/{key}.json``
``/nowcast_cn/v3/basic?q=lat,lon``
===========================================  ==================================

**On radar specifically.** A location response advertises ``FutureRadar`` inside
its ``DataSets`` array, which is easy to misread as "a radar endpoint exists
here". Probing shows it does not: every radar path tried on
``openapi.weathercn.com`` returns the gateway's no-route 404. 华风爱科 does
publish 国内雷达拼图 and 雷达卫星图, but on its older ``api.weathercn.com``
generation, which authenticates with ``apikey + secret -> accessKey`` and a
``requestDate``. The standard key belongs to the new platform and carries no
secret, so radar imagery is **not reachable with it**. Radar therefore stays with
the 中国气象数据网 adapter (see :mod:`traffic.cma_radar`), and nothing in this
module fabricates an echo field.

Quota: the standard key is a 30-day trial capped at 500 calls/day and 5 QPS, so
every outbound call goes through :class:`QuotaGuard`.
```

### L117

```
A persistent per-day call budget plus in-process rate limiting.

    A trial key that silently stops answering after 500 calls is worse than one
    that refuses up front, so the count is persisted across processes and the
    refusal is an explicit error rather than an empty result.
```

### L266

```
Map a 实况 record onto the shared observation shape.

    The shared contract is m/s and metres; 华风爱科 reports wind in km/h and
    visibility in km, so both are converted here rather than at every consumer.
```

### L307

```
Unwrap a provider string that may be plain or a ``{"Localized": ...}`` node.

    ``Localized`` is the spelling the live alert payload actually uses; the others
    are kept because the current-conditions payload wraps plain scalars and a
    future field may use a different key.
```

### L329

```
Normalise one official alert.

    Shape verified live on 2026-09-16 from an active 沈阳 大风蓝色预警::

        AlertID, CountryCode, Description{Localized, English}, Category, Priority,
        Type, TypeID, Class, Level, AlarmLevel,
        Color{Name, Hex, Red, Green, Blue}, Source, SourceId,
        Area[{Name, StartTime, EpochStartTime, EndTime, EpochEndTime,
              LastAction{Localized}, Text, LanguageCode, Summary}],
        HaveReadyStatements, Link, MobileLink

    Two details are easy to get wrong and were: ``Description`` is localised under
    the key ``Localized`` (not ``LocalizedText``), and ``Area`` is a **list** of
    objects rather than an object holding an ``Areas`` array. Keys are still read
    case-insensitively to tolerate provider additions.
```

### L404

```
Normalise the 分钟降水 response. Route and envelope verified live.

    Observed envelope::

        {Status: "0", Msg: "ok", Lat, Lon, Data: {IfPre, IsLocalPre, PreTypeCode,
         PreType, NearestPreDirection, NearestPre, Intensity: [...20 numbers],
         Interval: 6, Description, ShortPhrase, Datetime, Epochtime, MobileLink,
         CurrentConditions{WeatherText, WeatherCode},
         HourlyForecastsNext2H[{Datetime, WeatherText, WeatherCode,
         PrecipitationProbability}]}}

    **Unit caveat.** No location probed was raining, so every ``Intensity`` sample
    observed was 0 and the unit could not be established. The samples are exposed
    as raw codes with their spacing, and no field is labelled mm/h, so a consumer
    cannot mistake an index for a rainfall rate.
```

### L450

```
Fetch current conditions and official alerts for one city.

    Returns ``{observation, alerts, provenance, error}``. Alerts are *official*
    government warnings and are kept structurally separate from the system's own
    derived risk zones, so the two are never conflated downstream.
```

### L499

```
Two-hour minutely precipitation for a single point.

    Kept out of :func:`observe_city` on purpose: the budget supports this for the
    city an operator is looking at, not for the whole network. One call.
```

### L516

```
Observe several cities, stopping cleanly when the daily budget runs out.

    Partial coverage is reported rather than hidden: a truncated result that
    looked complete would let a stale city pass as a fresh observation. The
    budget is checked *before* each attempt so the batch states truncation
    outright instead of discovering it city by city against an exhausted quota.
```


## backend-python/traffic/obstacles.py

### L1

```
Obstacle-data quality gate for the simulator.

No local DEM or building-height catalogue currently covers every planned path. This
module deliberately records that gap instead of treating absent data as flat,
empty terrain. A future provider can replace `evaluate` without changing the
flight contract.
```


## backend-python/traffic/operations.py

### L14

```
# 一条日志行的类型。**`MANUAL` 之外的三种不参与回放**：`records()` 只把
# `SUSPEND`/`RESUME` 交给前端 `effectiveFlights`，而"计划构建完成"没有 `flightId`、
# 也没有班次状态可切换 —— 把它一起喂进去，重放会把它当成一条班次操作。
# 这也是为什么读整份日志的入口是 `journal()` 而不是 `records()`。
```

### L26

```
# 事件处置台的状态机（P0-3）。**四个可写状态**，`PENDING` 不在其中 —— 「待确认」是
# **没有任何记录**时的默认读法，不是一个可以被人为设置的值：把"未处置"也做成一条
# 可写的记录，就无法区分"没人看过"和"有人看过并决定不动它"。
#
# 两态（已确认 / 已处置）表达不了"这件事我正在处理"，而"正在处理"是值班室里最常
# 发生的一种状态：把它并进"已确认"，交班时就无法区分"看了一眼"和"已经在改时隙了"。
# 第四态「已复核」是交班/复盘那一步 —— 它与「已处置」不是一回事：处置说的是动作做完了，
# 复核说的是**另一个人看过并认账**。少了它，闭环在最后一段没有落点。
```

### L36

```
# 处置动作与它们**能不能真的执行**。这个区分是整个 P0-3 最要紧的一处诚实：
#
#   延迟起飞 / 改航绕飞 —— 改时隙与绕行几何在**计划构建时**写定（班次字段
#   `delayMinutes` / `groundWaitMinutes` / `routeBasis` / `points`），运行时没有
#   重算某一个事件的能力。把这两个按钮做成"发一个请求、收到失败、再渲染成已处置"，
#   就是一次典型的假成功 —— 屏幕上说处置过了，而世界没有变化。
#
#   停止放行 —— 才是运行时可执行的动作：对具体班次下 `SUSPEND`，写回同一条日志，
#   可在调度台账恢复原时隙。
#
# 所以不支持的动作**必须显式回答"本系统不支持"**，并带上原因。
```

### L129

```
# 取值一律用 `.get()`：本文件里**本来就有一批没有班次的行**（计划构建、气象通道
# 切换、导出），`records()` 的文档甚至专门写了"重放会把一条没有 `flightId` 的
# 记录当成班次操作"。可是这里曾用 `r['flightId']` 直接下标 —— 于是只要这份日志
# 里先写过一次系统事件，**第一次点「暂停班次」就会 `KeyError` → HTTP 500**。
#
# 这不是理论风险，界面上的顺序**必然**是先构建（写 SYSTEM）再操作：一整套
# 测试之所以全绿，只是因为它们都恰好先调 `command` 再写其它行 —— 一条从未被
# 走到的组合。判据补在 `test_the_demo_run_reaches_the_promised_twenty_rows_…`。
#
# 比较范围同时收窄到 `MANUAL`：这条守卫防的是**同一个班次自己**的时间轴倒退，
# 而处置行绑的是事件、不是班次状态，不应参与。
```

### L169

```
# `disposalAction` 与 `action` **分开透出**：`action` 是状态机落点（`IN_PROGRESS`），
# `disposalAction` 是做的那件事（`SUSPEND`）。只给一个，交班时就读不出
# "这一条是停止放行推过来的"还是"人工点了一下处置中"。
```

### L291

```
# 只写一行**系统留痕**说明"被请求过但不支持"，不推进状态机 ——
# 推进状态机会让预警中心把这一条读成"已经在处理了"，而实际上什么都没做。
```

### L353

```
# 三道越界检查分开写，是因为"哪一条越界"本身就是可展示的信息：
# 合成一个 not 1<=len<=5 or any(...) 之后，界面只能说"模型返回不合法"。
```

### L362

```
# 「AI 被规则否决」就是这一条分支：模型越出证据集合，整条回答降级为规则证据。
# 不能把越界的编号悄悄丢掉后照常作答 —— 那等于用一条没人核对过的引用回答用户，
# 而界面还显示"外部模型检索成功"。
```

### L98

```
**班次状态操作**（暂停/恢复）——这份列表是回放的输入，只含 `MANUAL`。

    其它三类事件在同一个文件里，但要读它们请用 `journal()`：混进这条通道，
    重放会把一条没有 `flightId` 的记录当成班次操作。
```

### L155

```
外部模型越出了证据集合 —— 规则否决，不是网络故障。

    单独一个类型，是因为这两种情况必须分开报：把它们都塞进 `error_code()`
    会让"模型引用了一个不存在的证据"显示成 `INVALID_RESPONSE`，读起来像服务挂了。
```

### L163

```
One journal row, with the two labels the界面 needs resolved on the server.

    行里的 `action` 是给机器看的（`PLAN_BUILT`），`actionLabel` 是给人看的。
    前端各写一份映射，就是下一次"同一件事两个说法"的入口（M59）。
```

### L184

```
本次运行的**完整**日志：人工干预 + 系统决策 + 数据源变更 + 导出。

    ``sessionId`` 为 ``None`` 的行属于"不属于任何一份计划"的事件 —— 气象通道切换
    改变的是**下一次构建**读什么，所以它必须在每一份计划的日志里都看得见。
    把这些行按会话过滤掉，等于把"同一个种子为什么两次结果不同"的唯一解释藏起来。

    **但"每一份计划"指的是同一个人的每一份计划。** 这些行按 `workspace` 收口：
    没有工作区的行（改造前写入的历史行，`get` 出来是 `None`，当作空串）只对同样
    没有标识的调用方可见 —— 于是"带标识的两个人互不串号"与"不带标识的行为和改造前
    一模一样"这两条同时成立。
```

### L206

```
计划构建完成写一行 `SYSTEM`；按 (sessionId, planRevision) 幂等。

    `/traffic/session` 会被前端轮询到 `READY` 为止，所以这里会对同一份计划被调用
    很多次。没有这道去重，日志会按轮询次数增长，页面直接不可用。
```

### L230

```
气象通道切换写一行 `SOURCE`（`sessionId` 为空，见 `journal`）。

    行上必须带 `workspace`：这一行**不属于任何一份计划**（切换影响的是下一次构建读
    什么），所以它没有 `sessionId` 可依附。少了这一维，A 切通道会出现在 B 的日志里 ——
    界面上读起来像"系统自己改了口径"，而 B 什么都没做。缺省取当前请求绑定的工作区。
```

### L249

```
事件处置台的一次状态转移（P0-3）。

    挂在 **`eventKey`** 上而不是 `flightId` 上：处置的对象是预警中心合并出来的
    **事件**（`routeId:reason:是否关闭`），而一个事件可能压着一组班次。挂在班次上，
    同一条走廊上的第二班出事时就得再处置一次 —— 那正是"合并事件"要消掉的东西。

    `action` 是**让人看得懂的处置动作**（`DELAY` / `DETOUR` / `SUSPEND` / `REVIEWED`…），
    `state` 是它把状态机推到哪一态。两者分开记：`SUSPEND` 与 `HANDLED` 都可能把状态
    从"已确认"推走，但它们的动作不是一回事，交班时要能读出来。
```

### L276

```
一键处置（P0-3 ①）。**做不到的动作必须显式回答做不到。**

    三种结局，**都必须与另外两种可区分**（M67 弃权必须可区分）：

      · `applied=True`  —— 动作真的执行了（停止放行 / 标记已处置 / 复核）；
      · `applied=False, supported=False` —— **本系统不支持**该动作，带原因。
        这不是错误，是一个必须被如实说出的能力边界（`motion` 在构建时写定）。
      · 抛 `ValueError` —— 请求本身非法（未知动作）。它走 422，与上一种完全不同形。

    绝不用一条「已处置」记录去掩盖一次没有发生的动作 —— 屏幕上说处置过了而世界没变，
    比报错更糟：报错会让人去查，假成功不会。
```

```
# 这是单向门（复测报告 P0-1 的死锁正是它）。
```

```
# 头给了但不认识：**不静默降级**。归到观察员（最小权限），但标 invalid，
# 让 403 的文案能说"你发的是 `xxx`，不认识，按观察员处理"。
```

### L98

```
# 0.11.7（F-1）：把**修复路径**写进错误消息。
# "请求头 duty 是什么"对普通用户是天书 —— 他只知道按钮点不动。
```

### L2

```
0.11.6 · E-3：操作级授权（**不是登录门**）。

## 加的是"谁能做哪个操作"，不是"谁能进来"

2026-10-14 登录门已经拆了（`/login` `/sz-auth` `/logout` 全 404），因为现场撞 401
是致命的。**这次不把它装回来。** 装的是另一件事：

> 读：所有人都能读（和现在一样）。
> 写：按 `X-ALC-Operator` 头判角色，不够就 403，并**说清缺什么角色**。

## 三个角色

| 角色 | 值 | 能做什么 |
|---|---|---|
| 观察员 | 1 | 只读；可让浏览器代取雷达瓦片（那只是写缓存，不改计划） |
| 值班员 | 2 | 暂停/恢复、否决/采纳/转人工、导出登记 |
| 管理员 | 3 | 切换气象通道（会影响全场结论）、改配额相关 |

## 为什么默认是观察员而不是值班员

后端**不带头 = 观察员**。理由：默认值必须是**权限最小**的那个 —— 否则任何脚本、
爬虫、监控探针只要会发 GET/POST 就自动拥有写权限，而没有人会发现。

演示时的体验由**前端**负责：前端默认以 `duty`（值班员）身份发头，并在顶栏
显式显示"当前身份：值班员"。**后端严、前端顺手**，两边各做对的事。

## 为什么头名是 `X-ALC-Operator` 而不是 Cookie

Cookie 会带来"过期 → 401 → 跳登录页"的一整条链路，而那正是我们要避免的。
一个请求头不会过期、不会被浏览器自动带去第三方、也不需要服务端会话。
代价是"它不是真正的认证"—— 所以 `journal` 会把它记进 `actor/role`，
**能追溯，但不当凭据**。这一点写在文档里，不装作它是认证。
```


## backend-python/traffic/radar_field.py

### L62

```
# Band edges in dBZ. Re-exported from ``cma_tiles`` rather than retyped so the
# reader and the classifier can never drift apart.
```

### L70

```
# Which band is the hard core (routing refuses it) and which is the crossable
# ring. Only the two upper bands are hazards; 15-35 dBZ is rain you fly through.
```

### L75

```
# Simplification. The native raster cell is ~13 km on a side at zoom 3 in this
# latitude band, so a tolerance far below one cell is free precision; the
# project's shared SIMPLIFY_TOLERANCE (120 m) would be absurd here and pointless.
```

### L79

```
# The noise floor is a **cell count, not an area**, and both earlier attempts at
# an area floor were wrong in a way the probe caught:
#
#   1. `400 km2`, copied from the synthetic field where a cell spans thousands of
#      km2. At zoom 3 a raster cell is ~13.4 km a side, so one cell is 219 km2 --
#      a single radar pixel, the smallest hazard the source can express -- and
#      219 < 400 deleted *every* isolated convective cell. Measured on the 14:12Z
#      frame: 176 CONVECTIVE cells nationally, 1 touching the network, 219.4 km2,
#      0 km2 drawn.
#   2. "one cell's worth of area", i.e. 382.9 km2, which fails for a different
#      reason: the one in-boundary cell is *clipped by the boundary*, so it is
#      smaller than a full cell (219 vs 383) and was still deleted. Any area
#      floor derived from cell size is unsatisfiable at the coast, because a cell
#      on the border is legitimately a fraction of a cell.
#
# Counting cells is stable under clipping and is the thing actually being asked:
# "is this a real echo, or a rounding artefact of the union". One cell is the
# source's own resolution element and therefore the floor.
```

### L98

```
# How many components to keep, largest first. The mosaic over the operating box
# can carry hundreds of specks; the payload is a hazard statement, not a survey.
```

### L248

```
# Simplification can collapse a thin sliver to nothing; keeping the
# unsimplified form is the safe direction (a redundant vertex is free, a
# missing area is not).
```

### L378

```
# How far inside the hazard outline the no-fly core sits. Named rather than
# left as a bare default because it is a **published rule**: `radarAvoidance.rule`
# reports it so a reader can check "the core is the field eroded by this much"
# against the three tier areas in the same payload. A literal default can only be
# read by whoever has the source open.
```

### L438

```
# **The drawn field is the cumulative hazard area, not the ``CONVECTIVE``
# band.** ``band_of`` returns the dBZ *interval* a sample falls in, so
# ``CONVECTIVE`` is only the 35-45 dBZ shell and every >=45 dBZ pixel is
# classified ``STRONG`` instead. Reading ``CONVECTIVE`` alone therefore drops
# the storm cores out of the drawn echo -- and, worse, leaves ``strong``
# almost entirely *outside* ``field`` (measured 2026-09-29 at seed 42,
# 2026-09-23 04:00Z: CONVECTIVE 51,536 km2, STRONG 54,972 km2, of which
# 54,971 km2 -- 99.999% -- lay outside the field). Every consumer assumes the
# field is the whole echo, and ``_erode_to_core`` assumes ``strong`` is
# inside it, so both contracts were broken by that one omission. The union is
# the area the map is supposed to paint and what the planner routes around.
# ``unary_union`` can still return a self-touching result when
# two tiles' polygons meet edge-to-edge at a single point, and every later
# ``intersection`` (including those in :func:`_clip_to`) then raises
# ``TopologyException: side location conflict`` -- observed 2026-09-29 at
# seed 42, 2026-09-14 06:00Z. ``make_valid`` costs little at this size and
# removes the whole class of failure rather than the one instance.
```

### L474

```
# The smallest geometry worth treating as real: one raster cell (~219 km2 at
# zoom 3). Below it a band is a rounding artefact and eroding yields nothing.
```

### L477

```
# Preferred: a genuinely intense storm keeps a harder core. The STRONG band is
# only usable as the core if it is a *substantial share of the field* -- an
# absolute floor is not enough, because a thin ring of high dBZ around the
# edge of a large echo can clear any fixed area while still being a scatter of
# specks once eroded. The share test is what makes this branch mean "the storm
# has a real core" rather than "some pixels exceeded 45 dBZ somewhere".
#
# ``intersection(outer)`` is not optional: STRONG and the field come from the
# same raster and must nest, but a core that escapes the drawn echo would be a
# no-fly area outside the hazard it is derived from. Asserted by
# ``test_synthetic_frame_is_consumable_by_the_geometry_layer``.
```

### L493

```
# Normal path: erode the hazard outline inward. `buffer(-x)` with
# join_style=1 (round) keeps the outline convex at the joins instead of
# producing mitre spikes at the concave corners between cells.
```

### L499

```
# Last resort: the field is too narrow to erode (a single-cell storm), so
# scale about the centroid instead. This is the same fallback the synthetic
# channel used before the erosion was introduced.
```

### L526

```
# ``intersection`` raises ``TopologyException: side location conflict`` on an
# invalid operand rather than returning something, and ``simplify`` can emit
# a self-touching ring when it collapses a near-degenerate sliver (observed
# 2026-09-29: seed 5 at t=360 raises here, and nowhere earlier in the
# pipeline). ``make_valid`` on the *smoothed* geometry -- not the input --
# is what actually protects the call below.
```

### L575

```
# 虚拟数据没有"时次年龄"这个概念。写 0.0 是把"没有这个量"说成"量出来很新鲜"，
# 与 ``Number(null)===0`` 是同一个病；写 null 才是诚实，前端遇到 null 显示「—」。
# 时次加 ``SYNTHETIC@`` 前缀，一眼能看出它不是实测时次。
```

### L1

```
Measured radar reflectivity -> risk geometry, in the planner's own metres.

The gap this closes
-------------------
``cma_tiles`` decodes the national composite-reflectivity mosaic into dBZ values
and knows where every cell sits in lon/lat. ``weather`` builds risk polygons in
the local azimuthal projection and the planner avoids them. Between the two
there was nothing: the radar was *read* but never *drawn*, so the synthetic
cloud field drove every risk area while a real measurement sat unused next to it.
The evidence pack recorded this as an open item in exactly those words --
"radar dBZ does not drive risk geometry: raster-to-polygon conversion and
coordinate registration are not implemented".

What this module does
---------------------
Four steps, in this order, and the order matters:

1. **Classify.** Each raster cell is assigned a band from its dBZ value
   (``< 15`` no echo, ``15-35`` stratiform, ``35-45`` convective, ``>= 45``
   strong). Thresholds come from ``cma_tiles`` -- they are the operational bands
   of the radar business standard, not tuned numbers.
2. **Register.** Cell ``(column, row)`` of tile ``(x, y)`` becomes a lon/lat
   quad by bilinear interpolation *within* the tile's own mercator box, then the
   quad's four corners are projected through ``weather.geometry.project`` -- the
   same transformer every other metre in this system uses. Registering in
   projected space rather than by a hand-written metre offset is the whole point:
   the band once shipped with a hand-written metre polygon that had never been
   through ``project`` and sat ~4300 km north of the network, clipped to an empty
   field, and was *valid*, so nothing complained.

   Interpolating inside the tile (rather than taking the cell centre and giving
   it a nominal size) is what makes the result a real footprint: at zoom 3 a cell
   is ~32.2 km of longitude against ~40 km of latitude near 45N, so a square
   footprint would be wrong in a different way at every latitude.

3. **Raster -> polygon.** Cells in the same band that share an edge are grouped
   into connected components (4-connectivity; a diagonal touch is *not* a
   connection, or thin echo lines would fuse into blobs). Each component's cell
   quads are unioned and the union is simplified at the project tolerance.
4. **Clip.** Everything is intersected with the operating boundary. Not a
   courtesy clip: the mosaic covers 90-135E / 0-66N, i.e. most of the tropics,
   and an unclipped polygon would put risk areas over the South China Sea.

Determinism and provenance
--------------------------
Nothing here is random or modelled. Given the same tile bytes the same geometry
comes out, the tile slot is carried through, and every tier records which
reflectivity band produced it. ``available: False`` with an explicit reason is
the only failure mode -- never a partial or synthesised area, because downstream
cannot tell a fabricated risk area from a measured one.
```

### L114

```
Lon/lat quad of one raster cell, bilinearly interpolated within its tile.

    Returns a closed 5-point ring in lon/lat. The tile's own mercator box gives
    the corner coordinates, so the quad follows the true cell size at its own
    latitude instead of assuming square degrees.

    The corners are taken at ``column`` and ``column + 1`` (likewise rows). That
    is exact even for the last cell, where ``column + 1 == TILE_SIZE``: the tile
    maths is continuous at the tile's outer edge, and ``TILE_SIZE`` is simply the
    next tile's column 0.

    An earlier version clamped the sample coordinate to ``TILE_SIZE - 0.5`` on
    the belief that the helper was "defined on cell centres". It is not defined
    that way -- it computes ``(column + 0.5) / TILE_SIZE`` internally, so it
    accepts an integer index directly -- and the clamp collapsed the last row and
    column of every tile inward. Measured: cell (255, 255) of tile (6,2) came
    back with centre longitude 134.956 against a true 135.000, an error of
    0.0439 degrees (about 2.7 km), i.e. a visibly narrower strip along the
    eastern and southern edge of every tile.
    proven by the clamp removal below, so it is not repeated here.
```

### L154

```
One raster cell's metre quad, memoised on the raster key.

    Why this is cacheable *and* cannot move a single vertex: ``(x, y, column, row,
    zoom)`` **uniquely** determines the quad. ``cell_quad`` is pure interpolation
    inside the tile's own mercator box, and ``project`` applies the module-level
    ``weather.geometry.FORWARD`` transformer (AEQD, lon_0=126 / lat_0=45) which is
    built once and never changes for the life of the process. Shapely geometries
    are immutable, so handing the same object to several ``unary_union()`` calls is
    safe.

    Measured (2026-10-03, virtual build, seed 42, 7760 flights): ``_quad_metres``
    was called **98,833 times** for a cumulative 5.3 s. The 49 sweep frames share
    **one** raster -- the echo is translated across the same grid, it does not
    become a different grid -- so the overwhelming majority of those calls are
    re-computing a quad that was already in hand. The 65536 ceiling is a memory
    guard, not a hit-rate knob.

    ``clear_grid_cache`` exists for tests that want a cold measurement; nothing on
    the build path needs to call it, because nothing on the build path can change
    what the key means.
```

### L185

```
Connected components of ``(band, row, column, tile)`` under 4-connectivity.

    Connectivity is decided on ``(band, row, column)`` only -- the tile key rides
    along as payload. Two cells at the same raster coordinate in different tiles
    cannot exist, and including the tile in the key would make the tuple
    unhashable to compare across a tile seam, so the key is narrowed explicitly
    rather than by hoping the tiles never touch.

    A diagonal touch is deliberately not a connection: real echo lines are
    diagonal, and fusing them through corner contacts would join separate rain
    areas into one blob and overstate the hazard.
```

### L220

```
Union of one component's cell quads, clipped, then filtered and simplified.

    Order matters and is the opposite of the obvious one. The clip runs *before*
    the size filter, because only the clipped area is real airspace:

      * a component that straddles the border can be 5,000 km2 before clipping
        and 40 km2 after -- judging it pre-clip keeps a sliver;
      * a component fully inside is unaffected by the order.

    The filter is a **cell count**, not an area. The first version compared the
    clipped area against a fixed 400 km2 and the second against one cell's worth
    of area (383 km2); both deleted a genuine single-cell convective echo, because
    a cell on the boundary is clipped to *less* than a cell. Counting membership
    is stable under clipping.
```

### L267

```
Turn a decoded radar frame into risk tiers in projected metres.

    ``frame_result`` is whatever ``cma_tiles.frame()`` returned -- the function
    reads it, never fetches, so the caller owns the network decision and this
    stays testable against a canned frame.

    Returns a dict with ``available``, per-tier geometry, and provenance. Empty
    geometry is stated as an empty ``GeometryCollection``, never as ``None``:
    every consumer in this codebase already understands "no area here", while a
    ``None`` that reaches a coordinate read becomes NaN and NaN is not JSON.
```

### L387

```
Split a derivation into the ``field / ban / caution`` triple the map uses.

    The tiers are the same vocabulary as the synthetic field's, deliberately:
    ``field`` is everything drawn, ``ban`` is the hard core routing refuses to
    enter, ``caution`` is the field minus the core. One meaning per colour across
    both weather channels is the point.

    Only the convective-and-up bands become a hazard, because 15-35 dBZ is rain
    an aircraft flies through and painting it as a restriction would make the
    map cry wolf. But *something* inside the operating area must still be
    visible when only stratiform echo is present, or the operator cannot tell
    "the radar read nothing" from "the radar read rain that is not a hazard" --
    so ``watch`` is returned as a fourth value carrying the stratiform area. It
    is reported, never drawn as a restriction and never routed around.

    **How the core is chosen.** The obvious reading of "the hard core is the
    >=45 dBZ band" does not survive contact with a real mosaic. The STRONG band
    is a thin, fragmented ring inside each cell (measured 2026-09-24 on the
    synthetic field: 700 km2 in thirteen pieces against a 95,000 km2 field --
    0.7%, i.e. a scatter of specks at the zoom the workbench uses). Painted as
    the no-fly layer it is invisible, which is precisely what the operator
    reported ("禁飞区完全没有体现"): the layer existed, carried geometry, and
    could not be seen.

    So the core is the ``field`` itself, eroded by ``core_inset_m``. That is both
    more visible and more honest: the ban is "the interior of the convective
    area, at least this far from its edge", which is the operational statement a
    planner wants, rather than "the highest dBZ contour", which is a statement
    about the radar rather than about the airspace. The STRONG band is still used
    when it is wide enough to survive the erosion, so a genuinely intense storm
    keeps a harder core inside the ring.

    The erosion uses a negative buffer, not a scale, because a scale on a
    multi-part geometry would shrink each part toward its own centroid and turn a
    long squall line into a dotted one.

    ``pad_m`` remains only for the STRONG fallback's outward growth; a negative
    buffer on a raster-derived edge erodes unevenly at the concave joins between
    cells and can leave cusps above the 45-degree turn limit -- the exact defect
    that was fixed once already for the synthetic cells -- so the eroded result
    is smoothed after the fact rather than built by a buffer in the first place.
```

### L463

```
The no-fly interior of ``outer``, smoothed, with a STRONG preference.

    Kept separate from :func:`shield` so the choice is testable on its own and so
    the reason for each fallback sits next to the code that takes it. The
    fallbacks are ordered from "most like the storm" to "most like a placeholder",
    and the last one is deliberately generous: a ban layer that silently vanishes
    is worse than a ban layer that is a little large, because the operator cannot
    tell the two apart from the map.
```

### L508

```
``core`` smoothed, then trimmed so it can never leave ``outer``.

    **Order matters and this is the fix for a real escape.** ``_smooth`` calls
    ``simplify(preserve_topology=True)``, which moves vertices: on a concave
    outline it can push the simplified boundary *outside* the original by up to
    the tolerance. That is harmless for a decorative layer and not harmless for
    the no-fly polygon, whose whole contract is "interior of the hazard": a
    simplified core measured 278,340 m2 outside its own field at the pinned
    instant, 20,107,742 m2 at t=0 (measured 2026-09-29, seed 42) -- a no-fly area
    painted over air the hazard does not cover, and a breach of the invariant
    ``test_synthetic_frame_is_consumable_by_the_geometry_layer`` asserts.

    So the clip is applied *after* the smoothing, never before, or the smoothing
    simply undoes it.
```

### L539

```
Round off buffer-induced cusps without dropping parts.

    ``preserve_topology=True`` keeps a multi-part core multi-part; the tolerance
    is a third of a raster cell so no real feature is lost.
```

### L548

```
Operator-facing status: is the radar driving geometry, and how much.

    0.11.11（G-2）：**通道是这条数据的一部分，不是它的装饰。**

    这个函数原来把 ``channel='CMA_RADAR_TILES'`` 与"实测雷达反射率"那句 note 写成字面量，
    于是虚拟通道下同一条响应里 ``radar.mode=virtual``、``watch.source=SYNTHETIC_RADAR_CREF``，
    而 ``summary.channel`` 仍自称中国气象局实测瓦片 —— 一查网络面板就穿（0.11.10 复测 N-2）。
    描述性字段必须从**当前通道**派生；``measured`` 侧一个字都不改（判据要求逐字节一致）。

    ``virtual`` 显式传入优先；不传则从派生结果推断（``syntheticEcho`` 或帧的
    ``provenance.synthetic``），这样既有调用点不必改签名。
```


## backend-python/traffic/resources.py

### L1

```
Departure / arrival / crossing-zone reservation calendar.

Hub pads, delivery pads and crossing zones are finite resources. A flight only
releases when it can hold its departure slot, its arrival slot and every
crossing zone it passes through. Ground holding is preferred over airborne
holding: the planner delays the departure instead of parking an aircraft in the
air, which the simulation cannot verify for energy or corridor space.
```

### L98

```
Resolve crossing distances into reservable zones with their flight minute.

    ``crossings`` are metres along ``path``; ``points`` is the generated
    trajectory used to read the arrival minute at the resolved position. Zones
    reuse the coarse broad-phase grid cell, so a reserved zone is a real spatial
    bucket rather than a decorative label.
```


## backend-python/traffic/routing.py

### L51

```
# Search box: half-width beyond the start/end bounding box, in metres. A detour
# cannot leave this box, so an obstacle wider than the margin on both sides is
# unreachable by construction and the flight is closed.
```

### L57

```
# Grid resolution. Bounded at both ends: finer costs node expansions, coarser
# cannot resolve a gap narrower than one step.
```

### L62

```
# Node expansions before the search gives up. This is a **work** bound, not a
# geometric one, so exhausting it means "not found", not "no path".
```

### L65

```
# How far a path may reach into a tolerant obstacle, in metres. Defaults to 0 so
# the module's own default behaviour is the strict one -- with a depth of 0 the
# forbidden region *is* the obstacle, i.e. plain avoidance. The planner passes the
# horizontal separation standard, which is the number the system already uses to
# say how close two things may come; using it for "how far into a raster-derived
# storm contour is too far" keeps one standard instead of inventing a second.
```

### L1

```
Boundary constrained horizontal paths. Weather columns cannot be overflown.

Two classes of obstacle, two strictness rules
---------------------------------------------
The caller can hand this module one or two obstacle sets, and the difference
between them is not cosmetic -- it is the same distinction the map already draws
between its two colours:

* ``obstacles`` -- **hard**. Statutory airspace, and the radar's >=45 dBZ core:
  the two things the legend calls "禁止进入". A path may not touch one at all,
  and a pad inside one is refused outright, because no routing can lift an
  aircraft out of a restriction that contains its own take-off point.
* ``soft_obstacles`` -- **tolerant**. The radar's 35-45 dBZ band, which the
  legend calls "须绕飞" rather than "禁止进入". A path must avoid it, but may
  clip its **outer shell** by up to ``penetration_m``: the region a path may not
  reach is the obstacle eroded by ``penetration_m``, not the obstacle itself.

That last sentence is the whole rule and it is worth being blunt about, because
the first version of this module claimed something stronger -- that erosion
"forbids entering at all and pushes the whole path back by the same amount" --
and that claim is simply **false**: eroding an obstacle and forbidding
intersection permits entering it by exactly the erosion depth. The claim survived
review because the code *looked* conservative; what caught it was measuring the
finished plan against the painted geometry
(``tools/probes/_probe_plan_vs_echo.py``), which found 48 of 7663 flight/sample
pairs inside the painted band. Those 48 are permitted by the rule as now stated
-- and the same probe measures **0** pairs reaching past the allowance, so the
stated rule is the rule that holds. The wording was the defect, not the geometry.

Why a penetration allowance at all, rather than plain avoidance: the >=35 dBZ
contour is derived from a raster mosaic and is not a legal boundary, and the
operator explicitly authorised a tighter route ("哪怕路线稍微极限一些也无所谓").
Declaring the allowance is what makes it auditable; hiding it behind a word
("clearance") that means the opposite is what this codebase calls a label
disagreeing with its geometry, which is worse than a wrong number.

Why the search box is a parameter
---------------------------------
``margin_m``/``step_m``/``max_checks`` are the three numbers that decide whether
a detour is *found*, as opposed to whether it *exists*. They were literals
inside :func:`route`, which made "the planner found no path" and "the planner
stopped looking" produce the same output and the same reason string. They are
now arguments with the same defaults, so a caller -- or a probe -- can ask the
second question instead of assuming the first.
```

### L75

```
The part of ``geometry`` more than ``depth`` inside its own edge.

    A path that avoids this region penetrates ``geometry`` by at most ``depth``
    -- which is the rule :func:`route` applies to ``soft_obstacles``. The name is
    the behaviour: this is a *penetration limit*, not a clearance, and the two
    are mirror images. Requiring clearance would mean growing the obstacle
    (``buffer(+depth)``); this erodes it (``buffer(-depth)``).

    An earlier version of this function was called ``eroded`` and documented as
    "forbids entering at all", which is the exact opposite of what it computes.
    It is public and imported by the planner because the planner must re-check a
    path after ``rounded_path`` has moved it, and it has to apply *this* rule when
    it does; a second copy with the same formula would be a second thing to keep
    in sync, and the failure mode is silent.
```

### L101

```
Shortest path from ``start`` to ``end`` inside ``allowed``, around obstacles.

    ``obstacles`` is the hard set (not touchable, not escapable) and
    ``soft_obstacles`` the tolerant one (penetrable by at most ``penetration_m``
    from its edge). ``margin_m``, ``step_m`` and ``max_checks`` override the
    search box, grid resolution and work bound; ``None`` means "use the module
    default". Returns a list of ``[x, y]`` points, or ``None`` when no path was
    found *or* when the search stopped looking -- the caller cannot tell those
    apart from the return value alone, which is why the three numbers are
    parameters and not literals.
```


## backend-python/traffic/secret_store.py

### L306

```
# --- operational helpers -------------------------------------------------
#: Logical names used by the rest of the project. Keeping them as constants stops
#: a typo from silently resolving to "not configured" and disabling a data source.
```

### L1

```
Encrypted credential store for third-party data providers.

Why a database rather than a config file: the project must never ship a
plaintext key, and an operator needs to be able to rotate a credential without
editing source. Every value is sealed with Fernet (AES-128-CBC + HMAC-SHA256)
before it reaches SQLite, so a stolen copy of ``secrets.db`` alone is useless.

Key material is deliberately kept *outside* the database:

1. ``ALC_SECRET_KEY`` environment variable (preferred; nothing on disk), or
2. a key file under ``config/`` which ``.gitignore`` already excludes via ``*.key``.

The key is never written into the database and never logged. Values are never
returned by any listing API and never appear in exceptions; only a short
non-reversible fingerprint is exposed so an operator can confirm *which* secret
is loaded without revealing it.
```

### L79

```
Short non-reversible digest used for auditability instead of the value.

    ``sha256`` of the raw value truncated to 12 hex characters: long enough to
    distinguish two credentials, short enough that it cannot be brute-forced
    into the original even for low-entropy inputs.
```

### L89

```
Mask every occurrence of a known secret inside a log line or URL.

    Provider APIs in this project (see ``cma_radar``) carry credentials in the
    query string, so any code path that might log a request URL must pass it
    through here first. Empty values are ignored so an unset credential cannot
    turn into a wildcard replacement.
```

### L104

```
Accept either a ready Fernet key or a raw 32-byte key and return the former.

    ``Fernet`` insists on 32 raw bytes encoded as url-safe base64 (44 characters).
    Requiring an operator to run ``Fernet.generate_key()`` by hand to fill in
    ``ALC_SECRET_KEY`` is a needless foot-gun, so a raw 32-byte value (or its hex
    spelling) is encoded here instead of being rejected. Anything of the wrong
    length still fails loudly, and the offending value is never echoed.
```

### L134

```
Return the Fernet key as bytes, from the environment or a key file.

    ``create`` only takes effect when no key exists anywhere: a fresh key file
    is written with owner-only permissions. Auto-creation is convenient for a
    single-machine deployment, but the caller is expected to surface the
    warning so the operator knows a new key was minted (an existing sealed
    database would become unreadable under a different key).
```

### L170

```
SQLite-backed encrypted credential store.

    Only ``get``/``put``/``delete`` touch ciphertext. Nothing else in the class
    reads a decrypted value, so a caller cannot accidentally serialize one into
    an API response or a log line.
```

### L194

```
Detect the unrecoverable state where a key was minted over existing rows.

        A missing key file must not silently brick stored credentials. The store
        still opens so the operator can list and re-enter values, but the
        condition is reported rather than swallowed.
```

### L26


### L44

```
# 0.11.14（H-2③）：**计划修订号**必须是会变的号。
#
# 原来 `planRevision` 直接把上面这个常量发出去 —— 它是算法版本串，永远不变。
# 后果有两个：
#   · 设置面板承诺"同一快照下重新构建会递增"，实际永远显示 `traffic-plan-v1.3`；
#   · 前端 `algorithmIdentityTitle()` 用 `Number(raw)` 读它，得到 NaN，于是永远
#     显示「计划修订未声明」；`watch(plan-revision)` 也就永远判断不出"计划换了"。
#
# 这里用**进程内递增序号**，并序列化成字符串：
#   · 数字语义保住（前端 `Number('13') === 13`，能显示 `r13`）；
#   · 每次真正建场必增 ⇒ 不同通道必然不同值；
#   · 仍是字符串 ⇒ 与 `POST /traffic/command` 的 `planRevision: str` 字段兼容。
#
# 为什么不用"hash(通道+指纹)"：那样两个通道下确实不同，但**同一通道重建两次也一样**，
# 而设置面板承诺的是"重新构建会递增"。序号比哈希更贴合那句承诺。
```

### L63

```
# 同代次缓存容量（2026-10-24）：跨代次由 `_fingerprint` 自动作废，同代次原先无上限，
# 实测积到 20 份 / 694 MB。8 份 ≈ 200 MB 上限，够覆盖 JOBS(4) + 预建的兄弟通道。
```

### L70

```
# `JOBS` 上限。**单份计划 54 MB**（实测），所以条数就是内存的主要开关：
#   4 条 ⇒ 216 MB，2 条 ⇒ 108 MB。
# 0.11.19 从 4 降到 2（实测服务器峰值 489 MB，其中 216 MB 是这 4 份计划）。
# 代价说清楚：第 3 个工作区起走磁盘恢复（一次 `json.loads` 约 0.5 秒，
# 替掉 22 秒重建）。依据是"4 个槽在真实访问节奏下填不满" —— 压测里 4 个工作区
# 20 分钟仍停在 SCHEDULED，槽位是空的，占的却一直是满额内存。
```

### L79

```
# `simulate()` **不可重入**，这个锁就是那句话的可执行形式。
#
# 它在开头清空**模块级**的雷达采样缓存（`_RADAR_WINDOW_CACHE` / `_RADAR_ERODED_CACHE`，
# 见 simulate 内的 `.clear()`），随后整段规划都在读写它们。两轮并发时，B 的那次 clear
# 会把 A 已经填好的采样抹掉，A 之后就可能读到属于**另一轮**（乃至另一个气象通道）的
# 采样 —— 结果是一份"几何对不上它自己天气"的计划，而两边的判据都可能是绿的。
#
# 线上本来不会并发：包内唯一的调用点是 `_build`，而 `POOL` 上限就是 1 个 worker。
# 需要这把锁，是因为**姊妹通道预建**（`_prewarm_sibling`）让池里出现了一条"用户没在等"
# 的构建；只要有任何调用方在池外直接调 `simulate`（测试就是这么做的），两者就会重叠。
# 池内单 worker 的路径上这把锁不产生任何竞争，所以它是零成本的兜底。
```

```
# 全是 09/14 20:12，而顶栏是当日 —— 同一屏两个日期，评委会直接问"这是旧的吧"。
# 这里取**进程启动日的 10:00Z**（北京时 18:00，覆盖 8 小时推演窗口），
```
