### L2938

```
# 种子不在这里传：扫掠会在**开始之前读一次**控制器的种子（见 `_virtual_radar_sweep`），
# 保证一次扫掠用的是同一套天气，而不是逐帧重读、把两套天气并成一个并集。
```

### L2951

```
# 虚拟通道的两条红线，与实测通道那条 CHANNEL_CHANGED_DURING_BUILD 同构：宁可
# 这份计划明说"我没用雷达"，也不要让它带着**别处来的**回波去避让。
#
# ① 帧上没有生成器的章 ⇒ 取帧走了别的通道（2026-10-15 那次失效的形状：这条路径
#    去问了一个会解析活开关的访问器，拿到实测帧，而实测源对云端出口整站 403）。
#    此时如果照旧把"扫掠为空"报成 RADAR_UNAVAILABLE，真正的原因就被一句听起来很
#    合理的弃权盖住了 —— 而调用方**有**一条读起来很合理的弃权分支。
# ② 生成器说帧不可用 ⇒ 不可能发生（回波是生成的）。出现即说明生成器被换掉了。
```

### L2970

```
# 帧都取到了、也过了闸门，只是本窗口内生成的回波未达 35 dBZ。这是**合法状态**
# （与实测通道同一句话），不是一次取帧失败：晴朗天继续放行，取帧失败要去查源。
# 两个结论的下一步动作相反，所以不能共用一句话。
```

### L2978

```
# The controller reads the live switch, so a flip during the build can hand
# back the other channel's frame. Refusing it is the honest move: dropping
# the radar from this plan is visible in `radarAvoidance.reason`, whereas
# accepting it would build a plan whose key and `weatherMode` name one
# channel and whose avoidance geometry came from the other -- the exact
# contradiction this whole change removes.
```

### L3001

```
# 三档各自的面积。``shield()`` 早就把 ban / caution / watch 三块几何算出来了，
# 这里原先只留了并集面积，把三块丢掉 —— 于是界面上能写「≥45 dBZ 内核不可触碰」
# 这句断言，却给不出任何一个能核对它的数。断言不是证据：同一份求值多带三个
# 数字，不新增任何推导、不新增第二条规则。
#
# 虚拟通道**没有**这三个字段，而且这不是遗漏：它的避让几何是沿时间轴逐帧取并集
# 的结果，没有"某一帧的三档"可言，硬报一个数就等于把扫掠并集说成瞬时观测。
```

### L3014

```
# 「帧取到了、判定过了，只是本帧没有 ≥35 dBZ 回波」与「没有纳入避让几何」是两件
# 相反的事：前者说明气象被读进来并且被判为不影响航路（晴朗天），后者说明机制
# 压根没跑。不区分，一次晴朗就被读成一次机制失效 —— 而这两个结论的下一步动作
# 完全相反（一个继续放行，一个去查数据源）。
```

### L3066

```
# 「读到了、判定过了，本帧没有城市低于偏低档」与「机制压根没跑」是两件相反的
# 事，下一步动作也相反（继续放行 / 去查数据源）。分开报。
```

### L3074

```
# 每个浅灰圆**带着自己的城**，不只是一个几何。理由不是整齐：豁免是逐圆做的，
# 而 `readings` 与圆列表是**两个不同的过滤结果**，按下标对齐会在某座城的圆为空时
# 静默错位一格 —— 于是"豁免了 A 城"会被记成 B 城，而计数仍然是对的（M79 的形态：
# 同一个量的两份副本各自被钉过，也不会报警）。
```

### L304

```
`config/rules-v1.json::operations` 的 SHA256 前 8 位。

    它进计划键（见 `identity()`），所以改阈值等于换键 —— 这一条不做，改阈值就会
    "看起来没生效"：磁盘上的旧计划仍然命中，而界面上一切正常，只有数字不对。
```

### L350

```
How the published aerodrome protection footprint is enforced.

    ``APPROVAL_REQUIRED`` (the default) mirrors 第十九条: controlled airspace is
    entered with approval, not absolutely forbidden, so crossings are recorded
    rather than blocking the network. ``PROHIBITED`` applies the stricter reading;
    it is exposed because the two readings give very different plans, and the
    measured difference belongs in the report rather than in a hidden constant.
```

### L479

```
Projected metres -> lon/lat GeoJSON that is **valid**.

    The inverse projection is not validity-preserving. Measured 2026-09-30, on
    the virtual channel at seed 42, ``shield`` returned valid geometry in metres
    for every tier, and the very same geometries came back self-intersecting once
    transformed to EPSG:4326 -- e.g. ``field`` at t=180 with a crossing at
    (123.486328125, 46.9802523552188), a raster tile corner. 16 of 54 sampled
    tiers were affected, and the affected layer is the one the operator sees:
    ``radar-footprint`` feeds MapLibre a fill, and a self-intersecting ring has
    no defined inside, so its filled area is decided by the triangulation rather
    than by the data.

    ``make_valid`` is applied *after* the transform because that is where the
    crossing appears; making the projected geometry valid first would not help.
    ``mapping`` is used instead of ``weather.geometry.geojson`` so the transform
    runs exactly once.

    The result is narrowed back to a polygonal geometry on purpose.
    ``make_valid`` on a ring with a hairline crossing returns a
    ``GeometryCollection`` holding the two faces *and* the shared edge, and the
    renderer accepts only ``Polygon`` / ``MultiPolygon`` -- publishing the
    collection would drop the whole surface and reproduce the "the layer is there
    but you cannot see it" failure this project has already paid for twice.

    The narrowing goes through :func:`traffic.weather._polygon_parts` rather than
    a local ``part.geom_type in (...)`` filter, and that is not tidiness: a
    ``MultiPolygon`` iterates *polygons* but a ``Polygon`` iterates its **rings**,
    so the obvious filter matches nothing for a plain Polygon and this function
    would have published no geometry at all -- silently, and only for the single
    -ring case, which is the common one. The shared helper already encodes that
    asymmetry.

    Empty means empty: ``None`` is returned rather than an empty collection so the
    caller writes its own explicit empty sentinel.
```

### L529

```
Measured radar reflectivity as risk regions, in the frame payload's shape.

    The radar channel is deliberately a *separate entry point from a separate
    source*, not folded into the weather snapshot. Two reasons, both about honesty
    rather than tidiness:

      * a point observation and a radar mosaic are different measurements of
        different things, taken at different cadences; a station reading cannot
        be interpolated into an echo, and an echo is not a station reading;
      * provenance has to survive to the operator. A risk area that came from a
        public dBZ mosaic must be labelled differently from one this system
        derived from a forecast model, or the two become indistinguishable in
        the one place it matters.

    Which source supplies the frame is decided by :mod:`traffic.weather_control`,
    not here: in measured mode this is the real CMA mosaic, in virtual mode it is
    the synthetic generator, and the record's ``source`` names which one it was.
    The geometry code below is identical either way -- the synthetic frame is
    contract-compatible, so it needs no branch.

    Returns ``{available, regions, summary, error}``. On any failure the region
    list is empty and ``error`` says why -- never a partial or synthesised area.
```

### L643

```
低能见度（雾）两档几何，作为**独立图层**发布。

    坐标空间与 ``cells``（暖色派生风险区）**相同 = 投影米制**，理由充分：它同样来自
    站点点观测，走同一条前端换算路径（``localGeometry``）。它与 ``radar_regions``
    **不同** —— 雷达是另一套来源、经纬度、不走投影换算。两套坐标的边界由
    ``radarFeatureGeometry`` 承载，而哪一套坐标由**消费点自己断言**，不由注释声明（M72）。

    每条记录 = 一座城 + 两条几何：``ban`` 是深灰内核、``caution`` 是它外面的浅灰环。

    **不做连通分量合并**，与暖色风险区相反：合并之后就没法回答"是哪座城"，
    而"是哪座城"正是这条图层存在的理由（用户要的是把不知道原因的关闭说得清）。
```

### L702

```
One derived virtual frame, cached per ``(seed, slot)``.

    A frame+derive cycle costs ~0.8 s (measured 2026-09-24, seed 42); the
    frontend refetches weather every 2 minutes of playback and slots quantise
    to 6 minutes, so consecutive fetches mostly ask for the same slot. Without
    this cache a 60x playback would burn a fresh derive on nearly every tick.
    Tests that monkeypatch the frame source must call ``.cache_clear()``.

    **这两维就是全部输入，而且必须真的只用这两维。** 这里原先调的是
    ``weather_control.radar_frame(slot=slot)``，而那个访问器自己会解析**当前工作区的
    活开关** —— 于是 ``seed`` 参数根本没被用上，函数名里的 ``(seed, slot)`` 是个假承诺。
    这不是洁癖问题，是一次真实的线上失效（2026-10-15）：``_prewarm_sibling`` 会在**还处于
    实测通道**的工作区里预建虚拟通道的计划，这条路径于是拿实测帧当虚拟回波；而实测源对
    云端出口整站 403，49 个时次全部退化，操作台上看到的是 12 架载具停在禁入区内核里
    （计划 ``radarAvoidance.sweepSlots: 0``）。生成器是纯函数，直接调它是唯一能让
    "读哪一帧"由实参决定、而不是由"此刻开关朝哪边"决定的方式。

    ``syntheticEcho`` 不是装饰：``radar_field.derive`` 重建 provenance 时**不**透传
    ``synthetic``（它连 ``provider`` 都写死成 ``CMA_DATA_NET``），所以下游没有办法从
    derived 里分辨生成帧与实测帧。自检必须在这里盖章 —— 盖章的依据是**帧自己的**
    provenance，而不是"我请求了虚拟"这个意图。
```

### L732

```
Radar geometry sampled along the plan timeline, as ``(minute, field, core)``.

    Sampled at RADAR_SWEEP_STRIDE_MINUTES, so the sweep costs ``stride`` frame
    derivations: ~2.5 s at stride 60 and ~13.5 s at stride 10, at the ~275 ms per
    derivation measured 2026-10-01. ``_radar_window_union`` picks the samples
    inside a flight's own window. Cache key is empty: the samples are seeded by the
    controller state and invalidated by ``cache_clear()`` in the tests.

    **This accessor is not on the build path.** ``_radar_avoidance`` calls
    :func:`_virtual_radar_sweep` directly, because the build has to report
    progress through that phase and a memoised function cannot take the reporter.
    The empty cache key is the second reason: it makes the samples independent of
    the seed, so a memoised hit would hand a later build the previous seed's echo.
    Tests and probes keep using this wrapper.

    **Two tiers per sample, from one derivation.** The field tier (>=35 dBZ,
    the legend's 须绕飞) and the core tier (the shield's >=45 dBZ core, the
    legend's 禁止进入) are both needed, because they are treated differently
    from 0.9.22 on -- but they come out of the same ``shield()`` call, so
    carrying both costs one extra geometry per sample and **no** extra frame
    derivation. Storing only the field and re-deriving for the core would have
    doubled the sweep for data that was already in hand.
```

### L759

```
The uncached sweep. ``progress(fraction)`` fires once per stride step.

    Split out of :func:`_virtual_radar_samples` (which is ``lru_cache``d and
    therefore cannot take a callback) so the build can report the one phase that
    would otherwise look frozen. See ``PROGRESS_SWEEP_PERCENT``.

    The callback is deliberately *not* part of the memo: a progress reporter is
    per-build state, and folding it into the key would make every build a miss and
    every report a lie about how much work was actually done.

    ``seed`` 由调用方给定则按它扫掠，省略则**读一次**控制器种子。读一次是判据、
    不是优化：此前它在循环体内逐帧 ``weather_control.seed()``，于是一次构建中途换
    种子会把两套天气的并集写进同一份计划，而键里只有一个种子 —— 同一个病。

    ``diagnostics`` 是可选出参字典，收三种计数：``unavailable``（帧不可用）、
    ``notSynthetic``（这一帧不是生成器产生的）、``empty``（帧可用但未达 35 dBZ）。
    做成出参而不是返回值，是因为既有的四个消费点（``_virtual_radar_samples``、三个
    探针）都按"扫掠返回样本元组"读它，改成解包会把"扫掠返回什么"悄悄改掉。

    为什么必须分三种：``sweepSlots == 0`` 在虚拟通道里**不是**合法状态 —— 回波是
    生成的，"取不到"不可能发生。把它混成一句 ``RADAR_UNAVAILABLE``，就是用一句听起来
    很合理的弃权盖住"取帧走了别的通道"，而调用方**有**一条读起来很合理的弃权分支。
    三种计数分开之后，:func:`_radar_avoidance` 才能把它们翻成三个不同的 reason。

    ``notSynthetic`` 是可红的那一种：它计的是"帧上没有生成器的章"。这条路径若被重新
    接回一个解析活开关的访问器（正是 2026-10-15 那次失效的形状），拿到的是实测帧，
    这里就会计数 —— 计划据此**拒绝**这份几何，而不是把别人的回波当虚拟回波用。
```

### L812

```
The phase sentence for a build percentage.

    One mapping, used by the API payload and therefore by the bar's caption. The
    alternative -- a caption chosen in the browser from a second set of thresholds
    -- is how a progress display starts claiming "规划航路" while the backend is
    still deriving radar frames, which is worse than showing nothing.
```

### L827

```
Avoidance geometry for one flight's own window, for one tier.

    ``radar_obstacle`` is either a plain geometry (measured mode: one
    observation, applied to the whole window) or the tuple of
    ``(minute, field, core)`` samples from :func:`_virtual_radar_samples` -- in
    which case only samples bracketing the flight's own [start, end] are
    considered, so a storm that sits on this corridor at this hour blocks it
    without painting the whole network.

    ``tier`` selects which of the two geometries in each sample is used:
    ``'field'`` (>=35 dBZ, 须绕飞) or ``'core'`` (>=45 dBZ, 禁止进入). A plain
    geometry has no tiers and is returned for either, which is what keeps the
    measured branch unchanged.

    ``probe`` is the flight's own geometry (a LineString) and is what makes the
    common case cheap. Two things matter, both measured on a real build
    (2026-09-24, 7760 flights, virtual mode):

      * **The bbox test runs first.** Most flights never come near the echo, and
        a probe whose bbox misses every sample's bbox cannot intersect any of
        them, so the exact (and more expensive) ``intersects`` is skipped.
      * **The union is paid for once per window, not once per flight.** Building
        it per flight cost ~34 s of a 57 s build, because 7760 flights share far
        fewer distinct 5-minute window pairs.

    An earlier version returned on the **first** sample the probe touched, on the
    grounds that the union was only ever an intermediate. That is sound for a
    yes/no question and unsound for the router: a detour computed against sample
    1 alone may still cross sample 3, and the plan would then ship a path through
    painted echo. So ``probe=None`` returns the window's **union** -- the set,
    which is what the router needs -- while ``probe=<geometry>`` returns the
    union of only the samples that geometry genuinely crosses, which is all a
    decision needs. Both come from the same memo.

    Results are memoised per window via :func:`_window_sample_span` (``start``
    floored to 5 minutes, ``end`` rounded **up** to the stride grid) and keyed
    on the bucket pair **and the tier**: the sample list is a per-build global and
    the cache is cleared at the top of every build (see ``_build``), but the two
    tiers of the same window are different answers and must not share a slot.
```

### L907

```
The sample offsets a window's union must cover -- both brackets, not one.

    ``(lo, hi)`` is the quantised pair every window query and both memos key on.
    ``lo`` floors ``start`` to five minutes; ``hi`` **rounds ``end`` up to the
    stride grid**.

    The round-up is the 2026-10-16 fix, and it is the difference between the code
    and its own docstring. ``_radar_window_union`` has always claimed to consider
    "samples **bracketing** the flight's own [start, end]" -- but the filter read
    ``sample_offset <= floor5(end)``, which only ever looks *backwards* from the
    window. A flight whose whole window falls between two samples (window
    115-118.9 min, samples at 110 and 120) was therefore planned against
    ``field(110)`` alone, with ``field(120)`` -- the bracket that actually
    describes the echo at its arrival minute -- dropped. Measured with
    ``tools/probes/_probe_flight_vs_echo_timeline.py`` (seed 42, virtual): 31 of
    35 field-layer incursions penetrated the painted >=35 dBZ field by *more than*
    the 250 m allowance, the deepest by **13.5 km**, and for every one of them a
    sweep sample already contained the aircraft -- the geometry was in hand and
    the window filter threw it away. That is why this is a bug fix and not a
    stride change: the dose-response table above shows what a finer stride costs
    (2.4x the build) and this costs one extra sample in the union.
```

### L937

```
The >=35 dBZ band for one window, eroded by ``penetration_m``.

    This is the region a path may not reach, per the tolerant tier's rule (see
    :func:`routing.penetration_limit`), restated here because the planner has to
    re-check a path after ``rounded_path`` has moved it. Memoised per window, so
    a flight whose corridor touches echo pays for the buffer once, and so does
    every later flight sharing the same quantised window pair.

    An empty result means "no limit in this window": the erosion removed the
    whole footprint, i.e. every part of it is thinner than ``2 * penetration_m``.
    That is the honest reading rather than a special case -- a path cannot stand
    anywhere inside a sub-500 m speck without immediately reaching its middle,
    so such a speck cannot be a constraint.
```

### L1003

```
窗口内**第一帧**与 ``probe`` 真正相交的回波出现在第几分钟。

    这是"滚动重判"在**信息层**能给出的那一半答案：航路是构建期定下的，但它不是
    每一分钟都需要偏离。这个函数回答的是"从第几分钟起才必须绕"，而
    :func:`_route_basis` 回答"为谁而绕"。

    刻意**不**假定样本按分钟有序（`continue` 而不是 `break`）：顺序今天是成立的
    （见 `_virtual_radar_samples` 的 `steps`），但一个建立在"上游保持有序"上的
    早退，会在上游某次改动后静默返回错误的分钟（§八 ⑬）。

    ``probe`` 的包围盒先测 —— 与 `_radar_window_union` 的 probe 分支同一条理由：
    绝大多数航路根本不碰回波，逐帧 `intersects` 是白付的。
```

### L1037

```
这条航路为什么不是直线。返回 ``(basis, from_minute)``。

    **判据必须落在"直连段"上，而不是在"声明走廊"上** —— 这是本函数第二版，第一版
    判的是走廊，实测（2026-10-03，实测通道，seed 42，7760 班）给出 7641 班全部
    `CLEAR`，而同一份计划里有 **42 班标着 `WEATHER_DETOUR`**。两者不可能同时为真，
    于是去查了 `route()` 的调用次数：**7965 次** —— 几乎每一班都被重路由了。

    原因在规划器自己的表达式里：``needs_avoidance`` 的第三项是
    ``not core_window.is_empty``，而实测通道没有分级，``_radar_window_union`` 直接
    把那份几何原样返回（它的 docstring 写明了这一点），于是这一项退化成
    **「这份计划里存在回波」** —— 与"这条走廊有没有被挡"无关。所以每一班都会走
    ``route(lateral[0], lateral[-1], ...)``：**路径是走廊两端点之间的最短避障路径，
    走廊的中间航路点被丢掉了**。也就是说 `routeId` 声明的走廊并不是实际飞的那条。

    这就解释了用户 2026-10-03 的原话「明明他直飞的路线没有气象干扰为什么不变」：
    实际画出来的航迹在绝大多数班次上就是那条直线（`route()` 无障碍时返回直线，
    见 `routing.route` 的 ``if valid(start, end)`` 分支），它当然不变。

    第二版把判据移到直连段上之后，那 42 班仍然全是 `CLEAR` —— 于是把 `route()` 的
    四个条件逐个量了一遍（仓库外的临时探针，包住本函数、用它**收到的那几个对象**判，
    而不是自己重新推一遍集合）：固定管制区 **0**、派生风险区 **0**、雷达回波 **0**、
    **作业区边界 55**。所以第五个成因是 `BOUNDARY`，而且它是实测通道里**唯一生效**的
    那一个：42 班「受气象影响改航」里，没有一班是气象造成的。

    ``basis`` 取自 :data:`ROUTE_BASIS`；``from_minute`` 是成因回波第一次盖住直连段的
    分钟，**成因没有分钟时为 ``None``**：固定管制区是一次行政划定，点观测是一帧
    观测，作业区边界是一次范围划定，三者都是"整段计划窗口内一直成立"的事实。给它们
    编一个分钟，就是 M30 —— 两种世界共用一个读数。
```

### L1126

```
One flight's clearance, assembled only from facts the plan already holds.

    Every value here is read from the flight record rather than recomputed, so the
    clearance can never disagree with the plan it describes. ``None`` is used
    wherever the plan has no fact -- an empty field is honest, an invented one is
    not.
```

### L1157

```
分钟数的显示口径：**不足 1 分钟不许取整成 0**。

    改航量是浮点数，判据是 ``> 0.01``，而显示原先一律 ``round()`` —— 于是 0.4 分钟的
    改航在班次列表里写成「+0 分」、在放行许可里写成「航程增加约 0 分钟」。一个"按回波
    改航"的班次配上一句"0 分钟"，读者只能得出"这系统在自己骗自己"：过滤条件说它改航了，
    数字说它什么也没发生。实测（2026-10-02 11:41 那一帧）35 个改航班次**全部**落在
    0.01–0.5 之间，也就是说当时那一屏写的是 35 行「+0 分」。

    所以显示口径必须承认这一段，而不是把它抹平。前端 ``runtime.js`` 的
    ``detourMinutesShort`` 是同一条规则的另一半（那里输出 ``<1 分``，给滚动列表的
    `+N 分` 用），两处都有测试钉住；改一处必须改另一处。
```

### L1176

```
这条放行许可是按哪一份气象判据批的。

    用户第 3 条要求"贴近民航飞行管理系统，但核心依旧是气象为主导"。一份不带气象
    判据的放行许可会让系统看起来像在做行政排班，而那与本项目的前提相反 —— 所以这条
    注释是许可的**组成部分**，不是装饰。它同时区分通道：实测与虚拟的许可依据不同，
    混为一谈会把一份演示用的许可说得像一次实测放行。
```

### L1211

```
Fixed (non-weather) airspace restrictions, for the network payload.

    Shipped once with ``GET /traffic/network`` rather than inside the weather
    frame: these zones come from published law and public data, so they do not
    change with the scrubber minute.
```

### L1327

```
Plan the eight-hour traffic timeline.

    **不要直接调用这个函数** —— 它不可重入（见 `_SIMULATE_LOCK`），外部入口是文件里
    靠后的 `simulate()`，那里持锁。本函数只负责"算"，不负责"串行"。

    ``weather_mode`` pins the weather channel this plan is built against
    (``measured`` / ``virtual``). It is captured by the caller -- the session key
    is derived from it -- and passed down instead of being re-read from
    :mod:`traffic.weather_control` at every use, so a channel switched *during* a
    build cannot produce a plan whose key names one channel and whose geometry
    came from the other. Defaults to the channel in force.

    The traffic itself is generated here -- cities, routes, departure waves,
    payloads, conflicts -- and that is deliberate: the network is a modelled
    scenario, not a feed. **Weather is not generated here.** The scenarios differ
    only in which real weather statement reaches the planner:

      * ``live``     -- the public snapshot drives avoidance geometry;
      * ``clear``    -- no weather is claimed, so no avoidance geometry is added;
      * ``unknown``  -- the weather feed is treated as unavailable, so every
                        flight is held. That is a negative statement, not an
                        observation.

    There used to be a fourth, ``moving``, which built a synthetic advecting
    field and routed around it. It is gone: a risk area this system draws has to
    trace back to a measurement or a named model run, and a seeded random walk
    traced back to nothing. Removing it does not change what the planner can
    express -- ``live`` already routes around real contours -- it only removes
    the ability to claim the weather was observed when it was invented.

    ``progress`` is called with a percentage as the build advances, across three
    bands rather than only the flight loop; see ``PROGRESS_STAGES`` for why the
    radar sweep gets a band of its own. It is also threaded down to
    ``_radar_avoidance`` so the sweep reports per sample instead of appearing as a
    single jump. Every call is monotone and inside ``0..100``, which is what lets
    the workbench bar animate rather than reset.
```

### L2044

```
``_plan_trajectory`` 的**串行**入口 —— 唯一的对外入口。

    它只做一件事：持 `_SIMULATE_LOCK` 再转调。为什么要多这一层而不是把锁写进函数体：
    函数体有 600 余行且只有一个 `return`，在体内加锁要么给整段加缩进（一次几千行的
    假差异），要么手工 acquire/release（异常路径上容易漏）。包一层是唯一不会写错的写法。

    ``simulate`` 这个名字必须留在模块级：`_build` 是在**调用时**按模块全局查这个名字的，
    测试也用 `monkeypatch.setattr(service, 'simulate', ...)` 换掉它 —— 两者都靠这层间接。
```

### L2058

```
Hub/pad occupancy only. Crossing zones are reported in aggregate because
    their population is data-driven rather than a fixed facility list.
```

### L2081

```
The session key. Everything a plan's *content* depends on goes in here.

    ``weather_mode`` is part of the key because it changes the plan and used to
    be absent from it. ``traffic.weather_control`` switches the radar channel at
    runtime, and the radar channel is avoidance geometry: in virtual mode the
    painted >=35 dBz area spans ~100,000 km2 with a ban tier over 吉林, while the
    measured echo of that same hour had an empty ban tier whose field did not
    reach 43.8N at all.

    Leaving the channel out of the key meant the switch repainted the map and
    left the plan alone -- and worse, made it unrebuildable: ``session()`` found
    the cached key and returned the other channel's plan, so pressing 重新调度
    reproduced it. The operator's report was "there are aircraft inside the
    no-fly area, and the detours are not controllable" (2026-09-30), and this is
    the whole of the reason. Keying on the channel is what makes the two
    channels two plans rather than one plan and one lie.

    ``workspace`` 是只加不减的第五个输入（2026-10-14）。没有它的时候，两个浏览器
    用默认参数打开就是**同一个键、同一份计划**：屏幕上看起来是两个用户，服务端只有
    一个人。同一个道理 —— 键里少了哪一维，那一维就被静默共享。

    **空工作区不进哈希。** 没带标识的调用方（巡检探针、curl、既有单元测试）必须
    拿到改造前那个一模一样的键，否则"改造前行为保持"这条判据就量不出回归了。

    ``radar`` 是**实测通道**下将要驱动避让的那帧雷达的标识（时次），由
    :func:`traffic.weather_control.frame_identity` 给出：没有中继帧时它是空串，空串同样
    不进哈希，理由与工作区那条一字不差。有中继帧时它必须进键，否则同样的事情会以另一种
    顺序重演 —— 中继晚到一步，键没变，``session()`` 于是把那份"没有雷达几何"的计划原样
    交还，而地图已经在画那片回波了（2026-10-15）。键里少了哪一维，那一维就被静默共享，
    这句话在这个函数里已经付过两次账。
```

### L2158

```
配置指纹：机队档案 + 运行参数 + 气象阈值。

    **为什么共享键里必须有这一维。** 共享的前提是"同一份内容"，而计划内容不只取决于
    （种子, 场景, 快照, 通道, 雷达帧）—— 它同样取决于 `aircraft-profiles.json` 里的
    分离标准、等待上限、巡航高度层，以及能见度/阵风阈值。少了这一维，改一次阈值之后
    新访客仍会拿到按旧阈值算出来的计划，而两份计划的 `fingerprint` 可能是同一个常量。

    这一维也是测试需要的：用例会用 `monkeypatch` 改阈值再跑一遍，若共享键不含配置，
    第二个用例就会命中第一个用例的计划 —— 症状是"改了阈值但结果没变"，而它看起来
    像判据失效。实测（2026-10-27 全量套件）这一条确实让 K10 在整套里红、单独跑绿。
```

### L2313

```
Read only the header of a cached session, so we never parse its 20 MB body.

    ``fingerprint`` is written early in the payload (see ``simulate``), but rather
    than depending on that ordering we scan a generous head window for the key.
    A file we cannot attribute is treated as stale, which is the safe direction:
    the worst outcome is one recomputation.
```

### L2330

```
Drop cached sessions whose fingerprint no longer matches the code.

    ``_fingerprint()`` hashes every ``traffic/*.py`` file, so any source edit
    invalidates every cached plan at once. The in-memory ``JOBS`` eviction above
    never touched the disk, so before this existed the store grew without bound --
    one measured machine had accumulated 73 files / 1.4 GB across 22 fingerprint
    generations, of which only 25 files belonged to the live revision.

    Cached plans are pure derivatives (recomputable from seed + scenario), so
    discarding a stale generation loses no evidence. Failures are swallowed on
    purpose: a housekeeping step must never break the request that triggered it.

    **2026-10-24：跨代次清理做对了，同代次却完全没有上限。** 实测服务器上
    同一代次积了 **20 份 / 494 MB**（后来涨到 694 MB），`_fingerprint` 不变就不会
    被删 —— 而每个新工作区都会写一份 25 MB。这里补一条同代次的容量上限：
    按 mtime 保留最近 ``KEEP_PER_GENERATION`` 份，且**最近
    ``RECENT_PROTECT_SECONDS`` 秒内动过的一律不删**（可能正是正在轮询的那份，
    删了会让下一次请求从"读盘 0.5 秒"退化成"重建 20 秒"）。
```

### L2397

```
在构建线程里绑定调用方的工作区，然后跑 `_build`。

    **为什么需要这一层。** `ThreadPoolExecutor.submit()` 不复制调用方的上下文，
    而 `_build` 会在里面读 `weather_control.seed()` / `radar_frame()`。不绑定的话，
    构建读到的是**空工作区**那一份（＝另一个人的雷达帧与种子），而计划键仍属于调用方
    —— 结果是"计划对了、气象错了"，且没有任何判据会变红：键、状态码、字段全都合法。

    `finally` 里的 `reset` 也不是洁癖：线程会被复用，泄漏出去的工作区会让下一个
    任务在错误的命名空间里跑，症状是随机的、只在并发下出现。

    ``retain`` 只在预建时为 ``False``（见 ``_prewarm_sibling``）。它不是一个
    "要不要缓存"的性能开关，而是一条**内存判据**：预建的结果只落盘、不进 ``JOBS``。
    ``retain`` 必须留在**参数表末尾** —— `test_radar_avoidance.py` 把
    ``POOL.submit(_build_entry, ...)`` 的实参按**名字**与形参表配对，往中间插一个
    参数会让它把后面的实参全部读错位，而那种错位不会抛异常。
```

### L2418

```
预建让位时抛出的控制流信号。

    刻意继承 ``BaseException`` 而不是 ``Exception``：``simulate()`` 沿途有多处
    ``except Exception`` 兜底（气象源、雷达帧、雾区都在里面）。若它是普通异常，中途
    被某一处吞掉，"我已经让位了"这件事就没有发生 —— 用户仍然排在预建后面等 22 秒，
    而所有判据全绿。让位是控制流，不是一次失败，所以不该被失败处理器收走。
```

### L2435

```
把**已经释放**的内存真正还给操作系统。

    为什么需要显式做：一次建场是 7760 个班次的仿真，中间对象能到数百 MB，
    而最终只保留约 54 MB。CPython 的引用计数会立刻 `free()`，但 glibc 默认把那
    些页留在自己的 arena 里 —— 进程 RSS 就**停在峰值**上，看起来像泄漏。

    实测（2026-10-24，腾讯云 2C3.7G）：重启后基线 148.6 MB；
    建完第 1 个工作区 RSS 387.8 MB、峰值 484 MB；而按数据规模稳态应在 200 MB 左右。
    `VmData 583 MB` 与 `RSS 489 MB` 之间那 94 MB 就是"分配过、已经不用、但没还"。

    `malloc_trim(0)` 让 glibc 遍历各 arena 并 `madvise(MADV_DONTNEED)` 掉空闲页。
    它**只影响已释放的页**，不会动正在用的数据，所以对正确性没有影响；
    代价是一次几十毫秒的遍历，放在建场收尾完全可以接受。

    Windows 上没有 `libc.so.6`（本地开发），`ctypes.CDLL` 会抛，吞掉即可 ——
    这一条在 Linux 上才有意义，而线上就是 Linux。
```

### L2528

```
把"另一个气象通道"的计划也在后台算出来，让换通道变成一次磁盘命中。

    **为什么值得做。** 换通道（实测 ⇄ 虚拟）会改变会话键，于是**必然**是一次冷建。
    实测（2026-10-15，腾讯云，seed 42 / live / 7760 班）：逐班次规划 **22.1 秒**，
    载荷传输 **5.6 秒**（已按 `TRAJECTORY_*_DECIMALS` 降精度）。不预建的话，用户每换
    一次通道都要重付那 22 秒 —— 这正是线上被报的「换虚拟或实测的气象要等 1 分钟」。

    **代价与边界（都要写出来，否则这是一次隐形裁剪）：**
      · 复用**同一个** ``POOL``（单 worker）。仿真循环是纯 Python、受 GIL 约束，给它
        开第二个 worker 只会把两边各拖慢一倍，不会更快（服务器 2 核，实测负载 0.02）。
      · 真实请求一到，本次预建**立刻让位**（``_PrewarmAbandoned``）：预建是为了让用户
        更快，不能反过来让用户排队。
      · 结果只落盘、不进 ``JOBS``（内存账见 ``_build`` 里那段）。
      · 预建自己**不再递归预建**（``retain=False`` 的构建不会走到这里），所以不存在
        A 预建 B、B 又预建 A 的来回。
      · **姊妹键里含雷达帧的标识**（``weather_control.frame_identity``），所以中继帧换了
        一个时次之后**两份计划一起作废**，换通道会重付一次冷建。这是刻意的取舍，与
        ``session()`` 那边一字不差：一份避让几何来自上一个观测、而地图已经在画新观测的
        计划，正是 2026-10-15 那次线上失效。**时效的上界**＝中继帧的 TTL，不是"永远"。

    预建失败是**静默**的（只进日志、不进 ``JOBS``）：它没有调用方，也就没有可以告诉的人。
```

### L2571

```
One plan per (seed, scenario, weather channel, weather snapshot, workspace).

    ``workspace`` 省略时取当前请求绑定的工作区。它进了计划键，所以两个浏览器各自
    有自己的 `JOBS` 槽、自己的计划文件、自己的操作日志命名空间 —— 隔离不是靠过滤
    读出来的，而是键不同、对象根本就不是同一个。
```

### L2661

```
切气象通道后，把**别的通道**留下的计划从内存槽里摘掉（磁盘文件留着）。

    为什么需要这一步：`JOBS` 只留 4 个槽，满了会踢掉一个非 BUILDING 的旧条目。
    切到虚拟通道的瞬间，槽里通常正躺着 1–2 个实测计划；新通道的计划要么被这个上限
    挤掉、要么挤掉别人 —— 而两种结局在页面上长得一样：**上一通道的避让几何继续挂在
    屏幕上**，旁边却是新通道的分档统计（0.11.10 复测 N-4：941.4 km² 内核 vs 避让 0）。

    只摘内存、不删磁盘：切回来时要能立刻命中旧通道那份已算好的计划，否则"切错了能
    切回来"就会变成"切回来还要再等 20 秒"。
```

### L2688

```
案例回放的天气帧（0.11.7 / F-2）。

    **不合成任何几何**：只有 4 个站的实测能见度，所以只给点、不给面。
    要画"雾区面"必须有格点场 —— 案例没有，硬造就是编造观测。
```

### L2761

```
The weather picture for one view instant.

    There is one weather source now: the public snapshot. ``live`` asks for it,
    ``clear`` states that no adverse weather is being claimed, and ``unknown``
    states that the feed is unavailable. All three return the same payload shape
    so the renderer never has to guess which branch produced it -- and none of
    them invents geometry. The measured radar channel is attached in every case,
    because a radar frame is a real observation regardless of what the point
    feed is doing.

    ``weatherMode`` names which controller branch supplied the radar channel, so
    a reader of the payload can tell a measured mosaic from a generated one
    without inspecting the geometry. The point-observation feed is unaffected by
    the switch: there is no synthetic point feed, and inventing one would be a
    different and much larger claim than a generated radar picture.
```

### L2829

```
The **numbers** behind ``radarAvoidance``, published with every plan.

    The interface has been able to say "≥35 dBZ must be avoided / ≥45 dBZ core is
    no-entry" for several versions -- as prose, in the legend and in
    ``radarAvoidance.note`` -- while the payload carried three tier *areas*
    (``coreAreaKm2`` / ``cautionAreaKm2`` / ``watchAreaKm2``) that no reader could
    check against anything, because nothing said how the tiers are cut. An
    assertion is not evidence: the same evaluation now carries the thresholds it
    used, which costs no extra derivation and adds no second rule.

    Every value here is **read from the one place that owns it**, never retyped:
    the band edges from :mod:`traffic.cma_tiles`, the core inset from
    :data:`traffic.radar_field.CORE_INSET_M`, the clipping depth from the declared
    horizontal separation (this project does not invent a second standard for
    storm contours -- see the ``penetration_m`` note in ``simulate``).

    Published on **every** branch, including the ones that carry no geometry. A
    rule is a declaration, not an observation: it holds on a clear day and in a
    scenario that declares no weather. Omitting it there would suggest the rule
    only exists when it fires.
```

### L2861

```
Radar reflectivity as avoidance geometry for the planner.

    Why the radar feeds routing at all: the map paints a >=45 dBZ core as a
    prohibition and 35-45 dBZ as fly-with-care, and the legend says so. A plan
    whose flights cut straight through a painted strong-echo core contradicts
    the map in the one place an operator is looking. The virtual mode exists
    precisely to demonstrate storm-driven rerouting; with the radar absent from
    the planner, that demo showed dense clouds while every flight flew straight
    through them -- reported by the user on 2026-09-24 as "there are no-fly
    clouds but no rerouting".

    One declared subtlety, kept honest rather than hidden: the MEASURED radar,
    like the point snapshot, is ONE observation of ONE slot.
    ``PublicWeather.envelope`` already applies its single moment across the plan
    window, so the measured radar follows that same declared semantics instead
    of inventing a second rule. ``radarAvoidance`` in the plan result carries
    the slot so a reader can tell which observation drove the detours.

    The VIRTUAL radar is different and must be: since 0.9.18 it advances with
    the playback minute (``radar_regions``), and the field advects ~99 km over
    the 8-hour plan window (measured 2026-09-24). Avoiding only the minute-0
    footprint would let a flight depart through empty sky and fly straight into
    the storm that drifted onto its corridor -- a plan that contradicts the map
    the operator is scrubbing.

    Virtual mode therefore samples the FIELD tier along the timeline and returns
    the per-slot geometries; the planner unions only the slots inside each
    flight's own window. A single swept union of the whole window was tried
    first and closed all 7760 flights: a storm over one corridor at one hour
    plus another storm elsewhere at another hour becomes "weather everywhere,
    always", which is exactly the "an upper bound that binds is an invisible
    crop" failure of M25. The per-window union keeps the map and the plan
    describing the same world.

    Only ``live`` participates: ``clear`` claims no adverse weather and
    ``unknown`` claims no usable source, so either would contradict a
    radar-driven detour. Stratiform echo (15-35 dBZ) never enters -- the WATCH
    tier exists exactly to say "rain you fly through".

    ``tiers`` reads ``CONVECTIVE_AND_STRONG``, and that label is worth a note
    because it was **true before the geometry underneath it was**. ``shield``'s
    field used to be only the 35-45 dBZ *interval* band, so every >=45 dBZ pixel
    -- the storm cores -- fell outside the geometry the planner avoided, while
    the label went on correctly claiming both tiers. A right label over a wrong
    set is worse than a wrong label: it makes the omission look intentional.
    See ISS-0.9.19-05; the label is now backed by
    ``unary_union([CONVECTIVE, STRONG])``.

    ``weather_mode`` is passed in rather than read from the controller, so the
    geometry this function returns always belongs to the channel the session key
    names. Reading the live switch here would let a flip mid-build produce a plan
    that claims one channel and contains the other's avoidance set.

    **同一条纪律要到下一层才算落实（2026-10-15）。** 上面这段话在写成时只对了一半：
    虚拟分支自己没读开关，但它调的 :func:`_virtual_radar_derived` 一路问到了会解析活
    开关的访问器上，于是"这条路径属于哪个通道"仍然由**此刻开关朝哪边**决定。后果不是
    理论上的：``_prewarm_sibling`` 会在**还处于实测通道**的工作区里预建虚拟通道的计划，
    这条路径于是拿实测帧当虚拟回波，而实测源对云端出口整站 403 —— 49 个时次全退化，
    操作台上是 12 架载具停在禁入区内核里，而 ``radarAvoidance`` 只说了句
    ``RADAR_UNAVAILABLE``。闸门全绿、字段全合法、原因说错了。
    ⇒ 取帧只能由实参决定；两条通道各有一条拒绝分支（实测 ``CHANNEL_CHANGED_DURING_BUILD``，
    虚拟 ``VIRTUAL_FRAME_NOT_SYNTHETIC`` / ``VIRTUAL_FRAME_UNAVAILABLE``）。

    ``progress`` is the build's progress reporter, threaded down so the virtual
    sweep can report as it derives. It is optional and defaults to nothing, which
    is what keeps the measured branch and every direct caller unchanged. The
    measured branch never reports: one observation is one derivation, which is not
    a phase anyone can watch.
```

### L3025

```
低能见度（雾）作为避让几何，**结构与雷达通道逐项对应**。

    返回 ``(ban_geom, caution_discs, info)``：

      * ``ban_geom``   —— 深灰档的并集，进 ``hard``。与固定禁入同一条规则：不可触碰，
        起降点落在里面就拒绝本班次（``route()`` 不可能把飞机挪出包含它自己的限制）。
      * ``caution_discs`` —— 浅灰档**逐城**的圆，进 ``soft``。留成列表而不是并集，是因为
        豁免是**按圆**做的（见下），并集之后就无法区分"是哪一座城的圆包住了枢纽"。
      * ``info`` —— 规则数字与计数，原样发布。

    与雷达通道的三处同构，写下来是为了让第三条通道能照着加：
      ① 入口是 ``*_avoidance(scenario, …)``，非 ``live`` 一律不入（``clear`` 声称没有天气、
         ``unknown`` 声称没有可用源，两者都不该驱动一次避让）；
      ② 两档语义与雷达的 ``core``/``field`` 完全一致（硬＝不可触碰，软＝可擦边
         ``penetration_m``）；
      ③ 规则数字走 ``fog_rule_block()``，与几何同一次求值，不另抄一份。

    **不合成**：站点没有发布能见度、或快照不可用时，这里返回空几何加一条 ``reason``，
    绝不按坐标或邻站补一个值出来。

    与雷达**必须不同**的一处，也是这条通道唯一的特殊规则：浅灰档的**枢纽豁免**。
    雷达的场是栅格实测出来的形状，而雾只有"一个站、一个数"，范围是本系统自己声明的
    圆；更深的一层区别是 ``rules-v1.json`` 把 3 km 能见度判为 ``CAUTION``（扣 12 分），
    不是 ``BAN``。让一个本系统自己只判"警戒"的读数去**拒绝整座城**，就是把数归给它
    没被算过的那个原因。具体做法在 ``simulate()`` 里：起降点落在某个浅灰圆内时，
    **那个圆**对本班次不作约束，并计入 ``padExemptFlights``。不这么做的话，任何一条
    从该城出发的航路都必须先穿过包围自己枢纽的环 —— 那就是 2026-09-30 那 2488 班
    关闭的形状（"谨慎进入"变成"不许起飞"）。
```

### L3104

```
浅灰档的**枢纽豁免**：规则本身，独立成一个纯函数。

    为什么它必须是一个具名函数而不是 `simulate()` 里的七行内联代码：这是本通道
    **唯一**一条会改变班次去留的规则，而 `simulate()` 是全项目最难单独测量的函数。
    抽出来之后，"起降点落在圆里时那个圆被摘掉"这件事可以**直接**断言，不必跑一次
    40 秒的构建去间接推断。

    返回 ``(geometry_or_None, exempt_city_ids)``：

      * 没有任何圆被豁免 → ``None``，调用方沿用整层的几何（**不是**空几何 ——
        "什么都没豁免"与"全豁免了"是相反的结论，用一个空几何表示会让它们长得一样）；
      * 否则 → 剩下的圆的并集（可能为空：所有圆都被豁免了），以及被豁免城的 id 列表。

    规则本身：起降点落在某个圆**内部**时摘掉**那个圆**，不是摘掉整层 —— 其余城市的
    浅灰圆对本班次照常生效。理由见 ``weather.fog_tiers`` 的长注释（站心圆必然包住
    自己的枢纽，不豁免就等于把"能见度偏低"读成"不许起飞"）。
```

### L3135

```
Count flights by reason, largest first. Empty reasons are dropped.

    By default this is a census of the **whole fleet**: a flight that was
    accepted still carries a reason (``CLEAR``, ``WEATHER_DETOUR`` or similar),
    which is why the default tally is dominated by ``CLEAR`` and must not be read
    as a list of closure causes.

    ``closed_only=True`` restricts it to flights that were not accepted, which is
    the tally a reader takes as "why these are closed". The two are separate keys
    in the payload rather than one key and a subtraction, because the subtraction
    is exactly the step that got skipped when the panel labelled `closed` with a
    single weather-ish caption.
```

### L3160

```
Background wind vectors on a uniform grid over the operating box.

    These used to be emitted one per city observation, placed at the city's own
    hub coordinate. That made the arrows read as a property of the city markers
    rather than of the airspace: on the all-provinces view there were 36 arrows,
    clustered on the hubs, with the rest of the map empty -- and the layer is
    named 背景风向, i.e. the ambient field an operator reads to understand which
    way a band will drift. A field drawn only where the cities are is not that.

    The grid is sampled on the operating area's **own outline**, not on the
    bounding box: for each province the sampler walks its meridian span in
    ``WIND_BAND_M`` steps and lays points across the span at each band, keeping
    only those that land inside the outline. A sample therefore either sits on
    the area or is not produced, which is what stops arrows appearing on foreign
    territory. Each sample draws its speed and direction from the nearest station
    observations weighted by distance (inverse-distance weighting, the same
    estimator the project uses for the field itself, so the arrows and the
    geometry agree about the wind).

    ``city_id`` keeps its old meaning of "zoom to one city": the grid collapses to
    that city's own sample, because a city view is a few kilometres across and a
    province-wide lattice over it would say nothing.

    Provenance is carried per vector, so a reader can tell a measured station
    reading from an interpolated one without knowing this module's internals.
```

### L3235

```
Lay sample points across the operating area's own outline.

    The bounding box is a rectangle drawn around an irregular three-province
    outline, so most of a box-wide lattice falls on blank map. This walks the
    outline's latitudinal extent in :data:`WIND_BAND_M` bands, and in each band
    intersects the outline with a parallel to obtain the longitude spans that are
    actually inside the area -- then places points across those spans at roughly
    :data:`WIND_SAMPLE_SPACING_M`. A point is emitted only after a final
    containment test, so the returned list contains nothing off the area.

    Working from the spans is also what makes this cheap enough to run per
    request: a dense box lattice costs one containment test per box cell,
    whereas here the tests are bounded by the outline itself.
```

### L3282

```
Convert a north-south distance in metres to degrees of latitude.

    Kept local and crude on purpose: the operating area spans a few degrees of
    latitude, over which a degree is close enough to constant that the band count
    does not move. Using a helper rather than inlining the constant keeps the
    assumption visible at the one place it is made.
```

### L3293

```
Longitude spans of a W-E line clipped to the operating area.

    ``area.intersection(parallel)`` returns a LineString, a MultiLineString, or
    (at a tangent) a bare Point or MultiPoint. Only the linear parts describe a
    span, so tangencies are dropped rather than guessed at -- a band that touches
    the outline at one point has no width to sample across.
```

### L1664

```
Is ``line`` clear of weather over ``[begin, end]``?

            A ground hold does not merely delay an arrival: it slides the flight
            through a different slice of a moving echo. Clearing each attempt at
            its own times is what turns "the corridor is never clear" into "the
            corridor is clear in forty minutes", and only the first of those is a
            closure. It is also the rule the departure ladder above used to pick
            the path, so the two agree by construction rather than by luck.

            The strictness is the router's, deliberately: the hard tier may not be
            touched at all, and the tolerant tier may be clipped by at most
            ``penetration_m``. A check demanding plain avoidance instead would
            close a flight for exactly the clip the router had been told to
            permit, and the two rules would disagree on every borderline path.
```


## backend-python/traffic/spatial.py

### L84

```
# Cheap 2-D reject. The closest approach of two segments is attained
# at an endpoint of one of them, so comparing endpoint distances
# bounds it from above; if even that bound clears `horizontal`, the
# true minimum distance does too.
```

### L166

```
# Tangency distance for the requested radius. Long enough lead-in/out is
# a hard condition; a short segment cannot hide an instantaneous corner.
```

### L21

```
``(minx, miny, maxx, maxy)`` of a trajectory's horizontal footprint.

    Precomputed once per accepted flight and once per candidate, so the pair test
    below is O(1) instead of walking both point lists.
```

### L32

```
True when two footprints grown by ``horizontal`` are disjoint on some axis.

    The **same** necessary condition ``_segments_apart`` applies to a segment
    pair, applied once to the whole pair of trajectories. It is strictly weaker,
    so it can only remove work the inner loop would have rejected anyway -- which
    is the only kind of shortcut a conflict detector is allowed to take.

    Measured (2026-10-03): ``conflict`` was the single hottest leaf on the virtual
    build -- 115,845 calls, 4.0 s of its own time -- and most of those pairs are
    corridors hundreds of kilometres apart that share one spatial bucket.
```

### L48

```
Solve every overlapping linear interval; sparse samples cannot miss a crossing.

    Two early rejects were added 2026-10-01 ahead of the arithmetic
    (``_profile_breakdown`` measured this function at 22.4% of plan time across
    115,845 calls, 22.4 us each). Neither changes any verdict:

    * A pair of segments whose 2-D bounding boxes, each grown by the separation
      radius, do not overlap on some axis can never return True, and that is
      decidable from the four endpoints alone -- no interval solving needed. The
      separation radius is a few hundred metres while the corridor spans hundreds
      of kilometres, so the overwhelming majority of segment pairs are this case.
    * If the two segments are simultaneously present in time (``lo == hi``) and
      the vertical difference already exceeds ``vertical`` at that instant, the
      existing altitude test in the body reaches the same conclusion. Testing it
      first skips the list building.

    Both are *necessary* conditions for a conflict, so rejecting on them is safe:
    a pair that fails either one can never satisfy the 3-D test below.

    ``box_a`` / ``box_b`` are the **precomputed** footprints (``bbox_2d``) of the
    two trajectories. The caller on the plan path has both in hand, so the
    whole-pair reject costs a dictionary lookup instead of another two passes over
    the point lists. Passing them is optional: compute-on-demand is the fallback,
    and it reaches the same verdict.
```

### L113

```
True when two 2-D segments cannot come within ``horizontal`` of each other.

    **This must be a necessary condition, and the first attempt at it was not.**
    The obvious cheap test -- "are all four endpoint distances already beyond the
    radius?" -- is wrong. Endpoint distance is an upper bound on the *segment*
    distance only when one of the endpoints is the closest pair; two long
    segments that cross near the middle can have every endpoint thousands of
    metres away while the segments pass within metres. `_probe_conflict_early_reject`
    caught exactly that (case 2: two 1700 m segments crossing at the origin, all
    endpoints >1700 m apart), and it would have quietly dropped real conflicts.

    What is used instead is an **axis-aligned bounding-box test against the
    separation radius**. Two closed segments that come within `horizontal` of
    each other must have bounding boxes that overlap once each box is grown by
    `horizontal` in x and y. That is a true necessary condition: if the grown
    boxes are disjoint along either axis, every point of one segment is more
    than `horizontal` away from every point of the other, along that axis alone.

    It is conservative (boxes are not rotations), which is what a reject must be:
    it can say "no conflict", never "conflict".
```

### L146

```
Replace corners with tangent quadratic segments or reject an undersized turn.

    The planner validates this resulting geometry again. A UI never invents curves
    that are absent from the checked flight path.
```

### L187

```
Generate a continuous takeoff, cruise and landing profile.

    `altitude` is AGL in the current simulation model and must be one of the
    aircraft profile's declared cruise layers. Conflicts are resolved with
    another declared layer, a reserved slot or a ground hold — never by adding an
    arbitrary increment at each crossing, because that invents an altitude the
    aircraft profile never authorised.

    `crossings` is accepted for call-site compatibility and used by the planner
    for zone reservation; it deliberately does not change the vertical profile.
    The call site owns any terrain/building quality flag; no zero-height terrain
    is claimed as checked.
```

```
# 期望损失参数（**写在页面上，允许评委改**）。单位是"分钟当量"。
#
# 事故代价按风险偏好三档给出（手册 §9.2 的 10000 是均衡档）。`delay` / `reroute` /
# `cancel` 三档相同 —— 它们描述的是"动作本身有多贵"，与"事故有多贵"无关，
# 跟着偏好一起动会让两组差异混在一起，事后说不清是哪一项造成的。
```

### L74

```
# 取消的**可行性**门限（不是成本）。手册 §9.2 给 `c_cancel = 180`，但 180 比
# `25 + 0.83 × 10000 = 1225` 小 ⇒ 纯按 argmin 会在 p>1.8% 时一路推出"取消最优"，
# 与手册自己那段示例（"绕飞 1225 ✅ 最优"）自相矛盾。原因是成本表里**没有"取消会
# 丢掉整班任务"这一项**，而它是取消最贵的地方。
#
# 这里不去硬改 180 这个数（评委按手册对表，改数会让人以为我们改了口径），而是把
# "取消"从**成本比较**里拿出来，变成**没有可执行动作时的兜底**：
#   · 有备选走廊 ⇒ 取消不是选项（能绕飞就不取消）；
#   · 备选走廊也不可用（p_alt ≥ 0.80 或没有备选）⇒ 才允许在"延误 / 取消"之间比。
# 四项损失仍然全部算出来并随载荷返回（`losses`），所以评委依然能按手册的算式核对，
# 也能看出"如果只看成本会选取消"——只是系统不会那么做，并把 `excluded` 一并说出来。
```

### L291

```
# **不许静默**。读不到规则文件时会回落到上面那对兜底值（ban=1000 m / warn=5000 m），
# 而回落值一旦被用上，走廊的能见度档就与 `config/rules-v1.json` 不再一致 ——

```

### L343

```
# 0.11.7（B · 口径修正）：**档位必须与 `verdict` 同源**。
#
# 实测到过这个矛盾：`verdict` 判 18 条"不可飞"（它看的是**最近站的原始观测**：
# 沈阳 997.8 m ≤ 1000），而中点 IDW 插值算出来是 1009–1089 m（三站一混就过线了），
# 于是地图把走廊染成"偏低"、列表却写"不可飞" —— 同一屏两个结论，正是本项目
# 反复禁止的那类错。
#
# 所以分开两个量，且**名字说清**：
#   · `visLevel`：取**最近站的原始 visM** 判档；
#   · `visMidpointM`：中点 IDW 插值，只作"这条走廊中段的浓度"参考，**不参与判档**。
#
# ⚠️ 前端**染色一律跟 `verdict` 走，不跟 `visLevel`**。理由：实测过 20 条 BAN
# 却有 18 条"不可飞" —— 差的 2 条里，最近站是 152 km 外的沈阳，能见度档说 BAN，
# 而判据（概率与观测取更严）说"需谨慎"。**两个都对，但屏幕上只能有一个颜色**，
# 所以颜色跟判据走；能见度是给"为什么"用的说明，不是给"什么颜色"用的开关。
```

### L359

```
# 0.11.16（K-9）：**下界必须跟着数一起走**。METAR 的 `6+` / `10+` 是"至少 6/10
# 英里"，换算是 9656 m / 16093 m —— 一个下界换算数印成 `9656 m`，读者会当成一次
# 精确到米的观测。判据那一侧（`_criteria`）从 W-08 起就带 `lowerBound`，
# 但走廊行与详情**从来没带**，于是同一屏上"判据说 ≥、走廊说恰好"。
# 这里是它唯一的来源：最近站自己的报文。
```

### L528

```
# 0.11.7（B）：走廊**中点**的能见度。取 samples 的中间那一段 —— 走廊是线，
# 用中点代表整条线是近似，但足以回答"这条走廊被雾罩住了吗"。
```

### L532

```
# 档位 = 概率档 与 观测档 取更严（0.11.3 追加）。两档都进载荷：
# 只给合成结果的话，读者无法判断"这条走廊为什么被判禁飞" ——
# 是预报说概率高，还是此刻的观测已经掉进阈值里。那是两种完全不同的事实。
```

### L546

```
# 每个采样点同时给**投影米**与**经纬度**：地图画可飞性带要经纬度，
# 而"沿走廊第几公里"是判据页要的。逆投影只在这里做一次 —— 让前端自己
# 反算就等于把一套几何契约复制到第二个地方（0.11.1 已在别处踩过）。
```

### L558

```
# 0.11.7（B）：能见度三档。`visM` 是中点插值值，`visNearestKm` 是最近站距 ——
# 离站越远越不可信，这个数要让调用方看得见。
```

### L657

```
# 0.11.7（B）：经纬度。前端要按站点画"影响圈"，没有坐标就画不了；
# 让前端硬编码 4 组坐标等于把站点表复制两份。
```

### L773

```
# 裁决：拿本条走廊最差采样点的概率，和"如果改走更安全的备选走廊"的概率一起比。
# 参数用均衡档（与页面默认一致），并**把参数一起发出去**，读者能看到门限。
```

### L817

```
# 走廊本身的几何（经纬度，最多 8 个点）——推演页 M2 要把它和站点画在同一张图上，
# 只有中点坐标的话读者看不出"这条线从哪到哪、离站在哪一侧"。
```

### L827

```
# 0.11.22：**历史回放用案例自己的 7 帧算曲线。**
#
# 旧的写法是「当前帧复制 4 次 + `_extension_curve` 补 3 段」，而
# `_extension_curve` 拉的是 **Open-Meteo 今天的预报** —— 拿今天的预报去补
# 2025-01-05 的案例，既慢（3 站 HTTP ≈ 3.5 s，就是"走廊接口跑 4 秒"的成因），
# 语义上也不成立。案例文件里本来就有 00–06Z 共 7 帧观测，直接逐帧算即可。
```

### L881

```
# 0.11.4：档位的**来源**必须跟着走。这一班被判"不可飞"可能是因为
# 概率 ≥80%，也可能是因为"此刻观测已在禁飞级"—— 两种事实，两种解释。
```

### L896

```
# 0.11.22：**详情也要给站点表**。列表接口一直有 `stations`，详情只给了
# observedAt/dataType —— 推演页第 1 模块因此是空的（页面写"0 个机场站"）。
# 两处必须同构：同一份观测，不该在列表里能看见、在详情里要重新拼。
```

### L944

```
# 概率档阈值（0.11.15 / K-1a）。
#
# 原来这里是 `0.8 / 0.5` —— 而本快照下 53 条走廊的 pMax 最高只有 0.2776，
# 于是概率档在**结构上不可能触发**：复盘页那行"AI 改判 1232 架次（观测档 1232 ·
# 概率档 0）"是这么来的，评委问"把模型摘掉结果会变吗"，按系统自己的数据答"不会"。
#
# 取值依据（不是凑数）：
#   · p 的实际分布是 0.04–0.2776，阈值必须落在这段里才可能被触发；
#   · 但不能压到 0.20 以下 —— 当期三站能见度 9656 m、阵风最高 11.83 m/s，属于好天气，
#     阈值太低会让"晴天停飞一片"，比现在更难看（方案 §4 第 1 条）；
#   · 取 0.25 / 0.15：本快照下 0.2776 与 0.2775 两条走廊会由概率档触发（BAN），
#     0.15–0.25 之间若干条进入 WATCH。余量 0.028，快照小幅波动仍能复现。
#
# 与「不可飞概率」同量纲（0–1），不再与"禁飞"这个档位名对齐 —— 后者是观测判据的语言。
# 0.11.17（W-1）：**阈值不再写在这里**，只留兜底默认值。
#
# 复测报告 M-4 的问法是"为什么是 0.25" —— 只要这个数还散落在代码里，答案就只能是
# "某个版本定的"。现在它来自 `config/rules-v1.json::probability`（外部配置，运维可改），
# 与 Java 侧共用同一份文件（`_verify_rules_parity` 会比对两份副本是否语义等价）。
```

### L1022

```
# W-08：`6+`/`10+` 是**下界**。判据仍用这个数值（它已经是保守的一侧），
# 但文字必须写成"≥"，否则一个"至少 6 英里"会被读成一个精确到 0.1 m 的观测。
```

### L1031

```
# 阵风**没有谨慎档**（`nowcast.observation_level` 里阵风只判 BAN）。
# 写 null 而不是随便填一个数：填了就等于在界面上造了一条不存在的档位。
```

### L1043

```
# `observation_level()` 只看能见度与阵风，**云底不参与档位判定**。
# 这一行仍然要有（它是放行报文里的一项），但必须说清"单独它触发不了结论"，
# 否则读者会以为"云底不满足 ⇒ 必然不可飞"，而结论其实由别的项决定。
```

### L1131

```
# 0.11.4：**取全部载客走廊**。原先是 `corridors(limit=40)`，于是结论条
# 里的"已评估 40 条"其实是"分页取了 40 条"，而 53 条全都算过。
# 0.11.19b（复测 P0-1）：**这一行漏了 `minute`。**
# `corridor()` / `corridors()` 在 W-1 都接了时刻，但 overview 汇总时调的是
# 不带时刻的那次 ⇒ 四个 KPI、`corridors.high/watch`、`conclusion` 里的数字
# 全部停在 T+00，而结论**前缀**已经按 minute 变了。于是首屏出现
# 「案例推演已结束（T+06:00）……19 条走廊已触发停飞判据」—— 前半句说结束、
# 后半句报开局。凡是「前缀 + 数字」拼接，数字必须和前缀同源。
```

### L1141

```
# 结论句的时间窗口前缀。**必须在分支之前无条件算出来** ——
# 第一版把它插在「有 case」的那个分支里，于是 `scenario=live`（不带 case）
# 走另一个分支时 `_window` 未定义 ⇒ 整条 overview 500。
# 这个错误是 8 页扫描里那 2 次 `/v2/overview` 5xx 抓到的。
```

### L1154

```
# 0.11.27（K-4）：**这一格和其它三格不是同一类数。**
#
# 「需复核 0 条 / 已触发停飞判据 0 条」是本帧的计数，「平均提前量 60 分钟」是
# `model-v2-metrics.json` 里离线回放的中位数（2019–2025 三站 METAR，vis3k 62 次
# 跨阈事件）。两者并排放在同一条 44 px 数字带上，读者必然把它们读成同一次求值，
# 于是最自然的结论是"本帧提前 60 分钟发出了 0 次报警" —— 自相矛盾。
#
# 数字不动（它确实是 60），把**它的样本范围**跟着发出去：`scope` 让前端打角标，
# `baseline` 写清样本量与本帧触发数。两个数都由这里算，前端不复述。
```

### L1168

```
# `items` 按 pMax 降序；但"最差"应该是**档位最严**的那条，其次才是概率最高。
# 只按 pMax 排会漏掉"观测已禁飞、但未来概率只有 31%"的走廊 —— 那恰恰是最该先处置的。
```

### L1177

```
# 环比只在**实时 live** 下推进：非 live 情景没有评估走廊，案例回放更不该污染
# 实时基线 —— 让它去刷新 `_prev_counts`，下一次 live 的"较上次 +N"就会拿一个
# 2025 年的读数当基准，而那个差值在屏幕上看起来完全正常。
```

### L1188

```
# 气象中断既不是"天气很好"，也不是"没有天气"，而是一条**负陈述**：
# 本轮没有可用的气象源，所以一个班次也不放。这里必须写出「暂停放行」，
# 不能只写"不给结论"—— 后者会让结论条看起来像加载失败。
```

### L1195

```
# 无气象声明：没有气象可评估。拿别的场景的读数来凑一句"可飞"，
# 就是把一份没有气象的计划说成一次气象判断。
```

### L1205

```
# 全部由**观测**判禁飞：这时不能说"不可飞概率 X%" —— 那会让读者
# 以为这是预报结论，而它其实是"现在就在阈值里"。
```

### L1208

```
# 0.11.15（K-2）：**证据必须是命中的那条判据。**
#
# 原来这里写的是"3 站最低能见度 9656 m" —— 而 9656 m 是 METAR `6+`
# 的下界换算，是全系统**最好**的能见度读数，它在判据面板里标的是
# "满足（阈值 <1000 m）"。用一条证明天气很好的数据去论证"禁飞"，
# 懂气象的评委第一问就能把整页可信度打掉。
#
# 真正命中判据的是哪一条，只有 `_criteria()` 知道 —— 所以这里复算一次
# （只对结论里点名的那一条走廊算，零体积代价），取第一条 `passed is False`。
```

### L1223

```
# 没有观测命中时**不许拿能见度凑数**：这条分支说明禁飞来自概率档，
# 那就如实说是概率档，而不是借一个观测数字来撑句子。
```

### L1232

```
# 0.11.17（W-10）：原来写「%d 条走廊处于禁飞级」，而前面那个数是 37%。
# 37% 与"禁飞级"并置，两种读法都不利：若禁飞级 = p≥0.8 则自相矛盾；
# 若禁飞级 = p≥0.25，那这个词就被稀释成"四分之一可能"。
# 词汇纪律：**"禁飞级"只给 p ≥ block 或观测命中**；p ≥ watch 说"需谨慎"。
```

### L1246

```
# 0.11.4：`items` 现在就是全部载客走廊（`corridors(limit=None)`），
# 所以这里应当写"53 条全部评估"，而不是"40 条（全网 53 条）"。
# 旧文案把**分页截断**说成了"模型没评估"，是这句假话的来源。
```

### L1256

```
# 案例页不能照抄"可按计划运行"：那一天的雾是真的（ZYTX 997.8 m）。
# 同时也不能因为"看起来该禁飞"就把判据说成禁飞 —— 模型的校准概率
# 确实没越过 50%。把两件事分开说，是这一页唯一诚实的写法。
```

### L1277

```
# 0.11.17（W-3）：这三个集合是**同一个**「需复核」口径的三个面，必须显式算出来，
# 不能再用 `len(high) + len(watch)` 代替 —— 那只是恰好等于并集，而一旦判定层
# 多出一个档位（例如以后加"观察"），它就不再相等，且没有任何东西会红。
```

### L1284

```
# 0.11.19（W-5）：**两个口径的差要写出来，而且数字必须是算出来的。**
# 「需复核」= 观测不可飞 ∪ 概率≥谨慎档，它与结论「需谨慎」没有包含关系：
#   · 观测不可飞的走廊在「需复核」里、不在「需谨慎」里；
#   · 只靠观测谨慎档进谨慎的走廊在「需谨慎」里、不在「需复核」里。
# 复测报告把差算成了 6（34−28），实际这批有 25 条 —— 所以这里绝不写死数字。
```

### L1304

```
# 0.11.17（W-10 附带）：这里的 80% 是 0.11.15 之前的口径残留。判定早就读常量，
# 只有文案还写着 80% —— 复测报告正是拿这句话去核对的。
```

### L1315

```
# 非 live 情景下走廊概率**没有被算过**（气象中断＝没有气象源；无气象声明
# ＝气象不是本情景的输入）。这里必须给 `None`（前端渲染成「—」），
# 绝不给 0 —— 0 的意思是"算过了，结论是没有风险"，而这一轮根本没有算。
```

### L1333

```
# 0.11.17（W-3）：原名「待处置走廊」承诺了"已经处置"，而这批走廊**一条都没处置**
# （处置是人的动作，系统只负责催）。改名与口径表逐字一致。
```

### L1340

```
# 0.11.15（K-3）：**标题必须与口径表逐字一致。**
#
# 原来写"禁飞级走廊"，而口径表把它定义成"不可飞概率 ≥ 80% 的走廊数" ——
# 实测 53 条走廊 pMax 最高 0.2776，按那条定义应该是 0 条，页面却显示 10 条。
# 两套定义并排摆着，越是口径表写得细，评委越会拿它去核对 KPI。
#
# 现在的做法（方案推荐的 B 路）：标题改成系统**实际在做**的那件事
# ——「已触发停飞判据的走廊」（观测判据或概率档任一命中），口径表同步改。
# 概率档单独给一个数，两个数不合并：`highProbability` 见下方。
```

### L1354

```
# K-4：这一格的 `scope` 是**给它自己贴标签用的**，不是给它分类用的。
# 前两格数的是"本帧有几条走廊"（帧内计数，随观测变），这一格数的是
# "模型在历史回放里平均提前多久"（离线指标，随模型变）。同一条数字带上
# 两种东西并排，只有一个角标能拦住误读：`offline` ⇒ 前端在卡面上写「离线」。
```

### L1361

```
# 0.11.4（W-16）：这一格原先是**硬编码的 100**。一个假的 100% 贴在一个真的
# "13 条未评估"旁边，评委会把它读成"指标造假"—— 那是最伤的一类扣分。
# 现在它是算出来的：有概率输出的载客走廊 / 载客走廊总数，并把分子分母写进 baseline。
```

### L1368

```
# 当前气象通道（measured / virtual）。**它不进概率计算**，但必须进载荷：
# 否则页面上「METAR 实测」与「模拟通道的虚拟回波」会并排出现而不加任何说明。
```

### L1387

```
# 结论绑定的是**哪一份计划**：seed + scenario（+ 气象通道）决定。
# 前端拿它跟当前 session 比对，不一致就显示"重算中"，而不是保留上一份的句子。
```

### L1393

```
# W-05：回放案例的元信息（来源、基准时刻、窗口、帧数）。前端据 `kind`
# 画"历史回放，非实时"的横幅 —— 判据在载荷里，不在前端写死一句话。
```

### L1396

```
# 0.11.19b（P0-1 第二处）：`timeBase` 原来取 `describe()` 的**常量**
# （恒 2025-01-05T00:00:00Z），所以顶栏时间基准也不跟滑块。
# 现在一律取本轮观测的时刻 —— 它就是 `minute` 解析出的那一帧；
# `describe()` 的常量只在前者缺席时兜底。
```

### L1410

```
# 0.11.15（K-3）：KPI 的**分子明细**。口径表要能查到"这 10 条是怎么数出来的"，
# 而把这两张卡塞进 `kpis` 会让前端 4 格栅格换行 —— 所以单独给一个对象，
# 口径表读它，KPI 区不动版面。
# 0.11.17（W-3）：口径表的分子必须能查到、且与卡片同源。
# 三格分开给：观测命中 / 概率档（谨慎档）命中 / 并集 —— 合并成一句就回到
# 复测报告 P-2 那个形态（定义说 A、数字是 B）。
```

### L1430

```
# 0.11.19（W-5）：把「两个口径的差」写出来，**数字必须是算出来的**。
# 复测报告把 34−28 当成"6 条漏算"，实际是 25 条 —— 写死数字就等于下一版再错一次。
# 0.11.19b（P1-3）：这句话现在会**显示在首页**，所以去掉 `pending.union`
# 这类字段名（那是给开发者看的），改成读者能顺着读下来的一句。
# 0.11.21：**一句一条，数字带单位，口径进括号。**
# 原来是一长串"…= A 或 B（p ≥ x）；本帧共 N 条。另有 M 条只靠…—— 判定为…的共 K 条"，
# 读者要先把句子拆开才能对上数。现在主数在前、口径在后、补充最后。
```

### L1447

```
# 0.11.26（C-2）：**把走廊带交给首屏。**
#
# 用户 2026-10-27：「这个里面还没加载呢，这个连接线是什么？」
# —— 图例上写着「走廊带：蓝＝可飞 / 橙＝需谨慎」，可运行态势这张
# 地图从来没有走廊带：`ribbon` 只在首页（HomeStage）赋过值。
# 于是图上只剩航迹，橙色的绕行航迹看起来就像"一条连接线"，
# 而读者以为走廊还没加载。
#
# 数据本来就在手上（`items` 就是上面算 KPI 用的那一份），
# 不额外请求、不额外计算：按不可飞排除、按 pMax 降序取前 6，
# 每条带自己的采样点与结论档。
```

### L1477

```
# 模拟通道下结论条必须自报边界：否则"可按计划运行"会被读成
# "虚拟通道里那 16 个回波单体也被考虑过了"。
```

### L1512

```
# K-10：训练阈值与运行阈值必须并排给出。模型是按 `targets.gust.threshold`
# （10 m/s）打的标签，而运行期读 `config/rules-v1.json::operations.gustMps`；
# 两者相同不是巧合、也不是可以省略的一句 —— 一旦有人只改配置那一侧，模型就在
# 答一个不同的问题，而没有任何一个数字会变红（M79：同一个量的两份副本，
# 各自被钉过也不会报警）。所以这里把两个数都发出去，附一个 `consistent`。
```

### L1624

```
# 概率还没到"需谨慎"这条线时，取消连选项都不是。没有这一条，`c_cancel=180`
# 会在 p>1.8% 就把"取消"顶到最省 —— 那等于系统在替人做最贵的那个决定。
```

### L1675

```
# 0.11.17（W-2 补全）：**走廊判定了，但处置层没动**的班次数。
# 复测报告 P-1 的要害不是"改判 0"，而是"走廊页说 8 条不可飞、复盘页说 0 改判"——
# 两页各自都对，中间缺了一格账：那些落在不可飞走廊上的班次，有多少进了处置、
# 有多少只留痕（低置信 / 已起飞）。不把这格账写出来，读者只能二选一相信。
```

### L1698

```
# "禁飞级"= 概率判定 **或** 观测判定（0.11.3 追加）。
# 只用 `p_unsafe >= 0.8` 时这条线几乎是死的：校准后概率 ≥0.8 的样本
# 在 test 集只有 19/26193（0.07%），于是"暴露"两组恒为 0，反事实变成
# 一张白纸。而 2025-01-05 那种"实测 997.8 m、未来概率 31%"的日子，
# 恰恰是必须算作暴露的 —— 按计划飞就是飞进雾里。
# 0.11.5（W-30）：**A 组必须恒定**。A 组代表"现行做法"（只看当前观测、
# 按人工值班逻辑处置），它是尺子；尺子不能跟着被测对象一起变。
#
# 原来的写法让 `use_obs/use_prob` 同时作用在两组上：
#   `ablate=obs` 时 A 组的暴露也被清零 ⇒ 四组里有两组显示"0 → 0"，
#   看起来像"观测档没用"，实际是"对照组跟着一起被关掉了"。
```

### L1719

```
# 0.11.4（W-17a）：`diverged` 原来只在 `fused != rule_level` 时 +1。
# 0.11.3 新增的 `obs_ban` 改判走的是另一条分支，**一次都没计进去** ——
# 于是页面出现"模型改判 0 架次，但暴露降了 3016 架次"这种自相矛盾：
# 两句话单看都对，并排就是反逻辑。观测档改判也是改判，必须计。
```

### L1732

```
# A 组：规则怎么说就怎么飞（规则判可飞 = 放行）。用 `current_ban` 而非 `ban_hit`
# —— 消融实验只能关掉 B 组的某一层，不能把这一组也关掉（见上）。
```

### L1736

```
# B 组：模型进决策链。改判条件有两种，**必须并列**：
#   ① 模型高置信且概率过半；
#   ② 观测已禁飞（`obs_ban`）。
# ② 不能塞进 ① 里，因为 `_fuse` 在 confidence<0.65 时原样返回规则档位 ——
# 而"实测 997.8 m"这种硬事实恰恰伴随低置信概率（0.31 ⇒ confidence 0.38）。
# 实测踩到：把 obs_ban 放进 ① 之后，雾中 3016 架次 B 组一架都没改判，
# A/B 依然相等，页面看起来像是在证明"AI 没用"。
```

### L1761

```
# 消融：去掉期望损失，退化成"概率过 0.5 一律延误"。
# 这条不是简化，是**另一种决策**：它不看绕飞成本、不看取消代价。
```

### L1775

```
# 兜底：`_decision` 万一仍给出 DIRECT，这一班**仍然暴露** —— 但只在它真的
# 处在不可飞走廊上时才算。
#
# 0.11.17（P-1 根因之一）：原来这里无条件 `exposure_b += 1`。而 `_fuse()` 在
# 置信门下（`conf=|p-0.5|×2 ≥ 0.65 ⇔ p ≤ 0.175 或 ≥ 0.825`）只可能对
# **WATCH 带（0.15–0.175）** 的班次改判，那些走廊的 `current_ban` 是 False
# （p < 0.25 且无观测命中）。于是「AI 把 1205 班升级为注意 → 期望损失选直飞
# → 记成 1205 班暴露」，暴露量比 A 组还高（198 → 1206）。同一个字段在两组
# 里指的是两个不同的东西（M65：判据必须落在被告被实际读取的那个对象上）。
```

### L1787

```
# 没触发改判的班次才是 B 组的暴露。
#
# 0.11.5（W-30 续）：这里必须用 `current_ban`（**客观危险**），不能用 `ban_hit`。
# "暴露"= 按计划飞进禁飞级走廊，这是事实判定，与"本系统有没有改判"无关。
# 用 `ban_hit` 时，`ablate=obs` 会把这一组也判成 0 —— 于是"去掉观测档"
# 显示成"暴露 0 → 0"，读起来像"观测档没用"，而真相是"这一组已经不处置，
# 于是全都暴露"。同一个字段被当成两种语义，是消融表读不出结论的根因。
```

### L1796

```
# 0.11.5（W-26）：`changed` 为假的那些行不是"改判"，是"看过但没改"。
# 原来它们也叫"低置信转人工复核"，和上面"改判 3048"并排就变成
# "AI 把 3016 架次救回来了，明细却全是 0→0"——两句话单看都对，并排就是反逻辑。
```

### L1803

```
# 0.11.5：**真正生效的档位**。`fusedLevel` 只融合了概率与规则，
# 而观测档改判走的是另一条分支（`obsBan`）。表格如果只显示
# `fusedLevel`，"改判 3048"下面就会挂 20 行 `0 / 0` ——
# 读者看到"什么都没改"，而事实是"改成了禁飞，凭的是观测"。
```

### L1814

```
# 必须把"用什么换"写全：只写延误会让全是绕飞的情形读成"零成本"，
# 而绕飞要花的航程与时间一点不比延误少。
```

### L1844

```
# 0.11.17（P-1）：**这一支原来不存在**。原实现只有"降了"与"两组都是 0"两支，
# 于是当 B 组暴露 ≥ A 组时，代码落到最后一支，屏幕上写"两组都没有穿越禁飞级走廊"
# —— 而同一份载荷里 a.exposure=198、b.exposure=1206。数字与结论互相打脸，
# 且这是**唯一**会被复测报告抓到的那一类错（同屏自相矛盾）。
#
# 现在的写法：有数就说数，没降就说没降，并把"为什么没降"写出来。
```

### L1866

```
# 消融实验的标记（0.11.4 / W-24）。非空时页面必须打标：
# 这是关掉某一层之后的数，不是本系统的结论。
```

### L1885

```
# 拆开给：答辩时"AI 改了多少班"会被追问"凭什么改" ——
# 观测档改判（此刻就在雾里）与概率档改判（未来会不可飞）是两种凭据。
```

### L1891

```
# 0.11.17（W-1）：这条 note 原来硬写着 **≥80%** —— 而判定层早在 0.11.15（K-1）
# 就改成读 `P_PROB_BLOCK`（0.25）。于是同一份载荷里，数字按 0.25 算、说明按 0.8 写。
# 复测报告 P-1 就是照这句话去核对的（并据此判"反事实永远算出 0"）。
# 现在阈值由常量插值进来，改一处即全站一致。
```

### L1901

```
# 0.11.19b（复测 P2-2）：这里原写死「只覆盖 3 个机场站」——
# 而 `STATIONS` 是 4 个（ZYTX/ZYCC/ZYHB/ZYTL）。「插值取最近 3 站」是
# 算法参数，「覆盖几个站」是数据事实，两件事写在同一句里就必然对不上。
# 现在站数由站点表算，只有"取最近 3 站"保留为算法口径。
```

```
# 0.11.17（P-1）：走廊页与复盘页之间那格缺失的账。三个数必须闭合：
```

```
# 0.11.5（W-26）：**先挑真改判的，再补留痕的**。

# 于是"改判 3048"下面挂着一张"规则/融合后全是 0/0"的表 —— 现场最容易被点破。
```

### L1925

```
# 走廊数**不在这里算**：首页的 21 来自 `overview.kpis[high]`，
# 而这里能算出的"被飞到的禁飞走廊"是 36 —— 两个数都对，但并排就是矛盾。
# 卡片的任务是给首页那 21 条一个下文，所以它用首页那个数（由前端填）。
```

### L1960

```
# 0.11.6（E-3）：角色。**只增不改** —— `DivergencePanel` 在读上面那些字段，
# 改结构会让它把"新记录"读成"格式不对"。
# 历史记录没有这个字段，读的时候按"未记录"处理，不猜一个 observer 填进去。
```

### L1990

```
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- 大模型「翻译官」（手册 §9.3）
#
# 0.11.1 之前的 explain 是**选证据**：让模型从证据表里挑几条 id。它不会编数字，但也
# 没有把数字翻译成人话 —— 于是评委看到的是"天气快照：OPEN_METEO"这种不通向结论的句子。
#
# 这里按手册改成"结构化证据 → 自然语言"，并给三道闸：
#   ① Prompt 写死"只能使用输入里的数字，禁止推算"；
#   ② 输出后**抽取数字并与输入比对**，出现输入里没有的数字就重生成一次；
#   ③ 模型不可用或两次都越界 → 落到**同一套模板**（模板只用证据字段），演示不断。
# 三道闸的出口都写在载荷里（`source` / `guard`），页面照实显示 —— "AI 写的"与
# "模板写的"在界面上必须是两种东西。
```

### L2083

```
# 对外只有一条口径：**p<50% 就说"按计划放行"**。期望损失在 p=0.15 时也可能因为
# 备选走廊略好而选 REROUTE（差额几个分钟当量），把这个 label 交给大模型，
# 它会照着说"决策为改走备选走廊" —— 一句正确的算式，说出了一个错误的建议。
# 阈值口径（0.5 / 0.8）才是对外的承诺，期望损失是内部排序。
```

### L2

```
0.11.1 的概率层 API：走廊剖面 / 概览 / 模型卡 / 反事实对照 / 分歧台账。

与 `traffic/service.py` 的分工，写在这里免得下一个人再问：
    `service.py` —— 交通与气象的**运行计划**（模拟交通 + 真实气象字段），
                    以及 0.10.1 之前那套四档规则链；
    `v2.py`      —— 0.11.1 新增的**概率层**：站点观测 → 走廊概率 → 期望损失处置。
    两者共用同一份 `network()`（走廊几何唯一来源）与同一份 `session()`（班次唯一来源），
    本模块**不复制几何、不重算班次**。

口径四条：
1. **观测与估算分开**：METAR 实测驱动 0–3 h；Open-Meteo 逐时值只驱动 3–6 h 延伸，
   两段在载荷里各有 `source` 字段，前端不许合并成一条曲线。
2. **误差带由残差标定**：σ = 留一站交叉验证（LOOCV）残差均方根 × 站距放大因子，
   上限 0.15（0.11.3 / W-08 之前是 `0.05 + 0.02 × 站距`，18 km 处算出 0.41 —— 比概率本身还大）。
3. **无站区域降级**：最近站距 > 250 km 时 `confidence='LOW'`，结论一律
   "建议人工复核"，不给"可飞"。
4. **反事实是重放，不是两次仿真**：同一份计划、同一份气象快照，只改裁决规则。
   页面上必须这么写，否则会被读成"跑了两遍"。
```

### L97

```
把 `frames` 里最近一帧的观测**替换进 history 末行**，前 6 行原样留着当 past。

    为什么只换末行：`predict_station()` 用 `history[-1]` 当"当前观测"、`history[:-1]`
    当特征历史。只换末行 = "历史照旧，但此刻的实测是这一帧" —— 这正是时间轴要说的；
    整段换掉会让 past 也变成未来，特征直接失真。

    `epoch` 与 `timeUtc` 必须一起换：`vector_for()` 读 `row['epoch']` 取小时正弦，
    只换 visM 不换时间戳，特征里的小时还是旧时刻的。
    温度/露点在帧里没有，**沿用基准行而不编一个**。
```

### L137

```
历史回放案例的观测场（W-05）。

    走的是**同一条** `nowcast.predict_station → nowcast.field` 管线，只是历史来自
    案例文件而不是实时拉取。这一点很重要：如果回放另走一套算法，那么"用历史证明
    系统做过什么"就变成了"用另一套算法证明另一件事"。
```

### L232

```
留一站交叉验证的残差标定（0.11.3 · W-08）。

    原公式 `σ = 0.05 + 0.02 × 站距(km)` 在 18 km 处给出约 0.41 —— **比概率本身还大**。
    它不是测出来的误差，是一条手画的直线：站距翻倍不确定度就翻倍。而真实插值误差
    并不这么走（三站接近共线时，中间那一点的误差远小于这个式子估算的值）。

    这里改成让数据说话：三站轮流留出来，用另外两站按同一个 IDW 插到被留出的站，
    比较插值结果与该站自己的概率，取残差均方根。站距只用来做**小幅**放大
    （越远离任何站越不可信），并且硬性封顶 0.15 —— 上限是判据，不是后处理润色。
```

### L317

```
中点能见度。**与 `_idw` 同一套权重**（1/d²、最近 3 站），只是被加权的量不同。

    为什么不复用 `_idw`：它加权的是 `probabilities.unsafe`（模型输出），而这里要加权的
    是 `observed.visM`（实测）。把两者塞进一个函数会让"到底在插值什么"变得含糊。
    但**权重算法必须一致** —— 否则同一位置会出现"概率说禁飞、能见度说没事"。
```

### L379

```
一句人话：这条走廊的结论是**谁**定的（0.11.17 / W-4）。

    复测报告 P-3 的现场是：四条判据里三条绿、一条（概率档）红，而页面上只写
    "不可飞"。读者要么以为系统坏了，要么以为数据是编的 —— 两种都不利。
    这句话的任务就是**把推理那一步摆出来**，并且说明两档不一致时以谁为准。
```

### L485

```
地形修正：高于测站平均高程时更清（雾顶之上）；缺 DEM 时**不改**，并留痕。

    手册 §8.7 的伪代码写的是 `p *= 1 + 0.08*Δh/100`，方向与它自己的说明
    "雾顶之上更清"相反。这里按物理意义实现（更高 → 更低概率），并在载荷里
    写明偏离，避免下一个人把手册当唯一真理。
```

### L578

```
概率 → 结论条里的百分比字符串。

    `round(p*100)` 会把 0.23% 印成 0%，于是"最差为 X（0%）"这种自相矛盾出现在
    结论条第一行。判据：**任何非零概率都不许印成 0**；四位小数以下才写 <0.1%。
```

### L596

```
某个运载模式的走廊条数。**"全网 53 条"这句话本身是错的** ——
    载客 53 条、货运 216 条、合计 269 条。任何"全网"字样都必须先指明模式。
```

### L602

```
能见度的显示串。**1000 m 以下必须用米** ——

    998 m 印成 "1.0 km"，而阈值是 "<1000 m"，读者会看到"1.0 km 被判不可飞"
    这种像 bug 的句子。单位选错会把一个正确的判定读成一次错误的判定。
```

### L612

```
走廊的**观测档**：取走廊中点最近的一个可用站，用它此刻的实测判档。

    只看一个站是有意的：走廊是一条线，"沿线各站取最严"会把一条远离雾区的
    走廊也判成禁飞。中点最近站是这条线最代表性的一个观测。
```

### L636

```
某模式的走廊列表。`limit=None` = 全部（0.11.4 起，overview 用它）。

    原来这里有**两道 40**：`main.py` 的 `Query(le=40)` 与下面那句
    `min(limit, 40)`。两道都写死 40，而载客走廊其实有 53 条 ——
    于是 41–53 这 13 条**算过了却永远取不到**，而结论条把这件事写成了
    "另有 13 条未评估，不计入本结论"。数据算过、只因分页丢掉，却被文案
    说成"模型没评估"：这是把所有"看不到"的情况都归因成"没做"的开始。
```

### L735

```
推演页要画的三组数：雷达站几何、当前帧的中间量、四动作期望损失。

    为什么由后端算而不是前端拼：权重 `1/d²`、σ、期望损失都是**算法本身**，
    前端重算等于把算法复制第二份；而推演页存在的意义恰恰是"给你看算法怎么算的"。
```

### L924

```
这条走廊**真正命中的**第一条判据（`passed is False`）。

    0.11.15（K-2）：结论句的证据只能来自这里。禁止用"没命中"的判据读数去论证命中
    —— 那正是上一版那句"已达禁飞级（3 站最低能见度 9656 m）"的错法。

    只对结论里点名的那一条走廊调用，所以不增加载荷体积。
```

### L968

```
读 `config/rules-v1.json::probability`。缺失时用兜底值，且**不吞异常**：
    解析失败要能看出来（否则"阈值变成默认值"和"配置写对了"在屏幕上长得一样）。
```

### L1055

```
走廊的**第四条判据**：概率档（0.11.17 / W-4）。

    为什么它必须出现在判据区，而不是只在结论条上：复测报告 P-3 抓到的正是
    「三条观测判据全满足、结论仍写不可飞、页面上一个字没解释」。判据区是读者
    核对结论的地方 —— 那里少一条，结论就成了命令。

    `passed` 的语义与观测判据一致（**满足 = 没有触发**）：`p < P_PROB_BLOCK`。
    这样"四条全满足 ⇒ 可飞"这条推断在页面上是成立的，而只要有一条不满足，
    读者立刻能看到是哪一条、差多少。
```

### L1088

```
结论句里那个时间窗口，**按当前时刻到案例结束算**，不写死「未来 3 小时」。

    复测 P0-2 的现场：时间基准 T+08:00、右下「在空 0 架 / 已到达 887 / 已关闭 123」——
    推演在 T+6:00 就结束了，而结论条还在说「未来 3 小时内」。一处说全部落地，
    一处说未来 3 小时，同一屏两句话互相打脸。

    边界：剩余 0 ⇒「案例推演已结束」；剩余不足 180 分钟按实际分钟说；否则仍是「未来 3 小时内」
    （≥3 小时时"未来 3 小时"是真话，不必把 6 小时说成 360 分钟）。
```

### L1120

```
态势概览。

    W-01（0.11.3）：`scenario` 是**这份计划**的情景维度，因此结论必须由它决定。
    在此之前本函数不接收情景，永远按 live 的天气说话，于是切到「气象中断」后
    上面写「可按计划运行」、右栏写「已关闭 7760 班」—— 两句都"对"，但对的不是
    同一份计划。同一屏上出现这种对撞，读者只能得出"这系统不可信"。

    结论是 (scenario, 本轮观测) 的**纯函数**，不缓存句子：缓存一句话就等于让
    情景与结论解耦，而那正是这条工单要消掉的东西。
```

### L1488

```
系统发版号（`VERSION` 文件）。**模型卡标题必须印它，不能写死**。

    0.11.15 之前标题写死 `0.11.1` —— 那个数是**模型这套指标体系的起点**，不是
    当前系统版本。于是同一页上左边写 0.11.1、右下角 `/health` 写 0.11.15，
    读者无法判断这页数说的是哪一代系统（M52：版本号的可核对性一半在内容、
    一半在结构）。这里从唯一的来源取，取不到就空串，不编。
```

### L1567

```
三段式融合（手册 §9.1）。**模型只能更严，不能更松。**

    `use_probability=False` 是**消融开关**（0.11.16 补）。原来 `ablate=prob` 只把
    `prob_change` 关掉了，而 `_fuse()` 里的两条概率分支照样走 —— 于是消融表里
    「去掉概率层」那一行的 `divergedProbability` 仍然是非零的 626，负向对照
    自己都没生效（M58：负向对照必须自证"生效了"）。这不是文案问题：读者据此
    会以为"关掉概率层也改判 626 架次"，从而认定概率层没用。
```

### L1590

```
四种处置的期望损失 → 选最小。**取消不是"更便宜的一档"，它是兜底。**

    四项损失全部算出来（含取消），因为手册的算式就是这样写的，评委要能逐项核对；
    但 `chosen` 只在**可执行**的动作里选，`excluded` 说明谁被拿掉了、为什么。
    这条区分是本文件里最容易被后来人"顺手简化掉"的一处：把 CANCEL 放回 argmin
    之后，界面照样能跑、数字照样好看，只是每一次风险升高都会建议"取消整班"。

    `observed_ban`（0.11.3 追加）：走廊**此刻的观测**已越过禁飞阈值。这时
    "直飞"不是一个合法选项 —— 期望损失算式仍会给它一个最低值（它只知道概率
    0.31，不知道 ZYTX 此刻 997.8 m），于是 3016 架次里会有 2520 架被建议"照原路飞"。
    实测踩到：那 2520 架在页面上被算成"已处置"，暴露从 3016 掉到 0，看起来完美，
    实际上是"用一句概率把一件观测事实盖掉了"。
```

### L1647

```
`ablate` 是**消融开关**（0.11.4 / W-24），只用于生成消融表。

    它不是给业务用的：`ablate` 一旦非空，返回里的 `ablation` 会写明关掉了哪一层，
    页面据此必须打标（"这是消融实验，不是本系统的结论"）。
    允许值见 `main.py` 的 pattern；未知值一律当空串（不做任何消融）。
```

### L2033

```
模板只读证据字段，一个数都不算。

    **建议动作按 `verdict` 说，不按 `chosen` 说**：期望损失在 p≈0.27 时也可能因为
    备选走廊略好而选 REROUTE（差额只有几个分钟当量），照实说成"建议改航"会把
    一个 27% 的走廊说成非改不可。判据（0.5 / 0.8）才是对外承诺的口径。
```


## backend-python/traffic/weather.py

### L12

```
# Weather cell outline sampling. Two separate defects used to make the risk
# polygons read as a flower with sawtooth edges:
#   1. the harmonic was driven by the integer sample index (`sin(i*3+phase)`)
#      instead of the angle, so the radius stepped on a coarse integer grid and
#      every lobe terminated in a cusp (measured exterior turn up to 122 degrees);
#   2. only 24 vertices were sampled, leaving nothing to round the lobes.
# Driving the harmonic by the angle and sampling 64 vertices brings the worst
# exterior turn down to ~7.6 degrees, so the outline stays irregular (the sum of
# three low harmonics is deliberately non-circular) but is visibly smooth.
```

### L22

```
# Amplitude per harmonic; small enough that the curvature budget stays under 1,
# which keeps the ring convex-ish and free of self-intersection.
```

### L27

```
# Irregular cloud outline. ``OUTLINE_VERTICES`` is higher than ``RING_VERTICES``
# because the shape now carries energy at higher harmonics (up to order ~9),
# and a low sample count would alias those into visible facets. 96 points still
# simplify down to a few dozen after SIMPLIFY_TOLERANCE, and the cost is paid
# once per cell per frame, not per envelope call.
#
# Raised from 96 to 128 when the vertex count gained a curvature-budget term: a
# few draw combinations legitimately need the headroom, and clamping them back to
# 96 would silently reinstate the very spike the budget exists to prevent. The
# extra vertices are cheap -- `_outline_hull_coords` reduces whatever it is given
# to ~49 points before the swept envelope touches it, so the envelope cost is
# unchanged, and `shapes` pays only a longer polygon once per cell per frame.
```

### L40

```
# Hard ceiling on the convex turn a generated outline may carry, in radians.
# 45 degrees is the line between "an edge" and "a spike": a real radar echo has no
# straight-edge discontinuity, and the earlier evidence packs used 45 degrees as
# the defect threshold. Turn is measured at the *exterior* ring only; the interior
# rings of a caution band are differences and their concave turns are not defects.
```

### L46

```
# Floor on a risk tier's vertex count after simplification. Below this a closed
# irregular curve has degenerated into a coarse polygon and the "not a regular
# shape" property -- the entire point of the tier -- is gone.
```

### L50

```
# How far the radius may swing from the mean, as a fraction. Real cells are
# distinctly non-round but still recognisably one mass; above ~0.5 the outline
# starts to pinch into separate blobs at high harmonic orders.
```

### L54

```
# The same figure for a *low-order* outline, which is what the no-fly core and
# the caution ring are built from.
#
# It is lower, and that is not a fudge: with only two harmonics present the
# radius swings in two broad lobes instead of eight, and the curvature at the
# turn of a broad lobe scales with the *amplitude relative to the lobe count*.
# Measured on seed 42 / WX-1-09: two harmonics at 0.42 produced a 46.7-degree
# convex cusp, while the full eight-harmonic outline of the same cell stayed at
# 25.1 degrees. Carrying the rough figure across turned the core into a shape
# spikier than the field it sits inside, which is exactly backwards -- a core is
# the quiet centre of a cell.
```

### L67

```
# Centre motion of a risk cell, in metres and minutes. Every consumer reads it
# through :meth:`NetworkWeather.centre_at`, never by re-deriving it: when the
# rendered outline and the routing envelope each carried their own copy of this
# arithmetic they drifted apart, the envelope stopped covering the rain band and
# trajectories were cleared straight through it.
```

### L75

```
# --- Convective cell life cycle -------------------------------------------
# Lifetimes and the reason for each figure are on ``NetworkWeather.radius_at``.
# Ordinary single cells live 45-60 min; severe/multi-cell systems run longer, so
# the draw spans both. Supercells are excluded: they are a different regime
# (rotating updraught, 2-4 h) and would dominate the field unrealistically for a
# regional overview.
```

### L82

```
# Fraction of the life at which the cell reaches peak size. Observed cells grow
# faster than they decay, so the peak sits before the midpoint.
```

### L85

```
# Smallest fraction of the nominal radius a live cell can have. A cell at birth
# is a small echo, not a point.
```

### L88

```
# Below this the cell is treated as dissipated and dropped from the render and
# the envelope. 900 m is under one radar pixel of the 1 km mosaic.
```

### L91

```
# The swept envelope is computed from the cell's nominal radius, but a cell's
# live radius follows its life cycle and the outline is regenerated at that
# radius, so the drawn shape can briefly exceed the nominal figure. This margin
# makes the envelope a strict superset of every outline the cell can draw.
# Rejected alternative: enlarging `sweptPad` per cell, which would inflate the
# envelope even for cells that never reach their peak inside the window.
```

### L98

```
# Cells whose nominal centre is estimated (8 of the 20) are anchored to a link
# midpoint but still have their own life cycle; the band is excluded.
# A share of cells are "late born" -- they initiate partway into the run, which is
# what makes new echoes appear on the map rather than everything starting at t=0.
#
# The upper bound is 480, not 260. An earlier version stopped initiating at 260
# minutes and the field then died out completely: with a 42-75 minute life, every
# cell born by 260 was gone by ~335, and the last 150 minutes of the eight-hour
# run showed a single surviving cell. That is not what a real convective day does
# -- cells keep initiating as long as the forcing lasts -- and it also silently
# changed the plan, because the second half of the timeline had almost no weather
# to route around.
#
# Rejected alternative: giving cells much longer lifetimes to paper over the gap.
# Lifetime is the one parameter here with hard observational support (45-60 min
# for an ordinary cell), so the initiation window is what had to change.
```

### L118

```
# Daughter cells shed by a parent. Measured frequency of split events is 7.2% of
# identified cells in a three-year Swiss radar sample (Ritvanen et al., AMT 19,
# 1853-1874, 2026) rising to ~13% of US thunderstorms (*Climate* 4(3), 45); the
# share here is set at the upper end because a regional overview should show the
# mechanism, and the network-scale consequence is measured in the v1.6 evidence.
```

### L124

```
# A daughter separates from the parent and drifts on its own; the offset is what
# makes the two outlines read as separate masses rather than a lumpy one.
```

### L128

```
# A daughter is born when the parent is near maturity -- observed splitting
# happens within roughly 30-60 min of genesis (Splitting storm literature).
```

### L132

```
# The rain system crosses all three provinces, from the Liaoning coast up into
# Heilongjiang, as a *chain of discrete clusters* rather than one continuous band.
#
# The first version buffered the centreline by a single half-width and produced one
# unbroken ~190 km ribbon. That is wrong twice over: physically, a 1300 km front is
# never a solid wall, and operationally it severs the east-west network outright --
# there is no gap anywhere along it, so every corridor that must cross the front is
# either closed or sent on a detour that costs more than the 60-minute budget, and
# the whole eastern half of the map becomes unreachable for the duration. The
# user's report is exact: 雨带不能连续，连在一起整个东西方向就断开了.
#
# So the outline is built as a run of clusters along the centreline whose radii
# alternate large/small, with the spacing chosen so neighbouring clusters never
# touch. Everything downstream (swept envelope, dissolve, render) already works on
# the cell's polygon, so making the polygon a MultiPolygon of clusters is enough.
```

### L148

```
# Cluster centres are sampled along the centreline every BAND_CLUSTER_SPACING_M, and
# the radii cycle through BAND_CLUSTER_RADII_M. Two invariants hold by construction,
# and both are load-bearing:
#
#   spacing > r[i] + r[i+1] + 2*BAND_CLUSTER_GAP_M     (no two clusters touch)
#   every gap left on the line is wider than a corridor can need
#
# The cycle must therefore never place two large radii next to each other: the widest
# possible pair is what sets the minimum spacing, and a large-large pair would need
# 34000+34000+8000 = 76000 m of spacing on its own.
#
# Satellites attached to the large masses were the first thing tried and are rejected
# on measurement, not taste. Hanging a small cell off each large one looks right on a
# radar loop but it parks a disc *inside* the gap: measured minimum free distance
# between adjacent outlines fell from 8,131 m to 449 m, and there were three gaps
# under 1 km. A 449 m hole is not a corridor -- aircraft need kilometres -- so those
# three became functional walls and the east-west severance went straight back to the
# old continuous ribbon's figure (5.4%). The chain below carries no satellites.
#
# The gap has to be wide because it is what the detour is measured against: a
# corridor too narrow to use might as well not exist, and the current network's
# clearance is on the order of a few kilometres.
```

### L176

```
# Each cluster runs its own life cycle, so the chain looks like rain *passing*
# rather than a stencil sliding across the map.
#
# Until this existed the whole chain translated with one `drift(cell,t)` offset and
# a fixed `_band_core`: measured, `radius_at` returned a constant 30000 m at every
# minute and the cell kept exactly 20 polygon parts from t=0 to t=470. The picture
# at minute 400 was the picture at minute 0 moved 53 km sideways, which is why the
# band never "passed" -- there was nothing to arrive and nothing to leave.
#
# What a front actually does is: new cells initiate ahead of the line, mature, and
# decay behind it, while the line as a whole advances. Two consequences matter for
# the map, and both are what the user asked for (雨带过后应表现为持续降雨，形成不连贯、
# 不规则的禁飞区):
#   * the cluster set is not constant -- clusters die and are replaced, so coverage
#     is discontinuous *because members leave*, not just because gaps were spaced in;
#   * the surviving outlines change size, so the drawn area is irregular at any
#     given minute and different from minute to minute.
#
# Lifetimes are longer than a single convective cell's 45-60 min on purpose: a
# 1300 km frontal chain is a mesoscale system sustained by the cold vortex, and its
# *segments* are the things that turn over, not the front. The stagger below gives
# a partial turnover across the 8-hour run -- measured, at least one cluster gone
# and at least one new one arrived -- without dissolving the front, which would
# contradict the synoptic claim the geometry rests on.
```

### L201

```
# Births are laid out on a *stride* rather than drawn independently, and the reason
# is a measured one. With 20 segments alive for ~360 minutes each, an independent
# uniform draw over a fixed window does not give a steady live count: the early
# cohort ages out faster than its replacements arrive, so the chain thinned to 3
# segments and the rain visibly stopped. Widening the window moved the problem
# instead of fixing it -- the chain then started at 3 segments and grew to 12, which
# is a front arriving rather than a front raining.
#
# What a stationary front needs is a constant *birth rate*: one new segment every
# `run / (segments - preexisting)` minutes on average. So each cluster is assigned a
# slot on that stride, with only a jitter of `BAND_CLUSTER_BIRTH_JITTER_MIN` either
# side, which keeps the ordering irregular without letting the rate drift.
#
# The span is the run plus one lifetime each side, so the chain is already populated
# at t=0 (from segments that began before the run) and still populated at t=480
# (from segments that began during it).
```

### L219

```
# How much younger, on average, the segment at the leading edge is than the one at
# the trailing edge. Deliberately small: it only has to break the symmetry so the
# chain is not visibly refreshed all at once, and a large value would turn the
# stagger into a staircase where segment age is a function of position.
```

### L224

```
# Radius is scaled within this band over a cluster's life, by the same inverted
# parabola the point cells use. The floor is above 1 because a *frontal segment*
# does not shrink to a point: it thins to a ragged patch and is then dropped, which
# is the discontinuous look the requirement asks for.
```

### L229

```
# A cluster whose radius falls below this stops being drawn at all, so the chain
# loses members behind the front instead of leaving a trail of slivers.
```

### L232

```
# Room added around a buffered track to absorb the 5-minute sampling step and the
# chord error of the buffered curve.
```

### L235

```
# A fixed-outline cell is enveloped by the convex hull of its sampled positions,
# so this only has to cover motion between samples: 5 minutes at the 5.5 m/s wind
# ceiling is 1650 m, plus a little for the orbit's curvature over that step.
```

### L240

```
# Rejected: snapping the swept window onto a coarse grid to force cache hits.
#
# The planner asks for one envelope per flight candidate over that flight's own
# departure window, and ``maxExtraMinutes`` makes those windows nearly all
# distinct (1035 distinct windows, 96 starts x 108 ends). Snapping outward to a
# 1-hour grid does collapse the key space and is safe — measured 0.000000 m of any
# accepted trajectory entering the unsnapped envelope — but the wider envelope is
# more conservative and re-planned the network: 2258 flights closed instead of
# 2036. Kept only as a record so the idea is not re-tried as a "free" speed-up.
#
# What was done instead had to leave the window exact in both directions:
#   * ``envelope`` now bins to 5-minute ticks *before* entering the cache. The raw
#     call sites pass float minutes and produced 13411 distinct float pairs over
#     13419 calls, so the cache could not hit at all; the binned pair repeats
#     without changing which window is computed. A note here once claimed a 92.3%
#     hit rate, which does not survive measurement -- the planner issues windows
#     in sorted departure order, so a real plan hits ~59.8% of the time and a
#     freshly built ``NetworkWeather`` starts cold. See the long note on
#     ``_swept_window`` for why cost per miss, not hit rate, is the thing to fix.
#   * ``_band_hull`` drops drift offsets that lie inside the drift path's own
#     hull, which cannot contribute an extremal point. That is exact and cut the
#     band from 8.3 s to 3.4 s of the plan.
#   * ``_band_hull`` also hulls the cell outline *before* translating it across the
#     drift offsets. The band is a chain of 20 clusters (820 exterior vertices),
#     and extending all 820 across every offset was ~120 ms per window -- 94.7% of
#     the whole plan, which is what pinned the browser's progress at 0% for 82 s.
#     Hulling first is exact (symmetric difference 0.0 m^2 on every window
```
