```
# 每个匿名工作区一份状态。**不是「一份当前状态 + 一份默认状态」**：只要存在一个
# "全局当前"，A 切了通道、B 就会跟着变 —— 那正是这次改造要消掉的东西。
# 键就是工作区（空串 = 未标识），缺席的调用方拿到自己的那一份，彼此不受影响。
```

### L87

```
# 工作区由前端随机生成 ⇒ 不认识 id 的调用方可以把这张表撑大。上限加淘汰，
# **不淘汰正在被使用的那一份**：淘汰的是最早插入的键（dict 保序），够用且无状态。
```

### L1

```
Runtime switch between the measured weather channel and a virtual one.

Why a controller and not a flag
-------------------------------
Virtual weather is dangerous in a way the other data-source switches are not.
Traffic data is simulated everywhere and everybody knows it; the weather channel
is the one channel that is supposed to be a measurement, and a reader who cannot
tell the two apart has lost the only thing the channel was for. So the switch is
an explicit, named object with a readable state -- never an environment variable
read at import time, and never a silent fallback. Three properties follow from
that and are enforced here rather than trusted to callers:

1. **Explicit.** The mode changes only through :func:`set_mode`. Nothing infers
   it, and nothing flips it on failure. If the measured source fails while the
   system is in measured mode, the failure is reported -- it is not patched over
   with an invented frame.
2. **Runtime.** The mode is read on every request, so it takes effect on the next
   call with no restart and no cache flush. A cached frame carries the mode it
   was built under, so a switch mid-flight cannot relabel an old frame.
3. **Auditable.** Every frame this module returns carries provenance saying which
   branch produced it, and :func:`status` reports the current mode, who set it,
   when, and what the alternative would have been. That is what lets an operator
   who sees a suspiciously well-behaved convective line check whether it is real.

Scope
-----
This module switches the **weather and radar channels only**. Traffic is
simulated throughout the system by design and is not affected: there is no
"virtual traffic" mode because there is no measured traffic to switch away from.
```

### L116

```
Switch the weather channel. Returns the new state.

    ``target`` must be one of :data:`MODES`; anything else is rejected rather
    than coerced, because coercing an unexpected value is how a system ends up
    in a mode nobody selected. Switching to the mode already in force is allowed
    and is a no-op apart from refreshing the actor/reason and, if given, the seed
    -- so a caller can update the seed without a redundant mode flip.

    切换只作用于**调用方自己的工作区**：这个开关是"我这块屏看实测还是看虚拟"的
    口径选择，不是全机设置。
```

### L160

```
存入一份**由访问者浏览器代取回来**的实测帧（该工作区专用）。

    调用方（HTTP 层）必须已经用 :func:`cma_tiles.ingest_tiles` 校验过字节，
    这里不再重复校验 —— 校验只应有一处，两处会漂移。
```

### L190

```
The radar frame for the current mode, with the mode stamped on it.

    Measured mode: whatever :func:`cma_tiles.frame` returns, **unmodified** --
    including its failure, which is passed through with ``available: False``. The
    caller decides what to do about that; this function never substitutes a
    virtual frame for a failed measurement.

    一个例外，且不是降级：**若本工作区有一份尚未过期的中继帧，优先用它**。
    字节同样来自 CMA 的实测瓦片，只是传输路径是访问者的浏览器（服务器出口被源站
    按 IP 拒绝，浏览器直取可行）。来源没变，所以它仍然是"实测"，不是替代品；
    取证见 ``cma_tiles`` 里 RELAY 段落的注释。

    Virtual mode: a synthetic frame from :mod:`weather_sim`, seeded from the
    controller's state so the same slot is reproducible across requests.

    Every returned payload carries ``mode`` and, in virtual mode, a
    ``virtualNotice`` naming what it is. That label is not decoration: it is the
    difference between a demonstration and a system that lies to its operator.
```

### L238

```
当前**将要**驱动避让的那帧实测雷达的廉价标识（不发任何网络请求）。

    为什么需要它：计划与地图取帧走的是两条路 —— 地图在浏览器中继成功之后才取，计划在
    构建时取。中继落地**之前**建的计划拿到的是"服务器直取"，而在数据中心的出口上那是
    整站 403，于是计划里没有雷达几何、地图随后却画出一片 —— 屏幕上就是"飞机停在禁入区、
    没有任何绕行"（2026-10-15 线上，12 架载具）。让这一维进计划键，两个世界就变成两份
    计划，而不是一份计划加一句假话。

    只有**中继帧**是有标识的：它是唯一一份我们既知道时次、又能确认"计划与地图会读到同一
    份字节"的观测。直取路径在请求前不发网络请求，无从知道时次，于是返回空串 ——
    空串不进哈希，改造前的键因此一模一样（"改造前行为保持"那条判据才量得出回归）。

    ``weather_mode`` 显式传入而不是读开关：键里写着一个通道、内容来自另一个通道，是本
    项目已经付过一次的账（见 ``service._radar_avoidance`` 的文档串）。
```

### L307

```
Return to the default mode and clear the counters. For tests.

    ``workspace=None`` 清**所有**工作区（这是测试要的语义：一个用例跑完，不留残渣）。
    只清一个工作区请显式传名字 —— 默认清全部是刻意的：测试里漏传一次，症状会是
    "上一个用例的气象口径泄漏进下一个用例"这种只在全量跑时才出现的假失败。
```


## backend-python/traffic/weather_sim.py

### L70

```
# The synthetic mosaic is drawn over the same two tiles the real one publishes,
# so downstream registration, clipping and simplification run against the same
# footprint they always do. Reusing the constants (rather than a private copy)
# means a change to the real grid cannot silently desynchronise this one.
```

### L78

```
# Where the field is allowed to place cells, in degrees.
#
# This is the single most important geometric constraint in the module, and
# getting it wrong is silent: the tiles span 90-135E / 0-66N, but the operating
# area is only the 115-135E / 38-54N box. Scattering cells over the whole tile
# looks fine in the frame's own statistics (echo is present, dBZ is sane) and
# then yields **zero geometry downstream**, because ``radar_field.derive`` clips
# everything to the operating boundary and every cell lands outside it. The
# field is therefore placed in the operating box, and the surrounding tile area
# is simply left without echo -- which is also what the real product does, since
# no radar paints weather where there is none.
```

### L93

```
# Cells are kept this far inside the box edge. A cell centred on the boundary
# would have half its area clipped away, and the clipped remainder is a sliver
# whose component geometry can fall below the minimum-area filter -- so it would
# vanish from the layer. Pulling the centre in by roughly a mature cell radius
# keeps whole cells whole.
```

### L100

```
# Number of convective cells in a full-field frame. Media radar over a region the
# size of the three northeast provinces shows roughly a few dozen distinct
# cells on an active day; a few dozen keeps the drawn layer busy without turning
# it into a solid sheet (which would make every tier merge into one blob and hide
# the nesting the renderer is supposed to show).
#
# 0.9.15：34 -> 48。用户要求「虚拟气象下要有**大量**气象云团以及禁飞区存在，以验证
# 我的运行轨迹可行」。实测 34 个单体在操作区内只覆盖 **1.4—2.6%**（8 个种子），
# 在省域视图上读到的是几个小点，不足以判断航迹能否穿过。48 个单体配合下面的半径
# 下限，同一测量给出的覆盖率是 **4.1—12.0%（中位 9.7%）**，即约 32,800—94,900 km²、
# 中位 76,700 km²（40 个种子，slot 固定）。
#
# 为什么是 48 而不是更高：**上限不是由"想要多密"决定的，而是由"雨带仍要压住航路"
# 决定的**。扫描 34/48/64/96 x 半径 6/8/10 显示，对齐布点相对自由布点的航路穿越数
# 优势在 48 处最稳，到 96 个单体时**反转**——云团太密时自由布点也压到大片航路，对齐
# 的意义就消失了，而"这条雨带专门压在航路上"正是这个演示要证明的事。密度要保，但
# 只能保到不破坏该判据为止。
#
# 以上数字与两个判据可用一条命令同时复现：
#   python tools/probes/_probe_band_density.py
# 注意该探针**固定 slot**：frame() 省略 slot 时取当前分钟，会让每次运行量到不同的
# 天气态势（实测两次运行中位数 13.5 / 15.0）。
```

### L123

```
# Cells are seeded in a handful of clusters rather than uniformly: convective
# systems organise into lines and clusters, and this is also what produces the
# multi-component merged geometry downstream.
```

### L127

```
# Spread of a squall line's cells, in *tiles* (1.0 = one 45-degree tile, i.e. 256
# raster cells). 0.115 of a tile is ~29 raster cells, so a cluster's cells sit
# within a few tens of cells of each other -- close enough that their edges
# overlap on a mature day (which is what produces a merged multi-component
# geometry downstream) but not so close that they fuse into one disc.
```

### L136

```
# --- band placement: aimed at the network, not at a lucky seed ---------------
#
# The field used to place seven independent clusters anywhere in the operating
# box. Measured across seeds, the number of flight corridors the band crossed
# ranged from **4/53 to 23/53** -- so whether the demonstration showed a storm
# affecting traffic was a property of the seed, not of the system. The default
# seed (42) was one of the bad ones at 7/53.
#
# Two constants fix that without making the weather deterministic-looking:
#
# 1. The clusters are placed on a **corridor axis** -- the principal axis of the
#    flight network -- so a band laid along it crosses many corridors by
#    construction rather than by luck. The real atmosphere does exactly this:
#    convective systems organise along the low-level jet, and a transport network
#    is aligned with the terrain that also steers the jet.
# 2. Most of the cells are spent on **one long segment**, with the rest trailing
#    it, so the result reads as a segmented squall line rather than a scatter.
#
# BAND_AXIS_SHARE is the fraction of cells in the main segment; the remainder are
# split across the trailing clusters. Below ~0.6 the main segment stops reading
# as a line; above ~0.85 the trailing segments vanish and the band is a single
# blob, which loses the "分段" (segmented) property entirely.
```

### L159

```
# Segments are offset perpendicular to the band axis, alternating sides, so the
# band has visible gaps between segments instead of stacking into one fat stripe.
# In tiles: 0.055 is ~14 raster cells, i.e. a real gap at this latitude.
```

### L163

```
# The band's own length, in tiles. Chosen against the **cell radius**, not against
# the map: a cell's core radius is 4.5-13.0 raster cells (88-254 km), and a band is
# only a band if neighbouring cells overlap. With 21 cells on the main segment the
# spacing is BAND_LENGTH_TILES / 21, so at 0.30 tiles the spacing was 71 km against
# radii up to 254 km -- cells wide enough to merge sitting too far apart to do it,
# which fragmented the band into 13-32 separate pieces (measured) and cost corridor
# crossings.
#
# This value was picked by sweeping 0.10 / 0.13 / 0.16 / 0.20 / 0.24 over 16 seeds
# and comparing the crossing distribution: the median is flat at 11-12 across the
# whole range, so the choice is not a lever on the crossing count -- it is a lever
# on *fragmentation*, which the number of connected components reports directly.
```

### L176

```
# Where the band is centred, along the axis, as a fraction of the network's own
# extent. The network's centroid sits ~94 km *behind* the box centre on the axis
# (measured), so centring the band on the box leaves it 184 km off the network --
# an offset of more than two cell radii, which is how the band came to miss
# corridors it was aimed at. Centring on the network centroid instead removes
# that bias.
#
# The jitter is deliberately small. A sweep of 24 seeds showed the crossing count
# is dominated by *how much of the network the band sits over*, so a large jitter
# trades the property away for variety: at 0.02 tiles, 4 of 24 seeds fell below 8
# crossings. The per-seed variation now comes mainly from the axis rotation and
# the segment fan, which change the band's *shape* without moving it off the
# network.
```

### L190

```
# Per-seed rotation of the band off the corridor axis, in degrees. The axis is a
# statistical property of the network; a squall line does not have to be exactly
# parallel to it, and the trailing clusters fan off it anyway. This keeps the
# family of layouts varied while holding the band within the range where it still
# crosses a good share of corridors.
```

### L197

```
# Reflectivity structure, in dBZ. The band edges are imported from ``cma_tiles``
# rather than retyped, because these are the operational thresholds every other
# module already classifies against.
```

### L202

```
# The floor of a cell's own gradient. Below this the cell contributes nothing;
# it is the edge radius where the profile reaches the echo floor.
```

### L207

```
# Fraction of cells that carry a trailing stratiform shield. Real convective
# systems are mostly line/embedded, not every cell is isolated.
```

### L211

```
# Cell size, in raster cells. At zoom 3 one raster cell is ~0.176 degrees, so a
# 9-cell core radius is ~1.6 degrees, i.e. ~120-175 km across -- the scale of a
# real convective cell / small MCS in the radar product.
#
# 0.9.15：下限 4.5 -> 8.0（上限保持 13.0）。配合 CELL_COUNT=48，目的是让每个云团
# **本身**就有可见面积，而不是靠堆数量去凑覆盖：半径 8.0 栅格约 1.4°/141 km 直径，
# 在这个档位下实测覆盖率 4.1—12.0%，且连通分量仍是 5—25（中位 13）——即"多"来自
# 云团本身的尺寸，而分段结构没有被糊掉。复现：`python tools/probes/_probe_band_density.py`。
#
# 为什么不再往上抬：半径继续增大就把"分段雨带"变成"一整片中尺度对流系统"，下面
# 的 BAND_LENGTH_TILES 说明里记的正是同一件事的另一次测量。
```

### L230

```
# Radius and peak reflectivity both ride this curve: near zero at genesis, peak
# through maturity, decay back toward zero. It is deliberately not symmetric --
# convective cells grow faster than they dissipate.
```

### L235

```
# Cells are grouped into life-cycle cohorts, so a band is a generation of cells
# rather than 34 independent draws. COHORT_SIZE cells share a phase window of
# COHORT_SPAN_SLOTS; the cohort's own centre is drawn across the whole cycle, so
# different cohorts in the same band are at different stages.
#
# Why this exists (measured): with fully independent uniform phases, the band area
# at a fixed slot ranged 34,000-73,000 km² across 24 seeds and the corridors
# crossed ranged 3-18, because a seed that happened to draw old phases produced a
# field of decayed cells. Grouping makes the storm's vigour a parameter of the
# model instead of an accident of hashing.
```

### L246

```
# 14 of 46 slots is 1.4 hours -- cells in a line are visibly of similar age but
# not identical, which is the spread a real squall line shows.
```

### L250

```
# Mean advection: how far the whole field is carried, and over what range.
#
# These two constants replace a rate-plus-modulo, and the reason is a defect the
# operator could see. The old law was
#
#     travelled = (rate * span) % period
#
# which is a **sawtooth**: the field is carried steadily for ``period / rate``
# slots and then snapped back to where it started, all at once. Measured
# 2026-09-30 (``tools/probes/_probe_drift_continuity.py``, 320 slots, the
# **offset** rather than the painted centroid): a steady step of **15.3 km**
# median per 6-minute slot, and every **17.78 slots (106.7 min of playback)** a
# single step of **256.0 km** -- **16.8x** the median step, **17 times** in 320
# slots. On screen that is a storm that vanishes and reappears where it began,
# reported by the user as 「虚拟气象跑着跑着有一大团忽然消失然后回到初始位置了」.
#
# The wrap was not an oversight; it was the only way to satisfy three
# requirements that a *straight* translation cannot satisfy together:
#
#   * the field must move visibly (an operator scrubbing the timeline has to
#     see travel, which is why the rate is exaggerated well above any real
#     steering flow);
#   * the field must never be carried out of the operating box (measured
#     2026-09-24: an unbounded drift put every tier at 0 km2 from t=240 of a
#     480-minute run, and it never came back);
#   * the field must be a continuous function of the slot.
#
# A translation that is steady, bounded and continuous cannot exist: steadiness
# plus boundedness forces a reset, and a reset is a discontinuity. Something had
# to give, and the honest thing to give is **steadiness, not continuity** --
# a jump is an artefact of the generator, a slowing is not.
#
# So the displacement is now a raised cosine of the playback offset:
#
#     travelled = EXCURSION/2 * (1 - cos(2*pi*span / PERIOD_SLOTS))
#
# which is smooth everywhere, stays inside ``[0, EXCURSION]`` for **every**
# slot (not merely for the ones a demo happens to ask for), and starts at 0 --
# so the field begins where it always did and moves forward, never backward,
# throughout the 8 h window.
#
# The period is deliberately **twice the timeline**: at span = 0 the field is at
# one end of the swing and at span = PERIOD_SLOTS/2 it is at the other, so a
# whole playback is one half-cycle, monotone, and the reversal -- which is what
# a cosine does eventually -- falls outside the window the system plays. Beyond
# the window the field eases back, which is a statement about a stalled steering
# flow rather than a snap.
#
# What this costs, stated rather than discovered later: the peak speed is
# ``EXCURSION/2 * 2*pi / PERIOD_SLOTS`` = 0.00216 tiles/slot = **7.3 km per
# 6-minute slot at the fastest** (measured), against a 15.3 km median / 256.0 km
# snap before. The elapsed displacement over the playback is unchanged (the full
# excursion), so the operator still watches the band cross a large part of the
# region; it crosses at a plausible order of tens of km/h instead of at an
# exaggerated 150+ km/h.
#
# A second property worth keeping: **the largest possible single step is the
# fastest steady step**, so no slot anywhere can produce a discontinuity. That is
# asserted, not assumed -- see ``test_the_echo_never_snaps_back_to_where_it_started``.
#
# One honest caveat, because the probe measures two different things and only one
# of them is the drift: the **advection offset** is now continuous (max step
# 7.3 km, ratio 1.4x, 0 jumps), but the **painted field centroid** still moves up
# to **20.2 km** in one slot (2.8x its median) -- because cells are born and die,
# which shifts a centroid without anything translating. That is the life-cycle,
# not the drift, and it is 1/14 of the old 277.2 km centroid step with **0** jumps
# over 3x median. The probe reports both so neither can be quoted as the other.
```

### L321

```
# Extra along-track offset proportional to a cell's **vigour** (its life-cycle
# factor), so a mature cell sits slightly downwind of a young one instead of the
# whole cohort advancing as a rigid stamp. One lifetime of vigour contributes
# 46 * 0.00006 = 0.0028 tiles, i.e. ~9.5 km, against cell radii of 88-254 km.
# (Tile scale: the probe measures 15.3 km per 0.0045 tile of drift, i.e. ~3,400 km
# per tile at the field centre -- the single conversion used throughout.)
#
# The argument is the life-cycle factor rather than the age, and that is a
# correction: ``age`` is ``(slot + phase) % LIFE_SLOTS``, a sawtooth, so the old
# ``offset = rate * age`` gave every cell a **~9.5 km teleport** at the end of its
# life -- the same defect as the field-scale one, one cell at a time. The vigour
# factor is already continuous and is exactly zero at both ends of the life
# cycle, so the term is continuous by construction and needs no separate fade.
```

### L337

```
# The slot the drift is measured from: the **demo timeline's own zero**.
#
# This duplicates ``service.SIMULATION_BASE_TIME``, and it is kept as a date
# rather than a magic slot number because the anchor is no longer cosmetic. Under
# the old sawtooth a wrong anchor only rotated the phase, so nothing could see it;
# with a raised cosine it decides *which part of the swing* the playback traverses,
# and the whole "starts at zero excursion, ends at the full excursion" calibration
# depends on it. The duplicate is a coupling, so it is **asserted** in
# ``test_the_drift_anchor_is_the_demo_timeline`` rather than left to be noticed: if
# the two ever drift apart, that test says so.
#
# A correction worth recording, because the previous version of this comment
# repeated it: it claimed the old literal ``4970500`` was the slot index of
# 2083-05-28 while its own comment said 2026-09-14. Recomputed here,
# ``_slot_index(dt.datetime(2026, 9, 14, 10, 0, tzinfo=utc)) == 4970500`` exactly,
# so the literal was **right** and that claim was **wrong** -- an inherited
# conclusion (it came from a session note, not from running the conversion) that
# no one had re-derived. The reason to prefer a named date over the number is
# therefore not "the number was wrong" but "the number cannot be checked by
# reading it", which is what let a false story about it survive.
```

### L381

```
# A little multiplicative texture so the outline is not a clean conic. Kept
# small: the aim is a ragged edge, not a shattered one. Each octave is a single
# sinusoid of the azimuth, so the effective radius stays a single-valued closed
# curve and the polygon union downstream produces no slivers.
```

### L387

```
# Values below this are written as ``None`` (no echo) rather than as a number,
# matching the real tile's missing-cell representation.
```

### L422

```
# Set once `_squall_frame()` has reached a verdict, **including a negative one**.
#
# Without this flag the cache guard has to be "both values are None", which
# connotes "not yet computed" -- so a failed derivation is retried on every single
# call to `_position`, i.e. the cache is disabled precisely in the expensive case
# it exists for, and the fallback state cannot be represented from outside either.
# The flag makes "no axis" a cached result rather than a cache miss.
```

```
# Double the angle so the axis is undirected (a segment and its
# reverse must reinforce, not cancel).
```

### L500

```
# Centroid of the network in projected metres, converted to the same
# lon/lat -> tile path the field box uses so the units match.
```

### L582

```
# Generalise the single fixed axis by a per-seed rotation, and shift the
# centre by a per-seed jitter. The measurement says the *statistical* crossing
# is what improved, not that one particular layout is better, so the field
# must stay a family of situations: without the jitter every seed would draw
# the identical line and the generator would be a fixed graphic.
```

### L600

```
# Trailing clusters: a shorter line, offset perpendicular to the axis.
# The side alternates with the cluster so the band fans rather than
# drifting entirely to one side.
```

### L646

```
# Primary placement: on the corridor-aligned squall band. This is what makes
# the band cross the flight network by construction. It returns ``None`` only
# when the traffic catalog was unavailable, in which case the free placement
# below still yields a valid convective field -- a weather frame must not
# become unavailable because a traffic input was.
```

```
# Position along the line, centred so the cluster straddles its anchor. The
# spread is in tile units: 0.01 of a tile is ~0.45 degrees of longitude.
```

### L729

```
# ``dy`` is negated because ``field_y`` grows **southward** (see
# ``_cell_state``), while the bearing is the usual compass convention of
# north-up east-right. Without the sign the whole field is carried *south*,
# out of an operating box that sits in the northern half of the grid, and the
# derived tiers come back empty -- which is exactly how the no-fly layer was
# lost mid-playback (measured 2026-09-24: every tier 0 km2 from t=240 of 480).
```

### L763

```
# The cohort's own centre, drawn across the whole life cycle so successive
# cohorts in the band are at different stages -- a mature segment with a
# younger one trailing it is what a real line looks like.
#
# A version that restricted the centre to the vigorous part of the cycle was
# tried and reverted: because a cell's *position* is reseeded per cycle (see
# `_cell_state`), confining the phases confines the cycle number too, the
# cohorts then land on top of each other instead of along the line, and the
# measured band collapsed to 1,963-3,901 km² on the worst seeds. The vigour
# spread is handled by `_life_curve` and the cohort span instead.
```

### L813

```
# A composite index packs the cell id and its generation, so `_position`
# (which hashes a single integer) places each generation differently while
# staying a pure function of the two.
```

### L878

```
# Boundary perturbation, sampled on the true azimuth so the outline stays a
# single-valued closed curve (a non-single-valued radius would self-intersect
# and the polygon union would produce slivers).
```

### L887

```
# Radial profile: flat-topped core, then a steep drop. `t ** 1.9` keeps the
# inner contours broad (so a STRONG core is a real area, not a dot) while the
# outer bands widen quickly toward the edge.
```

### L900

```
# Offset the shield centre downshear so it trails the core rather than
# sitting concentrically under it -- that offset is what produces the
# asymmetric comma/comma-tail shape radar imagery is recognised by.
```

### L918

```
# Largest multiplicative reach of a cell's own texture, and of its shield. Used
# to bound the per-cell bounding box so the inner loop touches only pixels the
# cell can actually influence.
```

### L942

```
# Accumulate into a row-major, tile-sized buffer. Row 0 is the north edge,
# matching the raw grid (see `cma_tiles.decode_tile`), so tile-unit y grows
# southward and the offset below needs no flip.
```

### L969

```
# Screen rows grow southward, and `_cell_sample` wants dy positive

```

### L1

```
Synthetic radar reflectivity mosaic, shaped like the real CMA product.

Why this exists
---------------
The operational path reads the real national mosaic (``cma_tiles``). That source
is a public, uncredentialed tile service with **no availability guarantee**: it
can be down, rate-limited, or a frame can fail to publish, and when it does the
radar channel honestly reports ``available: False`` and draws nothing. That is
the right behaviour for operations, but it makes three things impossible:

  * demonstrating the radar channel when the upstream is unreachable;
  * reproducing a radar layer for a test without depending on the network;
  * showing the operator what a convective day looks like on demand.

This module generates a **stand-in** frame for exactly those cases. It is
selected only through the explicit controller in :mod:`traffic.weather_control`
-- never as a silent fallback. If the real source fails while the system is in
real mode, the system still reports the failure; it does not quietly paint
invented echoes.

What "looking like mainstream radar" means here
-----------------------------------------------
A weather-media radar image is not a collection of smooth discs. The features
that make it read as radar are morphology and intensity structure, and both are
modelled explicitly rather than approximated by noise:

1. **Cells, not blobs.** Echo is organised into discrete convective cells, each
   with its own elliptical footprint and its own irregular boundary, plus
   trailing stratiform rain shields behind the convective line.
2. **Intensity is a gradient, not a flat fill.** Each cell carries a peak dBZ at
   its core that falls off toward the edge, so the 35/45/60 dBZ contours nest
   inside one another the way they do on a real product. A flat-filled cell
   would put the STRONG and STRATIFORM bands on top of each other and the map's
   nested tiers would collapse into one shape.
3. **Life cycle.** Real cells grow, mature, then decay. A cell's peak reflectivity
   and radius both follow a life-cycle curve indexed by its age, so the same seed
   yields a physically ordered sequence rather than independent random frames.
4. **Organisation.** Cells are not scattered uniformly: they are seeded along
   squall-line axes and in clusters, so the resulting pattern reads as a system
   rather than as noise. This is also what makes the merged-component geometry
   downstream exercise its real code path.
5. **Motion.** The whole field advects with a mean wind, so successive frames
   translate instead of reshuffling. Media products show motion; a field that
   teleports is instantly recognisable as fake.

Output contract
---------------
The frame dict is **byte-compatible with** ``cma_tiles.frame()``: same keys,
same ``(x, y, rows)`` cell triple, same ``degreesPerCell``, same
``256 x 256`` raster with ``None`` for missing and dBZ as a float. That is what
lets ``radar_field.derive`` and ``service.radar_regions`` consume it with **no
branch at all** -- the value of matching the contract is that no consumer has to
know which generator produced the frame.

The one deliberate difference is provenance: ``provider`` is
``SYNTHETIC_RADAR`` and ``synthetic`` is ``True``, so anything that cares can
tell. Consumers that do not care are unaffected.

Determinism
-----------
Everything is a pure function of ``(seed, slot)``. No wall-clock read, no global
RNG: two calls with the same arguments produce identical bytes, which is what
makes a synthetic frame usable as a test fixture.
```

### L362

```
把漂移基准对齐到**本次推演的起点**（W-02 的漏项，2026-10-21 补）。

    为什么必须有这一句：W-02 把 `service.SIMULATION_BASE_TIME` 从钉死的
    2026-09-14 改成"演示当日 10:00Z"（理由很硬：民航情报的 CTOT 全是 09/14 20:12，
    顶栏却写当日，同一屏两个日期）。但**漂移基准没有跟着改**，于是相位 =
    `(索引 - 参考) % 周期` 每天平移：周期是推演窗口的两倍（960 分钟），
    一天 1440 分钟 ⇒ 每天相位推进 480 分钟 = **半个周期**，
    也就是隔一天演示的余弦就走到另一半、扫掠方向反过来。

    它不是"看起来一样"的问题：`weather_sim` 的漂移决定回波往哪个方向、走多快，
    而回波又决定计划绕哪一侧。相位翻面时，同一份种子会给出另一套避让结果。

    由调用方在**定义完自己的时间基准之后**调用（`service` 就是这么做的）：
    本模块不反向 import service，避免环状依赖。
```

### L393

```
Deterministic float in [0, 1) from the given integers/strings.

    A local integer hash is used instead of :func:`random.random` so the whole
    module stays a pure function of its inputs -- a global RNG would make the
    output depend on call order.
```

### L435

```
Principal axis of the flight network and its centroid, both in tile units.

    Returns ``(axis_deg, centre)`` where ``centre`` is an ``(x, y)`` pair -- or
    ``None`` when the axis is not derivable.

    The earlier revision documented a three-element return and *claimed* ``None``
    on failure while actually returning ``(None, None)``. That mismatch was not
    cosmetic: ``squall_axis()`` reads ``frame[0]`` and so treated it as falsy and
    worked, whereas ``_band_placement()`` unpacked it and raised
    ``TypeError: cannot unpack non-iterable NoneType``. Two callers, one wrong
    contract, and only one of them noticed. The contract is now ``None`` in both
    the docstring and the code, and asserted by a test.

    Derived from the corridors rather than hard-coded, so the band follows the
    network if the network changes; and computed once, because it is a property of
    the catalog and not of the frame.

    **Principal axis**, not the bearing of one corridor: the network has hundreds
    of segments and a single one would be an arbitrary choice. The standard
    approach for a set of undirected segments is to double every bearing into a
    full circle and average the unit vectors -- doubling is what makes 10 degrees
    and 190 degrees agree instead of cancelling, which plain averaging does not do
    for an undirected axis.

    The centroid is taken over the **cities** rather than over the box, because
    the box is a rectangle around an irregular three-province outline and its
    centre is measurably off the network (by ≈184 km along the axis).

    Degenerate cases return ``None`` rather than a guessed value, and the caller
    falls back to the previous free placement. A weather generator must not fail
    because the traffic catalog was unavailable: that would make a weather request
    depend on a traffic request, which is exactly the coupling the two weather
    channels are supposed to avoid.
```

### L518

```
Forget the cached axis, so the next call re-derives it.

    Exists for tests and for a caller that has changed the catalog at runtime. A
    cache with no invalidation path is the reason the failure state above could not
    be represented; keeping the reset explicit means the cache stays private.
```

### L530

```
Pin the axis for a test, or mark it as underivable with ``axis_deg=None``.

    A deliberate, named back door rather than monkeypatching module globals from
    the test: the tests need to exercise both the aligned and the fallback path,
    and doing that by assigning private names makes the test depend on the cache's
    internal representation -- which is what let the earlier version of this test
    assert the wrong thing and pass for the wrong reason.
```

### L551

```
Field position of cell ``index`` along the corridor-aligned squall band.

    Returns ``(x, y)`` in fractional tile coordinates, or ``None`` when the axis
    could not be derived (the caller then uses the legacy free placement).

    Three nested offsets, all in tile units, and each one exists for a reason a
    probe measured:

    * **along** -- position along the band. Cells are spread over
      :data:`BAND_LENGTH_TILES` so they merge into a small number of long
      segments instead of one disc. This is what produces "分段式" geometry.
    * **across** -- the band's width, one cell radius. Too wide and the band
      stops reading as a line; the value is a fraction of the cell radius so the
      band is roughly one to two cells thick.
    * **segment offset** -- alternating displacement perpendicular to the axis,
      which is what puts *gaps* between the segments. Without it every segment
      merges with the next and the band becomes one continuous blob, losing the
      segmentation the requirement asks for.

    Only the interior cells of the band are aimed; the trailing clusters keep
    their spread, because a real squall line has a leading edge and a messy tail,
    and an entirely regular composition would read as a drawn shape rather than
    as weather.
```

### L616

```
Inverse of :func:`cma_tiles.cell_centre_lonlat`, in fractional tile units.

    Returns ``(world_x, world_y)`` where the integer part is the tile coordinate
    and the fraction is the position inside it, at ``zoom``. ``world_y`` grows
    southward, matching the raw grid (row 0 is the northernmost row).

    The forward function is the only georeference the project had; a generator
    that wants to place a cell "at 124E, 45N" needs the inverse and there was no
    reason for two modules to own two versions of the same projection.
```

### L635

```
Field position of cell ``index``, in fractional tile coordinates.

    Cells are placed in clusters, and inside a cluster they are laid along a
    squall-line axis. The placement is done in **mercator tile units** so the
    result lines up with the raster grid without a second projection step, but
    it is *bounded to the operating box* (:data:`FIELD_LON_MIN` and friends):
    the anchors are drawn inside the box in lon/lat, converted, and then jittered
    by a spread expressed in tile units. Without that bound the field would be
    scattered over the whole 90-135E / 0-66N tile and every cell would be
    clipped away downstream.
```

### L674

```
Slot index of the demo timeline's zero, memoised.

    Memoised because :func:`_advection` runs once per cell per tile per frame,
    and the conversion is a datetime parse plus a division.
```

### L686

```
Mean-wind displacement of a cell, in **tiles**, bounded and continuous.

    Applied to every cell identically, so the field **translates** rather than
    reshuffling -- the single strongest visual cue that a radar loop is real.
    Because the drift is a whole-field offset it preserves the relative geometry
    of the squall lines, so a line stays a line as it moves.

    Four attempts are worth recording, because each fixed one defect and exposed
    the next (the first three observed on the map 2026-09-24, the fourth on
    2026-09-30):

      1. ``rate * age`` with ``age = (slot_index+phase) % LIFE_SLOTS``. ``age``
         wraps every 4.6 h, so the second half of an 8 h playback was a *replay*
         of the first; and the offset was capped at 0.041 tiles ~= 1.8 degrees
         against a 9-degree box, so the field read as stationary.
      2. ``rate * slot_index``. The index is ~5 million, which throws the field
         millions of tiles away and empties the raster.
      3. ``rate * (slot_index - REFERENCE)``. Monotonic and visible, but **it
         only goes one way**: the grid covers lon 90-135 E and the operating box
         sits at 120-131, so within four hours the field had been carried off the
         eastern edge of the grid and every tier came back empty -- the no-fly
         layer silently disappeared mid-playback (t=240 of a 480-minute run
         measured ``CONVECTIVE 0 km2``, and it stayed zero).
      4. ``(rate * span) % period`` -- the **sawtooth**. Bounded and steady, and
         it kept every tier alive for a whole playback, but the modulo *is* the
         discontinuity: one step in 17.78 carried the field 267.5 km back to
         where it started, 16.6x the steady step. Four such snaps per playback.

    The current law is a raised cosine of the playback offset, which is bounded
    for every slot, continuous everywhere, and monotone across the whole 8 h
    window (see the constants above for the arithmetic and for what it costs).

    ``vigour`` -- the cell's life-cycle factor -- adds a small along-track offset
    so a mature cell sits slightly downwind of a young one in the same cohort,
    instead of the whole line advancing as a rigid stamp. It replaces the old
    ``age`` argument, which was a sawtooth and therefore teleported each cell
    ~9.5 km at the end of its life.
```

### L739

```
Life-cycle phase offset for cell ``index``, in slots.

    Cells in a squall line are a **generation cohort**: they were triggered by the
    same front and are therefore of similar age, which is what makes a line read
    as a line rather than as a scatter of unrelated storms.

    The original implementation drew every phase uniformly from ``[0, LIFE_SLOTS)``.
    The *ensemble* average was fine, but individual seeds were not: at any given
    instant a seed whose 34 draws skewed old had most of its cells past maturity,
    and the resulting field was a set of decayed husks. Measured across 24 seeds,
    the drawn band area varied from **34,000 to 73,000 km²** and the number of
    flight corridors it crossed from **3 to 18** -- with area and crossings moving
    together. The demonstration was showing "the weather affects traffic" or "the
    weather misses traffic" depending on an accident of hashing.

    Cells are now grouped into cohorts of :data:`COHORT_SIZE`: within a cohort the
    phases span only :data:`COHORT_SPAN_SLOTS`, and the cohort's own centre is
    drawn across the full life cycle. So the band is always *a* storm -- young,
    mature, or decaying, varying by seed and by slot -- rather than a mixture that
    happens to average out to nothing. Both the mean and the variance of the band
    area are properties of the model again, not of the seed.
```

### L780

```
Geometry and vigour of one cell at one instant, or ``None`` if dead.

    This is the single place where a cell's parameters are derived.

    **Cyclic life cycle.** ``slot_index`` counts every 6-minute slot since the
    epoch -- a little under five million at the time of writing -- so an age of
    ``slot_index - birth`` would put every cell millions of slots past its
    ``LIFE_SLOTS`` and the whole field would be empty. Instead each cell gets a
    fixed phase offset and the age wraps:

        age = (slot_index + phase) % LIFE_SLOTS

    so cells are continuously born, mature and decay as the clock advances, and
    the population stays roughly constant. The *cycle* number -- how many whole
    lifetimes have elapsed -- reseeds the cell's position, so a regenerated cell
    appears somewhere new rather than popping back at the exact spot it died in.
    That is what a convective corridor does: the individual cell is short-lived,
    the region that keeps producing cells is not.
```

### L836

```
Cell vigour in [0, 1] for an age measured in 6-minute slots.

    Returns 0 outside the cell's life so a decayed cell contributes nothing
    rather than a residual disc.
```

### L863

```
dBZ contributed by one cell at offset ``(dx, dy)`` from its centre.

    ``dy`` is positive northward. The profile is a radial falloff from ``peak``
    at the centre to ``EDGE_DBZ`` at the effective boundary, so contours of
    increasing dBZ nest inward and the renderer's STRONG/CONVECTIVE/STRATIFORM
    tiers come out properly enclosed.
```

### L926

```
Render one tile's dBZ raster, cell-by-cell into each cell's own bbox.

    The obvious implementation -- for every pixel, loop over every cell and take
    the max -- is correct but costs ``pixels x cells x octaves``: measured at
    **71 s** for a two-tile frame, which is unusable inside an HTTP request.
    Inverting the loop changes the complexity class rather than shaving
    constants: each cell tests only the pixels inside its own effective radius,
    so the per-cell cost is proportional to its own area and the texture sum is
    evaluated once per pixel instead of once per (pixel, cell) pair.

    A cell's position is in fractional tile units, so its raster coordinate
    *within this tile* is ``(field_coord - tile_index) * TILE_SIZE``. A cell whose
    centre is off-tile but whose edge reaches in is still drawn, and because the
    arithmetic is the same for every tile a cell spanning a seam is rendered
    identically in both -- no discontinuity appears along the join.
```

### L988

```
Generate a synthetic radar frame with the real product's exact shape.

    ``seed`` selects the weather situation; ``slot`` fixes the instant (a
    :class:`datetime` or ISO string) and therefore the cells' life-cycle stage
    and the field's advection offset. Both are explicit, so the same pair always
    yields the same frame.

    The returned dict is a **superset** of ``cma_tiles.frame()``: every key that
    module produces is present with the same meaning, which is what lets
    ``radar_field.derive`` and ``service.radar_regions`` consume it with no
    branch at all.
```


## backend-python/traffic/weather_sources.py

### L21

```
# The Java service owns the QWeather credentials. Python never holds the key; it
# reads the internal raw endpoint, which the controller restricts to loopback or
# an explicit internal token.
```

### L29

```
# --- 免费、无需密钥的补充源（2026-10-17 实测全部 HTTP 200，来源见 docs）--------
# 为什么默认不启用：主链路每轮加载都要抓一次，多一个源就多一次超时风险。这三个
# 能力是**按需调用**的（`/traffic/weather/observations`、`/traffic/weather/models`），
# 不进主链路 —— 目标是"加载不超过 10 秒"这条硬线（见 README 的验收口径）。
```

### L38

```
# Aviation Weather Center（美国 FAA）的 METAR 接口：全球机场、无密钥。
# 用 **bbox** 而不是逐站 ICAO 代码：33 个城市里只有一部分有机场，硬编码 ICAO
# 一旦写错一个字母就静默无数据，而"这个城市没有机场"与"代码写错了"在界面上
# 长得一模一样。按范围取再就近匹配，就没有这个问题。
```

### L45

```
# 实测自证（ZYTL 2026-10-17 12:30Z）：rawOb `13009G17MPS ... 6000`
#   JSON wspd=17 / wgst=33 → 17.5 / 33.0 节 = 9 / 17 m/s  ✓ 单位是**节**
#   JSON visib=3.73      → 6003 m    而 rawOb 报 6000 米      ✓ 单位是**法定英里**
```

### L50

```
# Open-Meteo 同一供应商下的两个独立模型端点。用来看"两个模型是否互相支持" ——
# 这是不确定性，不是第二份真相，所以只报差值、不替换主源。
```

### L62

```
# --- 天气现象编码：两套空间，一个词汇 -------------------------------------
# Open-Meteo 返回 WMO 4677，和风返回它自己的图标代码。两组数字**会撞车**，把它们的
# 并集交给下游判定，就是把一种天气读成另一种，而且不会有任何一处报错。所以映射在
# 适配器里做（只有适配器知道自己的编码空间），下游只读一个与来源无关的
# ``conditionClass``。
#
# 只映射**极端**两类。轻度的雨、阵雨、毛毛雨不进来，因为它们不该生成任何风险几何
# —— 见 weather.py 的 ``_extreme_kind``。
```

### L72

```
# 和风图标代码：302 雷阵雨 / 303 强雷阵雨 / 304 雷阵雨伴冰雹；
# 307 大雨 / 308 极端降雨 / 310 暴雨 / 311 大暴雨 / 312 特大暴雨。
```

### L196

```
# The requested point is kept separate from any provider grid point, so a
# weather grid position never overwrites the verified city centre.
```

### L280

```
# --- MET Norway（全球数值预报，免费、无密钥，2026-10-17 实测 HTTP 200）--------
# 定位：**可作主源**。它逐点返回、全球覆盖，不像和风那样需要 Java 侧持凭据。
# 代价是逐点一次请求（33 城 = 33 次），所以必须并发 + 缓存；缓存 15 分钟与
# Open-Meteo 一致 —— 两个源的"新鲜度"口径不同会让界面上同一块牌子说两种话。
#
# MET Norway 的天气现象是**符号串**（`clearsky` / `heavyrainandthunder` …），
# 与 WMO 数字码、和风图标码是三个互不相交的空间。映射照规矩放在适配器里：
# 只有适配器知道自己的编码空间，下游只读与来源无关的 `conditionClass`。
```

### L390

```
# --- Aviation Weather Center METAR（真实机场观测报文，免费、无密钥）------------
# 定位：**补充观测通道，不作主源**。它是机场单点观测，不是面上的气象场 ——
# 33 个地级行政区里只有一部分附近有机场，所以 `coverage` 必须如实报低覆盖，
# 不能拿"离得最近的那个站"冒充整座城市。
#
# 与雷达的界线（词汇纪律，硬）：METAR 是**报文观测**，不是雷达回波，不得换算成
# dBZ，也不得据此推断空域管制结论。
```

### L438

```
# METAR 的 wxString 是自由文本（-SHRA / BR / TSRA），只认两类极端，
# 与 WMO / 和风同一条纪律：轻度降水不生成风险几何。
```

### L489

```
# 同一个站会返回多条历史报文；只留每个站**最新**那一条。取错会让
# "两小时前的阵风"冒充当前观测，而两者在界面上长得一模一样。
```

### L516

```
# 这是**机场观测报文**，不是模型、也不是雷达。dataType 必须说出这件事，
# 否则下游会把它当成面上的气象场用。
```

### L553

```
# --- Open-Meteo 多模型一致性（同一供应商下的两个独立模型）--------------------
# 定位：**只报差值，不作第二份真相**。两个模型分歧大 = 不确定性高，这对低空
# 交通是有用信息；但把 ICON 的数值当成"另一个实测"就是造数据。
```

### L610

```
# 只有**两个模型都有值**时差值才有意义；单模型时报 None，
# 报 0 会被读成"两个模型完全一致"（M 家族：缺值 ≠ 零）。
```

### L657

```
# 2026-10-17 补充的三个**免费、无需密钥**的能力。前两个可作主源，
# 后两个是补充通道（不进主链路，按需调用）。
```

### L175

```
Normalise the Java internal raw payload into the shared observation shape.

    QWeather returns wind in km/h and visibility in km; the shared contract is
    m/s and metres so both providers can feed the same risk rules.
```

### L305

```
Normalise one MET Norway `compact` response into the shared observation shape.

    Units: the API already speaks m/s for wind and °C for temperature, so unlike
    QWeather (km/h) and METAR (knots) nothing has to be converted here. Visibility
    is **not** in the compact product at all — it is left as ``None`` rather than
    filled with a plausible number (缺报 ≠ 0，见 §16 夹具纪律).
```

### L407

```
Convert METAR visibility (statute miles) to metres, keeping "6+" a bound.

    ``"6+"`` 的含义是**大于 6 英里**，不是正好 6 英里。把它写成 9656 m 会让下游
    把"至少"读成"正好"，而"能见度只报了下限"与"能见度就是 9656"是两件事。
    所以返回 ``(None, True)``，由调用方把这一情况写进 qualityFlags。
```


## backend-python/traffic/zones.py

### L46

```
# Only these catalogue geometry kinds may be turned into a shape. Listing them
# explicitly means a future hand-edited polygon can never be silently rendered as
# if it were published airspace.
```

### L51

```
# Segment count for a generated circle. The smallest generated radius is 5 km and
# the largest 55 km; 32 segments already reads as a smooth circle at the map
# scales this system uses, and keeps the frame payload small.
```

### L58

```
# Per-flight zone reporting cap: a 55 km aerodrome area overlaps its neighbours,
# so a single long cruise can legitimately sit inside several of them. The counts
# stay complete; only the id list is capped.
```

### L75

```
# A registered site must say why it has no shape, and must carry the
# public coordinate that was actually verified.
```

### L80

```
# ``or {}`` rather than a dict default: a hand-edited catalogue can set
# the key to null, and a missing coordinate must surface as a diagnosable
# ValueError instead of an AttributeError from `.get` on None.
```

### L294

```
# No shape is emitted; the registration is still published so the
# operator sees that the category exists and what to look up.
```

### L304

```
# An unbounded ceiling is drawn up to the operation's own risk ceiling so
# the volume is visible; the label carries the legal range either way.
```

### L1

```
Fixed, non-weather airspace restrictions.

Everything here comes from the versioned catalogue in
``data/traffic/fixed-zones.json``, which is generated by ``_gen_fixed_zones.py``
from published law plus public datasets. Two properties matter more than the
zone count:

**No fabricated geometry.** A site only carries a shape when a published rule
fixes that shape — a statutory radius (GB 6249—2025 non-residential area /
planning restriction area), a standard protection radius (GB 31223—2014 weather
radar), or a published rectangle (the 55 km aerodrome protection area and the
runway inspection rectangle). Where the concrete extent is published per city as
aeronautical information rather than nationally — 军事禁区、军工设施、监管场所、
边境带、饮用水水源保护区、重要革命纪念地 — the site is registered with its
name, clause and verified public coordinate and ``geometry`` stays ``null``.
Inventing a buffer there would put unverified boundary data on the map, which is
exactly what the project forbids.

**Enforcement mirrors the legal instrument.** 第十九条 creates 管制空域
(controlled airspace) — entry requires approval, it is not an absolute ban — so
aerodrome zones default to ``APPROVAL_REQUIRED`` and are reported per flight
rather than silently blocking the network. ``PROHIBITED`` is reserved for what is
genuinely prohibitive for civil UAS. The strict aerodrome reading is available
through ``airportFootprintPolicy``; the measured cost of it is recorded in the
plan statistics so the choice is visible rather than hidden.
```

### L114

```
Runway-centred rectangle: ``half_width`` either side, ``beyond_end`` past each end.

    ``centre``/``half_width``/``beyond_end`` are in projected metres. The runway
    bearing is a true compass bearing, so the rectangle's long axis (local +x,
    which is compass 090) is rotated by ``90 - bearing``.
```

### L145

```
Union of every ``PROHIBITED`` zone, for lateral avoidance during routing.

    ``APPROVAL_REQUIRED`` zones are deliberately excluded: they gate operations
    through an approval step, they do not remove the airspace from use, and
    treating them as solid obstacles would silently reshape the whole network.
```

### L163

```
STRtree over the zone footprints.

    50 zones x ~200 trajectory samples x several thousand flights is millions of
    point-in-polygon tests if done naively, so each trajectory first queries the
    tree by its own bounding box and only the few overlapping zones are tested.
```

### L187

```
Which fixed zones a trajectory enters, and at what altitude.

    ``points`` are ``[x, y, z, minute]`` in the same projected metres the planner
    uses. The vertical band is honoured: an aerodrome protection area that starts
    at 真高 120 m does not flag a delivery drone holding at 80 m.

    Vectorised on purpose. The first implementation built a ``Point`` (and a
    ``MultiPoint`` hull) per sample and cost 3.5 s of an 8 s plan; feeding the
    coordinate arrays straight to ``intersects_xy`` and clipping them against the
    altitude band first removed almost all of it.
```

### L241

```
Registered and published zones whose *horizontal* footprint covers a point.

    Used for the ground resources — a hub pad or a delivery node standing inside
    a prohibited zone cannot be departed from at all, which is a different
    failure from a path merely crossing one.
```


## backend-python/weather/engine.py

### L110

```
# At most one-minute spacing plus a conservative envelope for movement and shape change.
# Bound: <= 8 m/s translation + 10 m/s boundary growth, including birth/death.
```

### L1

```
Deterministic births, continuous advection, lifetimes and risk derivation.

No real station or calibrated probability is implied by synthetic scenarios.
Time is relative to a persisted origin, never modulo a wall-clock period.
```


## backend-python/weather/metar_history.py

### L34

```
# 0.11.7：案例从 3 站扩到 4 站（补大连）。**只加坐标，不加进上面的 `STATIONS`** ——
# 那个元组是"实时拉取列表"，多拉一站会改变 `_loocv_sigma` 的留一折数，
# 进而改变所有概率的 σ。案例需要的是"这个站的坐标能查到"，就只是这一件事。
```

### L2

```
三站 METAR 实况与近 9 小时历史（换芯的运行时数据源）。

为什么运行时必须自己取历史：
    0.11.1 的模型特征里有 `visMin3h` / `gustMax3h` / `windChange3h` 这类**滞后量**。
    只在建场时取一次"当前值"是喂不出这些特征的；拿气候中位数填进去，模型会给出
    一个看起来很正常、其实没见过天气的概率 —— 那正是本项目最忌讳的那类静默错误。

数据源：Aviation Weather Center `https://aviationweather.gov/api/data/metar`
    - 一次请求覆盖三站（ids=ZYTX,ZYCC,ZYHB），`hours=9`；
    - `wspd` / `wgst` 单位是**节**，`temp` / `dewp` 是摄氏度，`altim` 是 hPa；
    - `visib` 可能是 `6+` / `10+`（下界）或分数 `1/2`，必须按字符串解析；
    - 没有阵风时报文里没有 `wgst` —— 缺测语义是"没有阵风"，用持续风回填，
      与训练侧 `build_metar_dataset.py` 的口径**必须一致**。

缓存 10 分钟。失败**不抛异常**：返回 `available: false` + 原因，由上层决定
是降级到"模式估算"还是不给结论。
```

### L54

```
能见度的**数值 + 它是不是下界** + 原始报文。

    0.11.3（W-08）：原来 `parse_visibility` 把 `6+` / `10+` 换算成一个精确数
    （9656.1 m）就返回了，下界这个事实被丢掉。于是界面上出现"能见度 9656.1 m"
    —— 一个精确到小数点后一位的数，而它其实是"至少 6 英里"。
    这不是精度问题，是**把下界读成了观测值**：9.7 km 与"≥9.7 km"在决策上不是一回事。
```

### L86

```
`6+` = 下界 6 SM；分数 `1/2`；纯数字按 SM。无法解析返回 None。

    数值型 API 保持原样（训练集与历史指标读的就是它）；需要区分下界的地方
    读 `parse_visibility_detail`，那里才带 `lowerBound`。
```


## backend-python/weather/om_forecast.py

### L47

```
# **不再无声返回 None。** 上层拿到 `None` 只会显示"预报不可用"，
# 而"为什么不可用"（超时 / DNS / 429 / 字段改名）在这之前没有任何痕迹。
# 这一条只加日志、不改返回值：调用方对 `None` 的处理是对的，缺的是可诊断性。
```

### L2

```
Open-Meteo 未来 8 小时逐时值（**模式延伸**用，不是观测、也不是本模型）。

为什么需要它：
    本模型的有效时效是 0–3 h（标签窗口就是未来 3 小时）。走廊"几点能飞"要回答
    6 小时，3–6 h 这一段的唯一诚实做法是**交给模式**，并在页面上标成"模式延伸"。
    拿模型对一个它没见过的时效给概率，比不给更糟。

口径：
    - 单位与训练侧一致（风速 m/s、能见度 m）；
    - 缓存 10 分钟；失败返回 available:false，上层据此把 3–6 h 画成"不可用"，
      **不要**用平滑/插值把缺口补上。
```


## backend-python/workspace.py

### L27

```
# 6..64 位安全字符。**不纠正、只拒绝**：把非法值"清洗"成合法值，会让两个不同客户端
# 被清洗进同一个空间（例如都变成同一个前缀），那是最难发现的一类串号。
```

### L1

```
匿名工作区：把同一进程里的可变状态按「哪个浏览器」分开。

**为什么需要它**

界面不再有登录门（2026-10-14 用户决定），于是服务端**没有**任何能区分调用方的东西。
而 `traffic.service.identity()` 原本只由 `(seed, scenario, weather_mode, 快照)` 算出，
所以两个浏览器用默认参数（seed=42 / scenario=live）打开，就会拿到**同一份计划**、
共用同一份操作日志、共用同一个气象口径开关 —— 屏幕上看起来是"两个用户"，服务端
看起来只有一个人。

这里放的是"谁"这件事的**唯一来源**：请求头 ``X-ALC-Workspace``（前端生成并存在本浏览器），
以及 ``?ws=`` 查询参数作为退路（WebSocket 握手在浏览器里发不了自定义头）。

**缺席时落回空串，而空串是「未标识」，不是一个默认工作区**

没有标识的调用方（巡检探针、curl、单元测试）看到的就是本次改造**之前**的那一份全局
数据。把「没带标识」和「带了标识」并进同一个桶，会让"改造前行为保持"这条判据失去意义 ——
判据必须能区分这两种情况，否则它量不出回归。
```

### L52

```
``with bind(ws):`` —— 成对绑定/还原。中间件与构建线程都用它。

    提供这个而不是让调用方自己管 token：漏一次 `reset` 的后果是**上下文泄漏到
    下一个任务**（线程会被复用），症状是随机的、只在并发下出现。
```


---

> 以下为 `traffic/service.py`、`traffic/operations.py` 的补档（首轮删除后因空块问题重跑）。

## backend-python/traffic/operations.py

### L14

```
# 一条日志行的类型。**`MANUAL` 之外的三种不参与回放**：`records()` 只把
# `SUSPEND`/`RESUME` 交给前端 `effectiveFlights`，而"计划构建完成"没有 `flightId`、
# 也没有班次状态可切换 —— 把它一起喂进去，重放会把它当成一条班次操作。
# 这也是为什么读整份日志的入口是 `journal()` 而不是 `records()`。
```

### L26

```
# 事件处置台的状态机（P0-3）。**四个可写状态**，`PENDING` 不在其中 —— 「待确认」是
# **没有任何记录**时的默认读法，不是一个可以被人为设置的值：把"未处置"也做成一条
# 可写的记录，就无法区分"没人看过"和"有人看过并决定不动它"。
#
# 两态（已确认 / 已处置）表达不了"这件事我正在处理"，而"正在处理"是值班室里最常
# 发生的一种状态：把它并进"已确认"，交班时就无法区分"看了一眼"和"已经在改时隙了"。
# 第四态「已复核」是交班/复盘那一步 —— 它与「已处置」不是一回事：处置说的是动作做完了，
# 复核说的是**另一个人看过并认账**。少了它，闭环在最后一段没有落点。
```

### L36

```
# 处置动作与它们**能不能真的执行**。这个区分是整个 P0-3 最要紧的一处诚实：
#
#   延迟起飞 / 改航绕飞 —— 改时隙与绕行几何在**计划构建时**写定（班次字段
#   `delayMinutes` / `groundWaitMinutes` / `routeBasis` / `points`），运行时没有
#   重算某一个事件的能力。把这两个按钮做成"发一个请求、收到失败、再渲染成已处置"，
#   就是一次典型的假成功 —— 屏幕上说处置过了，而世界没有变化。
#
#   停止放行 —— 才是运行时可执行的动作：对具体班次下 `SUSPEND`，写回同一条日志，
#   可在调度台账恢复原时隙。
#
# 所以不支持的动作**必须显式回答"本系统不支持"**，并带上原因。
```

### L129

```
# 取值一律用 `.get()`：本文件里**本来就有一批没有班次的行**（计划构建、气象通道
# 切换、导出），`records()` 的文档甚至专门写了"重放会把一条没有 `flightId` 的
# 记录当成班次操作"。可是这里曾用 `r['flightId']` 直接下标 —— 于是只要这份日志
# 里先写过一次系统事件，**第一次点「暂停班次」就会 `KeyError` → HTTP 500**。
#
# 这不是理论风险，界面上的顺序**必然**是先构建（写 SYSTEM）再操作：一整套
# 测试之所以全绿，只是因为它们都恰好先调 `command` 再写其它行 —— 一条从未被
# 走到的组合。判据补在 `test_the_demo_run_reaches_the_promised_twenty_rows_…`。
#
# 比较范围同时收窄到 `MANUAL`：这条守卫防的是**同一个班次自己**的时间轴倒退，
# 而处置行绑的是事件、不是班次状态，不应参与。
```

### L169

```
# `disposalAction` 与 `action` **分开透出**：`action` 是状态机落点（`IN_PROGRESS`），
# `disposalAction` 是做的那件事（`SUSPEND`）。只给一个，交班时就读不出
# "这一条是停止放行推过来的"还是"人工点了一下处置中"。
```

### L291

```
# 只写一行**系统留痕**说明"被请求过但不支持"，不推进状态机 ——
# 推进状态机会让预警中心把这一条读成"已经在处理了"，而实际上什么都没做。
```

### L353

```
# 三道越界检查分开写，是因为"哪一条越界"本身就是可展示的信息：
# 合成一个 not 1<=len<=5 or any(...) 之后，界面只能说"模型返回不合法"。
```

### L362

```
# 「AI 被规则否决」就是这一条分支：模型越出证据集合，整条回答降级为规则证据。
# 不能把越界的编号悄悄丢掉后照常作答 —— 那等于用一条没人核对过的引用回答用户，
# 而界面还显示"外部模型检索成功"。
```

### L98

```
**班次状态操作**（暂停/恢复）——这份列表是回放的输入，只含 `MANUAL`。

    其它三类事件在同一个文件里，但要读它们请用 `journal()`：混进这条通道，
    重放会把一条没有 `flightId` 的记录当成班次操作。
```

### L155

```
外部模型越出了证据集合 —— 规则否决，不是网络故障。

    单独一个类型，是因为这两种情况必须分开报：把它们都塞进 `error_code()`
    会让"模型引用了一个不存在的证据"显示成 `INVALID_RESPONSE`，读起来像服务挂了。
```

### L163

```
One journal row, with the two labels the界面 needs resolved on the server.

    行里的 `action` 是给机器看的（`PLAN_BUILT`），`actionLabel` 是给人看的。
    前端各写一份映射，就是下一次"同一件事两个说法"的入口（M59）。
```

### L184

```
本次运行的**完整**日志：人工干预 + 系统决策 + 数据源变更 + 导出。

    ``sessionId`` 为 ``None`` 的行属于"不属于任何一份计划"的事件 —— 气象通道切换
    改变的是**下一次构建**读什么，所以它必须在每一份计划的日志里都看得见。
    把这些行按会话过滤掉，等于把"同一个种子为什么两次结果不同"的唯一解释藏起来。

    **但"每一份计划"指的是同一个人的每一份计划。** 这些行按 `workspace` 收口：
    没有工作区的行（改造前写入的历史行，`get` 出来是 `None`，当作空串）只对同样
    没有标识的调用方可见 —— 于是"带标识的两个人互不串号"与"不带标识的行为和改造前
    一模一样"这两条同时成立。
```

### L206

```
计划构建完成写一行 `SYSTEM`；按 (sessionId, planRevision) 幂等。

    `/traffic/session` 会被前端轮询到 `READY` 为止，所以这里会对同一份计划被调用
    很多次。没有这道去重，日志会按轮询次数增长，页面直接不可用。
```

### L230

```
气象通道切换写一行 `SOURCE`（`sessionId` 为空，见 `journal`）。

    行上必须带 `workspace`：这一行**不属于任何一份计划**（切换影响的是下一次构建读
    什么），所以它没有 `sessionId` 可依附。少了这一维，A 切通道会出现在 B 的日志里 ——
    界面上读起来像"系统自己改了口径"，而 B 什么都没做。缺省取当前请求绑定的工作区。
```

### L249

```
事件处置台的一次状态转移（P0-3）。

    挂在 **`eventKey`** 上而不是 `flightId` 上：处置的对象是预警中心合并出来的
    **事件**（`routeId:reason:是否关闭`），而一个事件可能压着一组班次。挂在班次上，
    同一条走廊上的第二班出事时就得再处置一次 —— 那正是"合并事件"要消掉的东西。

    `action` 是**让人看得懂的处置动作**（`DELAY` / `DETOUR` / `SUSPEND` / `REVIEWED`…），
    `state` 是它把状态机推到哪一态。两者分开记：`SUSPEND` 与 `HANDLED` 都可能把状态
    从"已确认"推走，但它们的动作不是一回事，交班时要能读出来。
```

### L276

```
一键处置（P0-3 ①）。**做不到的动作必须显式回答做不到。**

    三种结局，**都必须与另外两种可区分**（M67 弃权必须可区分）：

      · `applied=True`  —— 动作真的执行了（停止放行 / 标记已处置 / 复核）；
      · `applied=False, supported=False` —— **本系统不支持**该动作，带原因。
        这不是错误，是一个必须被如实说出的能力边界（`motion` 在构建时写定）。
      · 抛 `ValueError` —— 请求本身非法（未知动作）。它走 422，与上一种完全不同形。

    绝不用一条「已处置」记录去掩盖一次没有发生的动作 —— 屏幕上说处置过了而世界没变，
    比报错更糟：报错会让人去查，假成功不会。
```


## backend-python/traffic/service.py

### L26

```
# Aliased: the flight loop already binds a local named ``zones`` for crossing-zone
# reservations, and shadowing the module there would silently mis-resolve.
```

### L44

```
# 0.11.14（H-2③）：**计划修订号**必须是会变的号。
#
# 原来 `planRevision` 直接把上面这个常量发出去 —— 它是算法版本串，永远不变。
# 后果有两个：
#   · 设置面板承诺"同一快照下重新构建会递增"，实际永远显示 `traffic-plan-v1.3`；
#   · 前端 `algorithmIdentityTitle()` 用 `Number(raw)` 读它，得到 NaN，于是永远
#     显示「计划修订未声明」；`watch(plan-revision)` 也就永远判断不出"计划换了"。
#
# 这里用**进程内递增序号**，并序列化成字符串：
#   · 数字语义保住（前端 `Number('13') === 13`，能显示 `r13`）；
#   · 每次真正建场必增 ⇒ 不同通道必然不同值；
#   · 仍是字符串 ⇒ 与 `POST /traffic/command` 的 `planRevision: str` 字段兼容。
#
# 为什么不用"hash(通道+指纹)"：那样两个通道下确实不同，但**同一通道重建两次也一样**，
# 而设置面板承诺的是"重新构建会递增"。序号比哈希更贴合那句承诺。
```

### L63

```
# 同代次缓存容量（2026-10-24）：跨代次由 `_fingerprint` 自动作废，同代次原先无上限，
# 实测积到 20 份 / 694 MB。8 份 ≈ 200 MB 上限，够覆盖 JOBS(4) + 预建的兄弟通道。
```

### L70

```
# `JOBS` 上限。**单份计划 54 MB**（实测），所以条数就是内存的主要开关：
#   4 条 ⇒ 216 MB，2 条 ⇒ 108 MB。
# 0.11.19 从 4 降到 2（实测服务器峰值 489 MB，其中 216 MB 是这 4 份计划）。
# 代价说清楚：第 3 个工作区起走磁盘恢复（一次 `json.loads` 约 0.5 秒，
# 替掉 22 秒重建）。依据是"4 个槽在真实访问节奏下填不满" —— 压测里 4 个工作区
# 20 分钟仍停在 SCHEDULED，槽位是空的，占的却一直是满额内存。
```

### L79

```
# `simulate()` **不可重入**，这个锁就是那句话的可执行形式。
#
# 它在开头清空**模块级**的雷达采样缓存（`_RADAR_WINDOW_CACHE` / `_RADAR_ERODED_CACHE`，
# 见 simulate 内的 `.clear()`），随后整段规划都在读写它们。两轮并发时，B 的那次 clear
# 会把 A 已经填好的采样抹掉，A 之后就可能读到属于**另一轮**（乃至另一个气象通道）的
# 采样 —— 结果是一份"几何对不上它自己天气"的计划，而两边的判据都可能是绿的。
#
# 线上本来不会并发：包内唯一的调用点是 `_build`，而 `POOL` 上限就是 1 个 worker。
# 需要这把锁，是因为**姊妹通道预建**（`_prewarm_sibling`）让池里出现了一条"用户没在等"
# 的构建；只要有任何调用方在池外直接调 `simulate`（测试就是这么做的），两者就会重叠。
# 池内单 worker 的路径上这把锁不产生任何竞争，所以它是零成本的兜底。
```

### L91

```
# W-02：时间基准**必须是演示当日**。原来钉在 2026-09-14，于是"民航情报"的 CTOT
# 全是 09/14 20:12，而顶栏是当日 —— 同一屏两个日期，评委会直接问"这是旧的吧"。
# 这里取**进程启动日的 10:00Z**（北京时 18:00，覆盖 8 小时推演窗口），
# 既是确定值（同一天内重启不改变快照，可复现），又不会跨天陈旧。
```

### L97

```
# 0.11.9：漂移基准必须与推演起点**同一时刻**（见 `weather_sim.set_drift_reference`
# 的说明）。W-02 改了基准却没改这里，于是每天相位平移半周期、避让方向反一次。
```

### L101

```
# Virtual-radar sweep stride for the plan's avoidance envelope, in minutes.
#
# **This was 60 until 0.9.24 and that was the bug the operator photographed.** The
# map redraws the virtual echo every WEATHER_BUCKET_MINUTES = 2, but the planner
# assumed the echo stood still for a whole hour: a 30x disagreement between the
# obstacle the plan avoided and the obstacle the screen painted. The field is
# ~100-150 km across and drifts ~50 km in that hour, so the union of two sampled
# positions leaves a gap roughly one storm wide, and a path routed around both
# samples runs straight down the middle of it at the minutes the flight is actually
# there. Measured by tools/probes/_probe_flight_vs_echo_timeline.py, which derives
# the *displayed* echo at each played minute independently of the plan: 134
# aircraft-minutes inside the 禁止进入 core across 108 distinct flights, every one
# of them 5-30 minutes away from the nearest sample.
#
# The dose-response (tools/probes/_probe_sweep_resolution.py, seed 42, virtual):
#
#     stride  sweep  build s  accepted  weather-closed  detours  core hits
#         60      9     33.6      7192               0       30        134
#         30     17     35.2      7168               0       40         17
#         20     25     45.3      7140               0       32          5
#         10     49     81.8      7148               0       20          0
#
# 10 is the shipped value because the core tier is 禁止进入 -- zero tolerance -- and
# only stride 10 reaches zero. The price is 2.4x the build, and the trade is
# deliberate: accepted flights move by 0.6% (7192 -> 7148) and **no flight is closed
# by weather at any stride**, so the finer sweep buys correctness by routing, not by
# refusing work. See ADR-37 and ISS-0.9.24-02.
#
# Two cheaper alternatives were measured and rejected rather than assumed:
#
#   * **Geometry simplification does not help.** Stripping vertices from the sample
#     geometry before the union (250 m then 500 m tolerance) cost *more* time
#     (82.1 s, 84.7 s), so the union is not vertex-bound and the tolerance buys
#     nothing -- and a tolerance that shrinks the 禁止进入 core is not a trade that
#     can be paid for in milliseconds anyway. See
#     tools/probes/_probe_sweep_affordability.py.
#   * **The union is the cost, not the sweep.** 112k union calls at either stride;
#     the distinct-window count barely moves (2420 -> 2511); what changes is ~2.1 ms
#     -> ~14.7 ms per uncached union, because a window that unioned 3 sample
#     polygons now unions 8. Attribution: tools/probes/_probe_sweep_cost.py.
#
# The sweep is 49 derivations at ~275 ms each (measured 2026-10-01) -- ~13.5 s of the
# build. It is also the phase that used to leave the progress bar sitting at 0, which
# is why it now reports per sample; see PROGRESS_SWEEP_PERCENT.
# The cadence this sweep has to keep up with. The workbench redraws the echo every
# DISPLAY_BUCKET_MINUTES, so the planner may lag it by at most
# RADAR_SWEEP_LAG_BUCKETS buckets -- beyond that the two disagree about where the
# storm is *between* samples, and a route that dodges two samples flies straight
# down the gap between them. That gap is what put 134 aircraft-minutes inside a
# painted 禁止进入 core (ISS-0.9.24-02).
#
# Declared on the Python side because this is the only place a test can bind the two
# files: frontend/src/traffic/runtime.js owns WEATHER_BUCKET_MINUTES, and
# tests/test_radar_avoidance.py reads that value back out of the file and asserts the
# declaration below still agrees with it. The original defect had these two numbers
# living in two languages with nothing joining them -- a 60-minute planning
# assumption against a 2-minute redraw -- which is exactly why *both* modules were
# internally correct and the screen was still wrong. See ADR-37.
```

### L162

```
# Build progress, as a percentage band per phase. Reported by ``progress()``
# through ``JOBS[key]['progress']`` and read by the workbench bar.
#
# Why bands rather than a single 0-100 counter over the flight loop. Measured on a
# virtual build (2026-10-01, 7760 flights): the per-flight loop is the long pole,
# but the radar sweep runs **before** the first flight is planned and costs
# ``stride`` derivations at ~275 ms each -- 2.5 s at stride 60 and 13.5 s at the
# stride the incursion fix needs. A counter that only moves inside the loop sits at
# 0 through that sweep and then sweeps 0->100 in one motion, which tells the
# operator the exact opposite of the truth: the long part looks instant and the
# instant part looks long. The bands are named constants for the reason
# ``DETOUR_WINDOW_PADDING_MINUTES`` is: when the numbers move, the report has to be
# able to point at them.
```

### L178

```
# Ordered ``(threshold, stage)``. The single mapping from a percentage to the
# sentence shown beside the bar, so the bar and its caption cannot disagree about
# which phase is running -- see :func:`progress_stage`.
#
# The band at 10 % is named for the *phase*, not for the virtual sweep, because the
# measured channel spends it on one real mosaic fetch instead. A label that said
# "展开时轴" there would describe a sweep that is not happening; measured mode has
# exactly one observation and no timeline to unfold.
```

### L192

```
# Departure offsets, as fractions of the ground-hold budget, at which a flight
# that cannot get a path *now* is asked to try again *later*.
#
# Why this exists. Until 0.9.22 the avoidance set was built over the padded
# window [scheduled, scheduled + base + max_extra] -- the worst the weather could
# do anywhere the flight might possibly be. For a storm crossing the corridor
# that union is a swept band lying right across it, which shuts the flight even
# though the corridor is empty both before and after the band passes. A flight
# does not need the corridor clear for three hours; it needs it clear for the
# forty-odd minutes it is actually up there, and it is allowed to wait for that
# window. The operator asked for exactly this: 「只要有合适的窗口就可以飞」.
#
# The ladder is deliberately short -- three probes, of which the later two cost
# nothing whenever the first succeeds -- because the measurements say space is
# not the constraint: widening the search margin 4x moved the accepted count by
# zero (see tools/probes/_probe_route_window.py). What the ladder buys is time.
```

### L209

```
# How much of the ground-hold budget is folded into the weather window a flight
# is *routed* against, in minutes. ``0.0`` means "the flight's own span".
#
# It is a named constant and not a literal because the two readings give very
# different plans -- the padded one closes flights whose corridor is empty both
# before and after the band passes -- and the measured difference belongs in the
# report rather than in a hidden number (the same reason
# ``airportFootprintPolicy`` is exposed). It is also the lever
# ``tools/probes/_probe_detour_windows.py`` flips to reproduce the pre-0.9.22
# planner in-process, which is the only way to compare two planners against one
# sky rather than against two weathers.
```

### L222

```
# 时隙选优的口径。用户 2026-10-01 第 2 条选定了「延迟与绕行取**总延误最小**」。
#
# 它是一个**具名常量**而不是隐藏在循环里的一行代码，理由与
# ``DETOUR_WINDOW_PADDING_MINUTES`` 相同：两种口径给出的是不同的班次计划，而
# 差异必须能在一份报告里被指出，而不是靠读代码才知道。它同时是
# ``tools/probes/_probe_delay_strategy.py`` 翻转的那个开关 —— 该探针用同一个
# 天空并排跑两种口径，是唯一能把"新口径更好"这件事说成测量而不是主张的方式。
#
#   'minimal' 收集所有可行候选，按 `delay + 绕行增时` 取最小（当前口径）
#   'greedy'  从 delay=0 起按步长递增，第一个可行方案即采纳
#             （0.9.22 及以前的口径；它最小化的是 delay 而不是总延误）
```

### L235

```
# Background wind arrows are drawn on a fixed lattice over the operating box.
# The lattice is what makes the layer a *field*: one arrow per observed city put
# 36 arrows on 36 hubs and left the rest of the map blank, so the layer read as a
# city annotation rather than the ambient wind that explains where a band is
# going.
#
# A fixed lattice over the *bounding box* cannot do this job. The box is a
# rectangle drawn around an irregular three-province outline, so a coarse lattice
# puts most of its nodes on blank map and a minority on the area: at 9 x 7 only
# 27 of the 63 nodes landed near the outline at all. Two consequences, both of
# which the operator sees:
#
#   * the surviving 27 are separated by up to 228 km, so the field reads as a
#     scattering of arrows rather than a field -- 38% of the operating area was
#     farther than 100 km from its nearest arrow;
#   * the nodes that landed near the *rim* were kept even when they fell outside
#     the outline (that was what WIND_EDGE_TOLERANCE_M was for), which put arrows
#     on foreign territory. 41% of them did, the worst 39.7 km outside.
#
# The fix is to sample the outline's own extent instead of the box's: walk each
# province's meridian span and place points across it, so a sample is either on
# the area or it is not generated. ``WIND_BAND_M`` is the north-south spacing
# between those walks, which is what actually sets the density; the per-province
# count follows from it rather than being fixed, so a long province gets more
# points than a short one at the same spacing.
```

### L261

```
# Target east-west spacing inside a band. The number of points in a band is
# ``ceil(span / WIND_SAMPLE_SPACING_M) + 1``, which keeps the lattice roughly
# isotropic without letting a wide province collapse to a handful of columns.
```

### L265

```
# Hard ceiling on emitted vectors. Sampling is bounded by the outline, so this is
# a backstop against a future boundary change -- not the density control. It must
# stay comfortably above the count the constants above produce, because a cap
# that binds is not a backstop: it silently truncates the field. At 45 km bands
# and 55 km spacing the current outline yields 357 samples, so a cap below that
# would drop the northernmost bands and the map would show no wind where the
# user is looking. Building 357 samples takes ~18 ms, which is the other reason
# not to trade coverage for a smaller payload.
```

### L274

```
# Inverse-distance weighting uses the nearest few stations only. With 36 stations
# over the box, taking all of them makes every grid point approach the province
# mean (the weights flatten out); the nearest handful keeps local structure.
```

### L278

```
# Retained for the legacy single-city path and for callers that still reason
# about "how far off the outline a sample may sit". Full-area sampling no longer
# uses it: a sample is either inside the outline or it is dropped.
```

### L283

```
# Which closure reasons are actually *weather*. A flight that was never accepted
# for one of these is closed because of the weather channel; every other reason
# is an airspace, geometry or resource limit that would close the flight on a
# perfectly clear day. The distinction is what keeps "本轮计划关闭" from
# claiming credit for a weather effect that is not there.
```

### L294

```
# The fixed-airspace catalogue is a planning input: changing it must
# invalidate cached plans, exactly like the aircraft profiles do.
```

### L416

```
# City-specific merchant/receiver nodes drive the corridor set. Node and
# corridor counts are configuration, not hardcoded geometry, so the delivery
# layer can be resized (8-16 nodes) without touching the scheduler.
```

### L554

```
# Virtual mode advances the slot with the playback minute, anchored at
# SIMULATION_BASE_TIME -- the same timeline the flights live on. The
# generator is a seeded advection model (25-35 km/h steering flow,
# 6-minute life-cycle slots; centroid drift ~99 km over the 8 h window,
# measured 2026-09-24), so scrubbing the timeline moves the echo the way
# a real storm field would drift. Measured mode CANNOT do this: the
# mosaic is one real observation and projecting it forward would be a
# fabricated forecast, so it stays put and the badge reports its age.
```

```
# ——换一条传输路径、由浏览器代取——就丢在服务端了。
```

```
# The source id travels with the geometry so a consumer can tell a real
# mosaic from a generated one without knowing about this module's internals.
```
